### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T12:10:24Z

I checked your workspace first. `~\enam` is an empty file, so we're starting from nothing. Python 3.12 and Node 22 are installed, which covers everything below. Your home folder is itself a git repo, so I'd put the project in its own repo at `~\finn`.

# FINN: product and technical plan

## 1. What the brief actually rewards

From the grading text, the priorities are:

1. **Judgment about filtering.** The brief says "surfacing the few things that actually matter and burying everything else." Ranking and collapsing routine filings matter more than showing everything.
2. **Analysing management, not summarising it.** The brief calls the promised-versus-delivered comparison "the kind of thing we value most." This should be the flagship.
3. **A clear point of view on interaction.** A chatbot plus a dashboard is the obvious answer they want us to move past.
4. **Getting the numbers right.** They're an investment firm. One made-up number in the demo undoes everything else.
5. **Cost discipline.** Model routing, caching and chunking earn bonus points.
6. **It runs.** "A demo that doesn't run is the worst outcome."

Two timing facts help us:
- **It's peak results season.** The Jul–Sep 2026 quarter results are being filed now (mid-Oct to mid-Nov), so the demo will have real, fresh results filings.
- **We have 6 days** (9–15 Nov). That's tight, so the scope has to be narrow.

## 2. Key product insight: three data shortcuts

Most entrants will run PDFs through an LLM and hope the numbers come out right. Three facts about Indian filings make a more accurate approach possible:

| Problem | Naive approach | Better approach |
|---|---|---|
| Quarterly results numbers | LLM reads the PDF | **Results filings in the standard SEBI format already contain three columns: this quarter, last quarter, and the same quarter last year.** One primary document gives both QoQ and YoY. Use the **XBRL** version (machine-readable, exact) where the exchange provides it, with PDF table parsing plus an LLM as fallback. |
| Bio of an incoming CFO or CEO | Scrape LinkedIn (against its terms, blocked, fragile) | **SEBI disclosure rules require a "brief profile" of any newly appointed director or key manager inside the filing itself.** Extract it from the filing, a primary source. Optionally add a clearly labelled web-search supplement. |
| Announcement classification | LLM on every filing | **BSE attaches its own category to each filing** (Result, Board Meeting, AGM/EGM, Insider Trading/SAST, and so on). Rules on the category and subject line should handle most filings for free. Only ambiguous ones go to a cheap model. |

**A strict rule for accuracy:** the LLM never produces a number. Numbers come from parsed tables and are computed in code. The LLM writes prose that refers to pre-computed facts by ID. A validator then checks that every number in the LLM's output exists in the fact table and rejects or regenerates the output if not. That's how we can honestly claim "no invented financial data."

**EBITDA is not reported directly.** We compute it as Revenue from operations − (Total expenses − Finance costs − Depreciation & amortisation), which excludes other income, and show that formula on the card. For **banks and NBFCs**, EBITDA doesn't apply. We show net interest income, operating profit, provisions and PAT instead, and label it that way. That's the kind of detail a finance reviewer will notice.

## 3. Recommended MVP

### Must have (the demo)

**Push: "The Brief"**
- A ranked feed of filings for any chosen window (24h by default, adjustable to 3d, 7d or custom).
- A **materiality score** that is deterministic and explainable: a base weight per category, adjusted for sub-type (a CFO exit outranks an independent-director exit; a rating downgrade outranks a reaffirmation), position weight in the portfolio, and same-day price reaction. Each card has a "Why is this ranked here?" explanation.
- Routine filings collapse into one line, for example "11 routine filings (ESOP allotments, compliance certificates)", which expands on tap.
- **Results card:** Revenue, EBITDA, PBT and PAT, each with QoQ and YoY, the margin change, a source chip linking to the filing page, and a confidence badge (arithmetic check: PBT − tax ≈ PAT).
- **Management-change card:** who left, who joined, and a profile extracted from the filing.
- Every other category gets a one-line "why it matters" summary, or no summary when a summary would add nothing.
- **End-of-day alerts:** configurable per stock or globally. Volume as a multiple of its 20-day average (default 2×), and price move as ±X% or in standard deviations of the stock's own volatility. A 3% move means different things for a utility and a small-cap.
- **Linking:** if a volume spike and a filing happen on the same day, they become one story ("Volume 3.4× — results filed 15:42").

**Pull: "The Research Memo"**
- Works for any NSE ticker. The pipeline is generic: symbol → screener page → filing documents.
- **Deterministic sections** (from screener's standardised tables, cross-checked against filings): financial snapshot, multi-year trajectory, balance sheet (debt-to-equity, working-capital days), and cash quality (cash from operations vs PAT, free cash flow). These render in seconds with no LLM cost.
- **Flagship: the "Promise Ledger."** Extract every guidance statement from the last 4 concall transcripts, each with a quote, quarter and page. Match each one against what was actually reported and give a verdict:
  - Delivered
  - Missed
  - Goalposts moved
  - **Silently dropped** (guidance that simply stopped being mentioned)

  The last verdict is the real analytical insight; summarisers can't produce it.
- **Bull vs bear:** three points each, every point with a citation chip that opens the source quote and page.
- Progressive rendering: the numbers appear first, and LLM-written sections stream in after.

**Mobile**
- An installable web app (PWA) with bottom navigation (Brief / Alerts / Research / Settings), built for one-handed use.

### Should have, if time allows (in this order)
1. Real phone notifications for triggered alerts via Web Push. This works on Android, and on iOS 16.4+ once the app is installed to the home screen.
2. **"Ask"**: free-form questions grounded only in that company's documents, with citations, and a refusal when the sources don't cover the question.
3. A 60-second audio version of the brief using the browser's built-in text-to-speech (free, and it covers the voice option the brief suggests).
4. Swipe gestures: swipe left to mute this category for this stock, swipe right to research it. This is the "inbox triage" interaction.

### Deliberately left out
- Intraday or real-time prices (the brief says end-of-day).
- User accounts and multiple users (one demo portfolio).
- News, broker notes and macro data (the brief forbids secondary sources on the pull side).
- Full RAG over 300-page annual reports (see section 5).
- A native app.

## 4. Interaction model: inbox triage, not a dashboard

The point of view to put in the writeup: **an investor's day is triage, so FINN works like an inbox you can clear in two minutes.** Opening the app shows "3 things matter today," ranked with reasons. Everything else is folded away. Every card is a doorway into research: tapping "Ask about this" on a results card opens that company's memo already scrolled to the Promise Ledger, so the reader can see "they said 15% growth; they delivered 9%." Push and pull are one continuous flow, not two separate tabs.

## 5. Architecture

```
            ┌───────────── Scheduled jobs (GitHub Actions cron, or local) ────────────┐
            │  every 30 min: BSE/NSE announcements → classify → score → enrich        │
            │  ~18:30 IST:   EOD prices (yfinance, fallback NSE bhavcopy) → alerts    │
            └──────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                           ▼
 Exchange PDFs/XBRL ─► Ingestion & parsing (pdfplumber/PyMuPDF, XBRL parser)
 screener.in       ─►        │  content-hash every document → cache extractions forever
                             ▼
                ┌──── Postgres (Supabase/Neon free tier; SQLite in development) ────┐
                │ companies · prices_eod · announcements · documents · facts        │
                │ guidance_items · alerts · alert_rules · extractions · llm_ledger  │
                └──────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                               ▼
              FastAPI (Python) ── model router ──┬─ rules (free)
                 │  REST + SSE streaming         ├─ cheap model: classification, extraction
                 │                               └─ strong model: Promise Ledger, bull/bear
                 ▼
         Next.js PWA (Vercel) ── Web Push (VAPID) ──► phone
```

**Data flow for a push item:** cron fetches new filings → deduplicate by exchange ID → rule classifier (cheap-model fallback) → materiality score → for high-materiality types, download the PDF or XBRL, hash it, extract structured JSON once, and validate it → store → if it crosses a user's alert rule, send a Web Push → the feed reads only from the database. The demo never depends on a live scrape.

**Data flow for a pull request:**
1. Resolve the ticker.
2. Fetch the screener financial tables and the list of documents. Cache both.
3. Render the deterministic sections immediately.
4. Run a per-transcript structured extraction (guidance statements, quote, page). This is a map step on a cheap model, cached by document hash.
5. Run one strong-model reasoning call over the extracted JSON plus the fact table to build the ledger and bull/bear.
6. Validate citations and numbers.
7. Stream the result to the client.

**Why per-document extraction instead of RAG:** a transcript is about 15–25k tokens and fits in context whole. Extracting it once into structured guidance items is cheaper, more complete (RAG misses guidance phrased unexpectedly), and reusable across every later question. Embeddings, using a free local model, are only needed for the "Ask" feature.

**Stack**

| Layer | Choice | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| Backend | Python + FastAPI | yfinance, PDF and XBRL tooling are all Python |
| Frontend | Next.js + Tailwind + PWA | Easiest for reviewers to open on a phone; free Vercel hosting |
| Database | Postgres (Neon/Supabase free) | Free tier; SQLite locally |
| Backend hosting | Fly.io or Render | Render's free tier sleeps (~50s cold start), so we keep it warm before the demo or use Fly |
| Bulk LLM work | Cheap/free model (Gemini Flash free tier, a Groq-hosted Llama, or Claude Haiku 5.5) | Classification and extraction |
| Reasoning LLM | Claude Sonnet 5.5 (or the best free option you have) | Only for the ledger and bull/bear |
| Cost visibility | `llm_ledger` table with tokens and cost per call, plus cache-hit rate, shown in the app and README | Makes the efficiency bonus visible |

## 6. Risks and data availability

| Risk | Severity | Mitigation |
|---|---|---|
| NSE's API needs a cookie handshake and often blocks cloud IPs | High | BSE is the primary source (more tolerant with proper headers); NSE is secondary. Run ingestion from a local machine or cron and write to the hosted DB. Verify on day 1. |
| GitHub Actions IPs may also be blocked | Medium | Fallback: run ingestion locally on a schedule during the demo week |
| yfinance breaks or gets rate-limited | Medium | NSE bhavcopy (the official daily price file) as fallback; cache all history |
| Scanned or image-only PDFs | Medium | Prefer XBRL. Use OCR only as a last resort; otherwise mark the item "manual review" rather than guess |
| Standalone vs consolidated numbers mixed up | High (accuracy) | Always choose consolidated, label it on the card, cross-check against screener's quarterly table |
| Screener rate limits or terms | Medium | Polite fetching, aggressive caching, fetch only on demand; use company investor-relations pages as fallback |
| Not enough concall transcripts for small companies | Medium | Show "insufficient sources" honestly instead of a hollow ledger |
| Ticker changes after demergers or renames (e.g. Tata Motors, Zomato→Eternal, ITC Hotels) | Low | Avoid these in the demo portfolio; validate every ticker on day 1 |
| LLM hallucinating numbers or citations | High | Numeric-grounding validator plus a check that every quoted text actually appears on the cited page |
| Cold start or downtime during review | High | Keep the backend warm; fall back to a seeded database snapshot; record a demo video as backup |
| LinkedIn bios | — | Not promised. We use the profile required in the filing. I won't claim a LinkedIn integration we don't have. |

## 7. Portfolio (candidates, to be validated on day 1)

The aim is a spread of sectors and sizes, including types that stress-test the logic: banks (no EBITDA), lenders, companies with lots of filings, and small, volatile names.

- Large caps: RELIANCE, HDFCBANK, INFY, BAJFINANCE, SUNPHARMA, MARUTI, LT, ASIANPAINT, TITAN, BHARTIARTL
- Mid and smaller caps: DIXON, POLYCAB, PERSISTENT, TRENT, HAL, IRCTC, DEEPAKNTR, PAYTM, SUZLON
- On-demand research demos (outside the portfolio): PIDILITIND, KPITTECH, plus one that you pick live to show it generalises.

## 8. Day-by-day plan

| Day | Deliverable |
|---|---|
| **9–10 Nov** | Data checks with a go/no-go per source: BSE/NSE announcements, XBRL availability, yfinance or bhavcopy, the screener documents list, and parsing 3 results filings and 3 transcripts. Database schema and ingestion. |
| **11 Nov** | Push backend: classifier, materiality score, results extraction with validation, management profile extraction, end-of-day alert engine. |
| **12 Nov** | Pull backend: deterministic sections, transcript guidance extraction, Promise Ledger, bull/bear with citations, number and citation validators. |
| **13 Nov** | Frontend: the Brief feed, cards, alerts, settings and thresholds. |
| **14 Nov** | Frontend: research memo, citation viewer, streaming. Deploy. Web Push if time allows. |
| **15 Nov** | **Accuracy check:** hand-verify about 10 results extractions and 30 classifications and publish the accuracy figures in the README. Then the writeup, a demo video, and exporting the AI build history. |

The accuracy check is cheap and is the strongest signal we can give an AI-engineering reviewer.

## 9. Decisions I need from you before I start

1. **LLM access:** which API keys do you have or can you get (Gemini free tier, Groq, Anthropic, OpenAI)? This sets the routing.
2. **Deployment accounts:** do you have GitHub, Vercel, and one of Neon/Supabase and Fly/Render? Can a scheduled job run from your laptop during the week if exchange sites block cloud IPs?
3. **Scope:** are you happy with the Must list plus the Promise Ledger as the flagship, with Web Push as the first stretch goal?
4. **Portfolio:** keep my candidate list, or do you have your own picks?
5. **Location:** is a new repo at `~\finn` fine?

Once you approve, I'll start with the day-1 data checks and report exactly which sources work before building anything on top of them. I can also publish this plan as a shareable page if you want to keep it as a reference or adapt parts of it for your writeup.

"""Spike 1: EOD price/volume availability (yfinance primary, NSE bhavcopy fallback)."""
import datetime as dt
import io
import zipfile

import requests
import yfinance as yf

TICKERS = ["RELIANCE", "HDFCBANK", "DIXON", "SUZLON", "PAYTM"]

df = yf.download([t + ".NS" for t in TICKERS], period="1mo", interval="1d",
                 group_by="ticker", auto_adjust=False, progress=False)
for t in TICKERS:
    sub = df[t + ".NS"].dropna()
    print(f"yf {t:10s} rows={len(sub)} last={sub.index[-1].date()} close={sub['Close'].iloc[-1]:.2f} vol={int(sub['Volume'].iloc[-1])}")

UA = {"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 Chrome/124 Safari/537.36"}
d = dt.date.today()
for _ in range(7):
    d -= dt.timedelta(days=1)
    url = f"https://nsearchives.nseindia.com/content/cm/BhavCopy_NSE_CM_0_0_0_{d:%Y%m%d}_F_0000.csv.zip"
    r = requests.get(url, headers=UA, timeout=20)
    print("bhav", d, r.status_code, len(r.content))
    if r.status_code == 200:
        z = zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(r.content))
        txt = z.read(z.namelist()[0]).decode()
        print(txt.splitlines()[0][:300])
        print(next(l for l in txt.splitlines() if ",RELIANCE," in l)[:300])
        break

```

"""Spike 2: corporate announcements from BSE and NSE."""
import datetime as dt
import json

import requests

UA = "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/124.0 Safari/537.36"

# --- BSE ---
s = requests.Session()
s.headers.update({"User-Agent": UA, "Referer": "https://www.bseindia.com/", "Origin": "https://www.bseindia.com",
                  "Accept": "application/json, text/plain, */*"})
to = dt.date.today()
fr = to - dt.timedelta(days=3)
url = ("https://api.bseindia.com/BseIndiaAPI/api/AnnSubCategoryGetData/w"
       f"?pageno=1&strCat=-1&strPrevDate={fr:%Y%m%d}&strScrip=500325&strSearch=P&strToDate={to:%Y%m%d}&strType=C&subcategory=-1")
r = s.get(url, timeout=20)
print("BSE scrip", r.status_code, r.headers.get("content-type"), len(r.content))
try:
    j = r.json()
    rows = j.get("Table", [])
    print("rows", len(rows))
    if rows:
        print(json.dumps(rows[0], indent=1)[:1500])
except Exception as e:
    print("json err", e, r.text[:300])

# all companies, single day
url2 = ("https://api.bseindia.com/BseIndiaAPI/api/AnnSubCategoryGetData/w"
        f"?pageno=1&strCat=-1&strPrevDate={fr:%Y%m%d}&strScrip=&strSearch=P&strToDate={to:%Y%m%d}&strType=C&subcategory=-1")
r = s.get(url2, timeout=20)
try:
    j = r.json()
    print("BSE all rows page1", len(j.get("Table", [])), "total", j.get("Table1"))
    cats = {}
    for row in j.get("Table", []):
        cats[row.get("CATEGORYNAME")] = cats.get(row.get("CATEGORYNAME"), 0) + 1
    print(cats)
except Exception as e:
    print("json err", e, r.status_code, r.text[:200])

# --- NSE ---
n = requests.Session()
n.headers.update({"User-Agent": UA, "Accept": "*/*", "Accept-Language": "en-US,en;q=0.9", "Referer": "https://www.nseindia.com/"})
r0 = n.get("https://www.nseindia.com/", timeout=20)
print("NSE home", r0.status_code, list(n.cookies.keys()))
r = n.get("https://www.nseindia.com/api/corporate-announcements?index=equities&symbol=RELIANCE", timeout=20)
print("NSE ann", r.status_code, len(r.content))
try:
    j = r.json()
    print(len(j), json.dumps(j[0], indent=1)[:1200])
except Exception as e:
    print("json err", e, r.text[:200])
r = n.get("https://www.nseindia.com/api/corporates-financial-results?index=equities&symbol=RELIANCE&period=Quarterly", timeout=20)
print("NSE fin results", r.status_code, len(r.content))
try:
    j = r.json()
    print(len(j), json.dumps(j[0], indent=1)[:1500])
except Exception as e:
    print("json err", e, r.text[:200])

```

```
"""Spike 3: NSE results XBRL parse + NSE windowed announcements + screener.in page."""
import collections
import re

import requests
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup
from lxml import etree

UA = "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/124.0 Safari/537.36"
n = requests.Session()
n.headers.update({"User-Agent": UA, "Accept": "*/*", "Accept-Language": "en-US,en;q=0.9", "Referer": "https://www.nseindia.com/"})
n.get("https://www.nseindia.com/", timeout=20)

for sym in ["RELIANCE", "HDFCBANK"]:
    res = n.get(f"https://www.nseindia.com/api/corporates-financial-results?index=equities&symbol={sym}&period=Quarterly", timeout=20).json()
    res.sort(key=lambda x: x["toDate"][-4:] + {"Mar": "03", "Jun": "06", "Sep": "09", "Dec": "12"}[x["toDate"][3:6]] + x["consolidated"], reverse=True)
    for x in res[:4]:
        print(sym, x["toDate"], x["consolidated"], x["bank"], x["filingDate"], x["xbrl"][-40:])
    latest = next(x for x in res if x["consolidated"] == "Consolidated")
    xml = n.get(latest["xbrl"], timeout=30).content
    root = etree.fromstring(xml)
    ctx = {}
    for c in root.iter("{*}context"):
        start = c.find(".//{*}startDate")
        end = c.find(".//{*}endDate")
        inst = c.find(".//{*}instant")
        dims = [m.text for m in c.iter("{*}explicitMember")]
        ctx[c.get("id")] = (start.text if start is not None else None, end.text if end is not None else (inst.text if inst is not None else None), dims)
    facts = collections.defaultdict(dict)
    for el in root:
        if not isinstance(el.tag, str) or el.get("contextRef") is None:
            continue
        name = etree.QName(el).localname
        s, e, dims = ctx.get(el.get("contextRef"), (None, None, None))
        if dims:
            continue
        facts[name][f"{s}..{e}"] = el.text
    print(sym, "fact names:", len(facts))
    for k in ["RevenueFromOperations", "OtherIncome", "Income", "Expenses", "FinanceCosts", "DepreciationDepletionAndAmortisationExpense",
              "ProfitBeforeTax", "TaxExpense", "ProfitLossForPeriod", "ProfitLossForPeriodFromContinuingOperations",
              "InterestEarned", "OperatingProfitBeforeProvisionAndContingencies", "ProvisionsOtherThanTaxAndContingencies",
              "BasicEarningsLossPerShareFromContinuingAndDiscontinuedOperations", "DateOfStartOfReportingPeriod", "WhetherResultsAreAuditedOrUnaudited"]:
        if k in facts:
            print("   ", k, dict(facts[k]))
    print("   sample names:", sorted(facts)[:60])

# windowed announcements across all companies
r = n.get("https://www.nseindia.com/api/corporate-announcements?index=equities&from_date=07-11-2026&to_date=09-11-2026", timeout=30)
j = r.json()
print("NSE window all:", r.status_code, len(j))
print(collections.Counter(x["desc"] for x in j).most_common(40))

# screener
s = requests.get("https://www.screener.in/company/DIXON/consolidated/", headers={"User-Agent": UA}, timeout=30)
print("screener", s.status_code, len(s.text))
soup = BeautifulSoup(s.text, "lxml")
for sec in ["quarters", "profit-loss", "balance-sheet", "cash-flow", "ratios"]:
    t = soup.select_one(f"section#{sec} table")
    if t:
        hdr = [th.get_text(strip=True) for th in t.select("thead th")]
        rows = [[td.get_text(strip=True) for td in tr.select("td")] for tr in t.select("tbody tr")]
        print(sec, hdr[-4:], [r[0] for r in rows])
docs = soup.select_one("section#documents")
if docs:
    for blk in docs.select("div.documents"):
        h = blk.select_one("h3")
        links = blk.select("a")
        print("DOCS", h.get_text(strip=True) if h else None, len(links))
        for a in links[:6]:
            print("    ", re.sub(r"\s+", " ", a.get_text(" ", strip=True))[:80], a.get("href")[:120])
    for li in docs.select(".concalls li")[:5]:
        print("  CONCALL", re.sub(r"\s+", " ", li.get_text(" ", strip=True))[:100], [a.get("href") for a in li.select("a")])

```

"""Spike 4: NSE integrated-filing endpoint discovery + BSE transcript PDF text extraction."""
import io
import json

import fitz
import requests

UA = "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/124.0 Safari/537.36"
n = requests.Session()
n.headers.update({"User-Agent": UA, "Accept": "*/*", "Accept-Language": "en-US,en;q=0.9", "Referer": "https://www.nseindia.com/"})
n.get("https://www.nseindia.com/", timeout=20)

for path in [
    "/api/integrated-filing-results?index=equities&symbol=DIXON",
    "/api/integrated-filing-results?index=equities&symbol=DIXON&period_ended=all&type=Integrated%20Filing-%20Financials",
    "/api/integrated-filing?index=equities&symbol=DIXON",
    "/api/corporate-announcements?index=equities&symbol=DIXON&from_date=01-10-2026&to_date=09-11-2026",
]:
    r = n.get("https://www.nseindia.com" + path, timeout=30)
    body = r.text
    print(path, r.status_code, len(body))
    try:
        j = r.json()
        data = j.get("data", j) if isinstance(j, dict) else j
        print("   n=", len(data) if hasattr(data, "__len__") else "?")
        if data:
            print("   ", json.dumps(data[0] if isinstance(data, list) else data)[:900])
    except Exception as e:
        print("   not json", body[:150])

B = {"User-Agent": UA, "Referer": "https://www.bseindia.com/"}
url = "https://www.bseindia.com/stockinfo/AnnPdfOpen.aspx?Pname=995a3a71-4a1e-4c1f-a29a-6c6747e4ca10.pdf"
r = requests.get(url, headers=B, timeout=60)
print("BSE pdf", r.status_code, r.headers.get("content-type"), len(r.content))
doc = fitz.open(stream=r.content, filetype="pdf")
text = [p.get_text() for p in doc]
print("pages", len(text), "chars", sum(map(len, text)))
print(text[3][:1500])

```

cat > spikes/s5_ixbrl.py <<'EOF'
"""Spike 5: parse NSE Integrated Filing XBRL (contexts, tags) for an industrial and a bank."""
import collections, requests
from lxml import etree
UA = "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/124.0 Safari/537.36"
n = requests.Session(); n.headers.update({"User-Agent": UA, "Referer": "https://www.nseindia.com/"})
n.get("https://www.nseindia.com/", timeout=20)
for sym in ["DIXON", "HDFCBANK"]:
    lst = n.get(f"https://www.nseindia.com/api/integrated-filing-results?index=equities&symbol={sym}", timeout=30).json()["data"]
    print(sym, [(x["qe_Date"], x["consolidated"][:4], x["type"][-10:]) for x in lst[:8]])
    x = next(x for x in lst if x["consolidated"] == "Consolidated" and "Financ" in x["type"])
    root = etree.fromstring(n.get(x["xbrl"], timeout=30).content)
    ctx = {}
    for c in root.iter("{*}context"):
        g = lambda t: (c.find(f".//{{*}}{t}").text if c.find(f".//{{*}}{t}") is not None else None)
        ctx[c.get("id")] = (g("startDate"), g("endDate") or g("instant"), [m.text for m in c.iter("{*}explicitMember")])
    f = collections.defaultdict(dict)
    for el in root:
        if isinstance(el.tag, str) and el.get("contextRef"):
            s, e, d = ctx[el.get("contextRef")]
            if not d: f[etree.QName(el).localname][f"{s}..{e}"] = el.text
    print("  periods:", sorted({k for v in f.values() for k in v})[:10])
    for k in ["RevenueFromOperations","Expenses","FinanceCosts","DepreciationDepletionAndAmortisationExpense","ProfitBeforeTax","ProfitLossForPeriod","InterestEarned","InterestExpended","OperatingProfitBeforeProvisionAndContingencies","ProvisionsOtherThanTaxAndContingencies","ProfitLossFromOrdinaryActivitiesBeforeTax","ProfitLossForThePeriod","LevelOfRoundingUsedInFinancialStatements","NatureOfReportStandaloneConsolidated"]:
        if k in f: print("   ", k, f[k])
EOF

"""Spike 6: validate candidate portfolio tickers on yfinance + screener + NSE integrated filings."""
import requests, yfinance as yf, time
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup
T = "RELIANCE HDFCBANK INFY BAJFINANCE SUNPHARMA MARUTI LT ASIANPAINT TITAN BHARTIARTL DIXON POLYCAB PERSISTENT TRENT HAL IRCTC DEEPAKNTR PAYTM SUZLON".split()
UA = "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/124.0 Safari/537.36"
px = yf.download([t + ".NS" for t in T], period="5d", group_by="ticker", progress=False)
n = requests.Session(); n.headers.update({"User-Agent": UA, "Referer": "https://www.nseindia.com/"}); n.get("https://www.nseindia.com/", timeout=20)
for t in T:
    ok_px = not px[t + ".NS"]["Close"].dropna().empty
    s = requests.get(f"https://www.screener.in/company/{t}/consolidated/", headers={"User-Agent": UA}, timeout=30)
    soup = BeautifulSoup(s.text, "lxml")
    name = soup.select_one("h1").get_text(strip=True) if soup.select_one("h1") else None
    nconc = len(soup.select("section#documents .concalls li"))
    q = soup.select_one("section#quarters thead")
    last_q = q.select("th")[-1].get_text(strip=True) if q else None
    try:
        f = n.get(f"https://www.nseindia.com/api/integrated-filing-results?index=equities&symbol={t}", timeout=30).json()["data"]
        last_if = f[0]["qe_Date"] if f else None
    except Exception as e:
        last_if = f"ERR {e}"
    print(f"{t:11s} px={ok_px} screener={s.status_code} {name!s:40.40s} concalls={nconc:3d} lastQ={last_q} nseIF={last_if}")
    time.sleep(1)
EOF

```
# FINN — data findings & decision log

A running record of what we verified, what we chose, and why. Dates are IST.

## 2026-11-09 — Day-1 data checks (scripts in `spikes/`)

| # | Source | Result | Decision |
|---|---|---|---|
| s1 | yfinance `.NS` EOD | Works: same-day close + volume for all 19 tickers | Primary price source |
| s1 | NSE bhavcopy (`BhavCopy_NSE_CM_…csv.zip`) | Works, full-market daily file | Fallback price source |
| s2 | BSE `AnnSubCategoryGetData` | Works per scrip code; returns `CATEGORYNAME` + `SUBCATNAME` | Fallback announcement source |
| s2 | NSE `corporate-announcements` | Works after a cookie handshake from a residential IP; returns `desc` (≈40 distinct exchange categories), the attachment PDF URL and a summary sentence | **Primary announcement source** |
| s3 | NSE `corporates-financial-results` XBRL | Ends Dec-2024 — SEBI moved results to Integrated Filing | Used only for pre-2025 quarters |
| s4/5 | NSE `integrated-filing-results` XBRL | Current quarter + YTD, exact rupee values, both Ind-AS and banking taxonomies | **Primary source for the results card** |
| s3 | screener.in company page (no login) | 13 quarters, 12 yrs P&L/BS/CF, working-capital ratios, AR + concall transcript links (BSE-hosted PDFs) | Pull-side financials + document discovery; cross-check for results |
| s4 | BSE transcript PDF via PyMuPDF | Text layer present: 24 pages / ~16k tokens for DIXON Q2 FY27 | Whole-document extraction fits in one context window → no RAG needed for the ledger |
| s6 | 19-ticker portfolio | All resolve on all three sources | Portfolio confirmed |

### Notable findings
- **Sources lag each other.** On 9 Nov, screener still showed Jun-2026 for HAL and IRCTC while NSE XBRL had Sep-2026; for INFY it was the reverse. The results card therefore uses XBRL first, falls back to screener, and shows which one it used.
- **Banks use a different taxonomy** (`InterestEarned`, `OperatingProfitBeforeProvisionAndContingencies`, …). EBITDA does not apply; bank cards show NII / pre-provision operating profit / provisions / PAT instead.
- **EBITDA is never reported directly.** We compute it as `Revenue from ops − (Total expenses − Finance costs − D&A)`, which excludes other income. This matches screener's "Operating Profit", so the two sources can be cross-checked.
- **Cloud IP risk:** NSE sits behind Akamai bot protection. Ingestion is designed to run wherever NSE answers (laptop or CI) and write to the shared database, so the hosted API never depends on scraping NSE live.

## Architecture decisions
- **Frontend: Vite + React + TypeScript (static PWA on Vercel)** instead of Next.js. All data comes from the API, so server rendering adds nothing; a static bundle has fewer deploy failure modes.
- **LLM layer is provider-agnostic** (OpenAI-compatible chat API). Routing by task tier:
  - `rules` (free): exchange category → our category for most filings.
  - `fast` (Groq free tier / Gemini Flash-Lite): ambiguous classification, one-line "why it matters".
  - `long` (Gemini Flash, large context): one-time structured extraction per transcript / filing PDF, cached by content hash.
  - `reason` (best available): Promise Ledger verdicts and bull/bear over *extracted JSON*, not raw documents.
  - Ollama (`gemma3:4b`, local) as the offline dev fallback.
- **The LLM never produces numbers.** Figures come from XBRL/screener tables computed in code. LLM prose cites fact IDs; a validator rejects any number not in the fact table, and any quote not found verbatim (after whitespace normalisation) on the cited page.
- **Cache everything by content hash**: PDFs, page text, extractions, LLM responses. Every LLM call is logged to `llm_ledger` (tokens, latency, cache hit) and shown in the app.

```

if DATABASE_URL.startswith("postgres://"):

elif DATABASE_URL.startswith("postgresql://"):

```
import datetime as dt
import json
from contextlib import contextmanager

from sqlalchemy import (JSON, Boolean, Date, DateTime, Float, Integer, String, Text, create_engine, select)
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, Mapped, mapped_column, sessionmaker

from .config import DATABASE_URL, DEFAULT_SETTINGS

engine = create_engine(DATABASE_URL, pool_pre_ping=True,
                       connect_args={"check_same_thread": False} if DATABASE_URL.startswith("sqlite") else {})
Session = sessionmaker(engine, expire_on_commit=False)


class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    pass


def now() -> dt.datetime:
    return dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None)


class Company(Base):
    __tablename__ = "companies"
    symbol: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), primary_key=True)
    name: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(200))
    sector: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(100))
    bse_code: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(16))
    in_portfolio: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)
    weight: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float, default=0.0)  # % of mock portfolio value


class PriceEOD(Base):
    __tablename__ = "prices_eod"
    symbol: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), primary_key=True)
    date: Mapped[dt.date] = mapped_column(Date, primary_key=True)
    open: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float)
    high: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float)
    low: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float)
    close: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Float)
    volume: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(Float)


class Announcement(Base):
    __tablename__ = "announcements"
    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), primary_key=True)  # "NSE:<seq_id>" / "BSE:<newsid>"
    symbol: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), index=True)
    ts: Mapped[dt.datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, index=True)  # IST, naive
    exch_category: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(200))
    text: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text)
    pdf_url: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(500))
    category: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(40), index=True)
    classified_by: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(20))
    materiality: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    reasons: Mapped[list] = mapped_column(JSON, default=list)
    summary: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text)
    enrichment: Mapped[dict | None] = mapped_column(JSON)
    enriched_at: Mapped[dt.datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime)


class Alert(Base):
    __tablename__ = "alerts"
    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), primary_key=True)  # deterministic -> idempotent
    symbol: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), index=True)
    kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(20))  # price | volume | announcement
    date: Mapped[dt.date] = mapped_column(Date, index=True)
    title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(300))
    detail: Mapped[dict] = mapped_column(JSON, default=dict)
    created_at: Mapped[dt.datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, default=now)


class KV(Base):
    """Generic cache / settings store: http bodies, pdf text, llm responses, research reports."""
    __tablename__ = "kv"
    key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(300), primary_key=True)
    value: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text)
    updated_at: Mapped[dt.datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, default=now)


class LLMCall(Base):
    __tablename__ = "llm_calls"
    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True, autoincrement=True)
    ts: Mapped[dt.datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime, default=now)
    task: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(60))
    provider: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(30))
    model: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100))
    tokens_in: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    tokens_out: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    ms: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, default=0)
    cache_hit: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=False)
    ok: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True)


def init_db():
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)


@contextmanager
def session():
    s = Session()
    try:
        yield s
        s.commit()
    except Exception:
        s.rollback()
        raise
    finally:
        s.close()


def kv_get(key: str, max_age: dt.timedelta | None = None):
    with session() as s:
        row = s.get(KV, key)
        if row is None or (max_age and now() - row.updated_at > max_age):
            return None
        return json.loads(row.value)


def kv_set(key: str, value) -> None:
    with session() as s:
        s.merge(KV(key=key, value=json.dumps(value, default=str), updated_at=now()))


def get_settings() -> dict:
    return {**DEFAULT_SETTINGS, **(kv_get("settings") or {})}


def portfolio_symbols() -> list[str]:
    with session() as s:
        return list(s.scalars(select(Company.symbol).where(Company.in_portfolio)))

```

```
"""NSE India: announcements, integrated-filing results and XBRL parsing.

NSE sits behind Akamai; a GET on the home page sets the cookies the JSON API needs.
"""
import collections
import datetime as dt
import threading
import time

import requests
from lxml import etree

from ..config import UA

BASE = "https://www.nseindia.com"
_lock = threading.Lock()
_session: requests.Session | None = None
_warmed_at = 0.0


def _get_session(force: bool = False) -> requests.Session:
    global _session, _warmed_at
    with _lock:
        if _session is None or force or time.time() - _warmed_at > 600:
            s = requests.Session()
            s.headers.update({"User-Agent": UA, "Accept": "*/*", "Accept-Language": "en-US,en;q=0.9",
                              "Referer": BASE + "/"})
            s.get(BASE + "/", timeout=20)
            _session, _warmed_at = s, time.time()
        return _session


def get_json(path: str, params: dict | None = None):
    for attempt in range(3):
        s = _get_session(force=attempt > 0)
        r = s.get(BASE + path, params=params, timeout=40)
        if r.status_code == 200:
            try:
                return r.json()
            except ValueError:
                pass
        time.sleep(1 + attempt)
    raise RuntimeError(f"NSE {path} failed: HTTP {r.status_code}")


def get_bytes(url: str) -> bytes:
    r = _get_session().get(url, timeout=60)
    r.raise_for_status()
    return r.content


def announcements(symbol: str, frm: dt.date, to: dt.date) -> list[dict]:
    rows = get_json("/api/corporate-announcements", {
        "index": "equities", "symbol": symbol,
        "from_date": frm.strftime("%d-%m-%Y"), "to_date": to.strftime("%d-%m-%Y")})
    out = []
    for r in rows or []:
        out.append({
            "id": f"NSE:{r['seq_id']}",
            "symbol": r["symbol"],
            "ts": dt.datetime.strptime(r["sort_date"], "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
            "exch_category": (r.get("desc") or "").strip(),
            "text": (r.get("attchmntText") or "").strip(),
            "pdf_url": r.get("attchmntFile") or None,
        })
    return out


# ---------- results (XBRL) ----------

def _qe(s: str) -> dt.date:
    return dt.datetime.strptime(s.title(), "%d-%b-%Y").date()


def results_filings(symbol: str) -> list[dict]:
    """All quarterly financial-results XBRL filings, newest first: integrated (2025+) and legacy."""
    out = []
    try:
        data = get_json("/api/integrated-filing-results", {"index": "equities", "symbol": symbol}).get("data") or []
        for x in data:
            if "Financ" in (x.get("type") or "") and x.get("xbrl"):
                out.append({"qe": _qe(x["qe_Date"]), "consolidated": x.get("consolidated") == "Consolidated",
                            "xbrl": x["xbrl"], "filed": x.get("broadcast_Date"), "revised": x.get("type_Sub") != "Original"})
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        for x in get_json("/api/corporates-financial-results",
                          {"index": "equities", "symbol": symbol, "period": "Quarterly"}) or []:
            if x.get("xbrl") and x["xbrl"].endswith(".xml"):
                out.append({"qe": _qe(x["toDate"]), "consolidated": x.get("consolidated") == "Consolidated",
                            "xbrl": x["xbrl"], "filed": x.get("filingDate"), "revised": False, "bank": x.get("bank") == "B"})
    except Exception:
        pass
    # Keep one filing per (quarter, basis); prefer the most recent revision.
    seen, uniq = set(), []
    for x in sorted(out, key=lambda x: (x["qe"], x["consolidated"], x["revised"]), reverse=True):
        k = (x["qe"], x["consolidated"])
        if k not in seen:
            seen.add(k)
            uniq.append(x)
    return uniq


def xbrl_facts(xml: bytes) -> dict[str, dict[tuple, str]]:
    """{concept: {(start, end): value}} for dimensionless contexts only."""
    root = etree.fromstring(xml)
    ctx = {}
    for c in root.iter("{*}context"):
        def g(tag):
            el = c.find(f".//{{*}}{tag}")
            return el.text.strip() if el is not None and el.text else None
        dims = list(c.iter("{*}explicitMember")) + list(c.iter("{*}typedMember"))
        ctx[c.get("id")] = (g("startDate"), g("endDate") or g("instant"), bool(dims))
    facts: dict = collections.defaultdict(dict)
    for el in root:
        if not isinstance(el.tag, str) or el.get("contextRef") not in ctx:
            continue
        s, e, has_dims = ctx[el.get("contextRef")]
        if has_dims or el.text is None:
            continue
        facts[etree.QName(el).localname][(s, e)] = el.text.strip()
    return facts

```

"""screener.in company page: standardised financial tables + primary-document links (no login needed)."""
import datetime as dt
import re

import requests
from bs4 import BeautifulSoup

from ..config import UA
from ..db import kv_get, kv_set

TABLES = {"quarters": "quarters", "profit-loss": "annual", "balance-sheet": "balance", "cash-flow": "cashflow",
          "ratios": "ratios", "shareholding": "shareholding"}


class NotFound(Exception):
    pass


def _num(s: str):
    s = s.replace(",", "").replace("%", "").strip()
    if s in ("", "-", "--"):
        return None
    try:
        return float(s)
    except ValueError:
        return None


def _fetch(symbol: str, consolidated: bool) -> str | None:
    url = f"https://www.screener.in/company/{symbol}/" + ("consolidated/" if consolidated else "")
    for attempt in range(3):
        r = requests.get(url, headers={"User-Agent": UA}, timeout=30)
        if r.status_code == 404:
            return None
        if r.status_code == 200:
            return r.text
        if r.status_code == 429:
            import time
            time.sleep(3 * (attempt + 1))
            continue
        r.raise_for_status()
    raise RuntimeError(f"screener {symbol}: HTTP {r.status_code}")


def _table(soup, sec_id):
    t = soup.select_one(f"section#{sec_id} table.data-table")
    if not t:
        return None
    cols = [th.get_text(strip=True) for th in t.select("thead th")][1:]
    rows = {}
    for tr in t.select("tbody tr"):
        tds = tr.select("td")
        if not tds:
            continue
        label = re.sub(r"\s*\+$", "", tds[0].get_text(" ", strip=True)).strip()
        if label.lower().startswith("raw pdf"):
            rows["_pdf"] = [a.get("href") if (a := td.select_one("a")) else None for td in tds[1:]]
            continue
        rows[label] = [_num(td.get_text(strip=True)) for td in tds[1:]]
    return {"cols": cols, "rows": rows}


def _has_data(tbl) -> bool:
    return bool(tbl) and any(v is not None for k, vs in tbl["rows"].items() if not k.startswith("_") for v in vs)


def company(symbol: str, max_age_hours: int = 12) -> dict:
    symbol = symbol.upper().strip()
    key = f"screener:{symbol}"
    if (hit := kv_get(key, dt.timedelta(hours=max_age_hours))) is not None:
        return hit
    basis, html = "consolidated", _fetch(symbol, True)
    soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "lxml") if html else None
    if soup is None or not _has_data(_table(soup, "quarters")):
        basis, html = "standalone", _fetch(symbol, False)
        if html is None:
            raise NotFound(symbol)
        soup = BeautifulSoup(html, "lxml")

    name = soup.select_one("h1")
    top = {}
    for li in soup.select("#top-ratios li"):
        k = li.select_one(".name")
        v = li.select_one(".value")
        if k and v:
            top[k.get_text(strip=True)] = v.get_text(" ", strip=True)
    about = soup.select_one(".company-profile .about p") or soup.select_one(".about p")
    bse = None
    for a in soup.select("a[href*='bseindia.com']"):
        if m := re.search(r"/(\d{6})/?", a.get("href", "")):
            bse = m.group(1)
            break
    sector = None
    for a in soup.select("#peers a[href*='/market/']"):
        sector = a.get_text(strip=True)
    tables = {name_: _table(soup, sec) for sec, name_ in TABLES.items()}

    docs = soup.select_one("section#documents")
    annual_reports, concalls = [], []
    if docs:
        for blk in docs.select("div.documents"):
            h = (blk.select_one("h3") or blk).get_text(" ", strip=True).lower()
            if "annual" in h:
                for a in blk.select("a"):
                    txt = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", a.get_text(" ", strip=True))
                    if m := re.search(r"(\d{4})", txt):
                        annual_reports.append({"year": int(m.group(1)), "url": a.get("href")})
        for li in docs.select(".concalls li"):
            date_el = li.select_one(".ink-600") or li
            date_txt = date_el.get_text(" ", strip=True)[:8]
            entry = {"label": date_txt, "transcript": None, "ppt": None}
            for a in li.select("a"):
                kind = a.get_text(strip=True).lower()
                if kind.startswith("transcript"):
                    entry["transcript"] = a.get("href")
                elif kind == "ppt":
                    entry["ppt"] = a.get("href")
            try:
                entry["date"] = dt.datetime.strptime(date_txt.strip(), "%b %Y").date().isoformat()
            except ValueError:
                entry["date"] = None
            concalls.append(entry)

    out = {
        "symbol": symbol, "basis": basis, "name": name.get_text(strip=True) if name else symbol,
        "about": about.get_text(" ", strip=True) if about else None, "bse_code": bse, "sector": sector,
        "top": top, "tables": tables, "annual_reports": annual_reports, "concalls": concalls,
        "fetched_at": dt.datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
        "url": f"https://www.screener.in/company/{symbol}/" + ("consolidated/" if basis == "consolidated" else ""),
    }
    kv_set(key, out)
    return out


def is_lender(c: dict) -> bool:
    rows = (c["tables"].get("quarters") or {}).get("rows", {})
    return "Revenue" in rows and "Financing Profit" in rows or "Financing Margin %" in rows

```

"""Primary-document fetch + page-level text, cached by URL (text survives in the DB, bytes on local disk)."""
import hashlib
import re

import pymupdf
import requests

from ..config import CACHE_DIR, UA
from ..db import kv_get, kv_set


def _headers(url: str) -> dict:
    h = {"User-Agent": UA}
    if "bseindia.com" in url:
        h["Referer"] = "https://www.bseindia.com/"
    return h


def fetch_pdf(url: str) -> bytes:
    path = CACHE_DIR / ("pdf_" + hashlib.sha1(url.encode()).hexdigest() + ".pdf")
    if path.exists():
        return path.read_bytes()
    if "nseindia.com" in url:
        from .nse import get_bytes
        data = get_bytes(url)
    else:
        r = requests.get(url, headers=_headers(url), timeout=90)
        r.raise_for_status()
        data = r.content
    if not data.startswith(b"%PDF"):
        raise ValueError(f"not a PDF: {url}")
    path.write_bytes(data)
    return data


def pages(url: str, max_pages: int | None = None) -> dict:
    """{"url","sha","pages":[text,...]} — page i is 1-indexed as pages[i-1]."""
    key = f"pdftext:{hashlib.sha1(url.encode()).hexdigest()}"
    if (hit := kv_get(key)) is not None and (max_pages is None or len(hit["pages"]) >= min(max_pages, hit["n"])):
        if max_pages:
            hit = {**hit, "pages": hit["pages"][:max_pages]}
        return hit
    data = fetch_pdf(url)
    doc = pymupdf.open(stream=data, filetype="pdf")
    n = doc.page_count
    limit = n if max_pages is None else min(n, max_pages)
    texts = [_clean(doc[i].get_text()) for i in range(limit)]
    out = {"url": url, "sha": hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()[:16], "n": n, "pages": texts}
    kv_set(key, out)
    return out


def _clean(t: str) -> str:
    t = t.replace(" ", " ")
    t = re.sub(r"[ \t]+", " ", t)
    t = re.sub(r"\n\s*\n+", "\n", t)
    return t.strip()


def norm(s: str) -> str:
    """Normalisation used for verbatim-quote verification."""
    s = s.lower().replace("’", "'").replace("‘", "'").replace("“", '"').replace("”", '"').replace("–", "-").replace("—", "-")
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9%.]+", " ", s).strip()


def find_quote(doc: dict, quote: str, hint_page: int | None = None) -> int | None:
    """Return the 1-indexed page containing the quote (normalised), or None if it is not in the document."""
    q = norm(quote)
    if len(q) < 12:
        return None
    order = list(range(len(doc["pages"])))
    if hint_page and 1 <= hint_page <= len(order):
        order.sort(key=lambda i: abs(i - (hint_page - 1)))
    # allow a quote to span a page break
    for i in order:
        hay = norm(doc["pages"][i] + " " + (doc["pages"][i + 1] if i + 1 < len(doc["pages"]) else ""))
        if q in hay:
            return i + 1
    # tolerate the model trimming the middle with an ellipsis
    parts = [p for p in re.split(r"\s*(?:\.\.\.|…)\s*", quote) if len(norm(p)) >= 12]
    if len(parts) > 1:
        for i in order:
            hay = norm(doc["pages"][i] + " " + (doc["pages"][i + 1] if i + 1 < len(doc["pages"]) else ""))
            if all(norm(p) in hay for p in parts):
                return i + 1
    return None

```

```
"""Provider-agnostic LLM router.

Every provider speaks the OpenAI chat-completions dialect, so routing is just (base_url, key, model).
Tasks ask for a *tier*; the router walks that tier's candidates and skips providers without a key or
that fail (rate limit, outage). Responses are cached by a hash of (model, messages) and every call is
logged to llm_calls so cost and cache-hit rate are visible.
"""
import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import time

import httpx

from .db import LLMCall, kv_get, kv_set, session

PROVIDERS = {
    "gemini": ("https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/openai", "GEMINI_API_KEY"),
    "groq": ("https://api.groq.com/openai/v1", "GROQ_API_KEY"),
    "openrouter": ("https://openrouter.ai/api/v1", "OPENROUTER_API_KEY"),
    "openai": ("https://api.openai.com/v1", "OPENAI_API_KEY"),
    "ollama": (os.getenv("OLLAMA_URL", "http://localhost:11434") + "/v1", None),
}

# Ordered candidates per tier. Override any model with env FINN_MODEL_<PROVIDER>_<TIER>.
TIERS = {
    # short inputs, high volume: classification fallback, one-liners, profile extraction
    "fast": [("groq", "llama-3.1-8b-instant"), ("gemini", "gemini-2.5-flash-lite"),
             ("openrouter", "meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct:free"), ("openai", "gpt-4o-mini"), ("ollama", "gemma3:4b")],
    # whole documents (~20k tokens): transcript guidance extraction
    "long": [("gemini", "gemini-2.5-flash"), ("gemini", "gemini-2.5-flash-lite"),
             ("openrouter", "google/gemini-2.0-flash-exp:free"), ("openai", "gpt-4o-mini"), ("ollama", "gemma3:4b")],
    # reasoning over extracted JSON: promise-ledger verdicts, bull/bear
    "reason": [("gemini", "gemini-2.5-flash"), ("groq", "openai/gpt-oss-120b"), ("groq", "llama-3.3-70b-versatile"),
               ("openrouter", "deepseek/deepseek-chat-v3-0324:free"), ("openai", "gpt-4o-mini"), ("ollama", "gemma3:4b")],
}


class LLMUnavailable(RuntimeError):
    pass


def _key(provider: str) -> str | None:
    env = PROVIDERS[provider][1]
    if env is None:
        return "ollama" if os.getenv("FINN_USE_OLLAMA") == "1" else None
    return os.getenv(env)


def available() -> dict:
    return {t: [f"{p}:{os.getenv(f'FINN_MODEL_{p.upper()}_{t.upper()}', m)}" for p, m in c if _key(p)] for t, c in TIERS.items()}


def _log(**kw):
    try:
        with session() as s:
            s.add(LLMCall(**kw))
    except Exception:
        pass


def _parse_json(text: str):
    text = text.strip()
    m = re.search(r"```(?:json)?\s*(.*?)```", text, re.S)
    if m:
        text = m.group(1)
    start = min([i for i in (text.find("{"), text.find("[")) if i >= 0], default=0)
    return json.loads(text[start:])


def chat_json(task: str, tier: str, system: str, user: str, max_tokens: int = 2000, temperature: float = 0.1):
    """Return parsed JSON from the first working candidate of `tier`."""
    errors = []
    for provider, model in TIERS[tier]:
        key = _key(provider)
        if not key:
            continue
        model = os.getenv(f"FINN_MODEL_{provider.upper()}_{tier.upper()}", model)
        ck = "llm:" + hashlib.sha256(json.dumps([model, system, user]).encode()).hexdigest()
        if (hit := kv_get(ck)) is not None:
            _log(task=task, provider=provider, model=model, cache_hit=True)
            return hit
        base = PROVIDERS[provider][0]
        body = {"model": model, "temperature": temperature, "max_tokens": max_tokens,
                "messages": [{"role": "system", "content": system}, {"role": "user", "content": user}],
                "response_format": {"type": "json_object"}}
        if provider == "groq" and "gpt-oss" in model:
            body["reasoning_effort"] = "medium"
        t0 = time.time()
        try:
            for attempt in range(2):
                r = httpx.post(base + "/chat/completions", json=body, timeout=180,
                               headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {key}"})
                if r.status_code == 429 and attempt == 0:
                    time.sleep(min(float(r.headers.get("retry-after", 8) or 8), 20))
                    continue
                break
            if r.status_code != 200:
                raise RuntimeError(f"HTTP {r.status_code}: {r.text[:200]}")
            data = r.json()
            content = data["choices"][0]["message"]["content"]
            out = _parse_json(content)
        except Exception as e:
            _log(task=task, provider=provider, model=model, ms=int((time.time() - t0) * 1000), ok=False)
            errors.append(f"{provider}/{model}: {e}")
            continue
        usage = data.get("usage") or {}
        _log(task=task, provider=provider, model=model, ms=int((time.time() - t0) * 1000),
             tokens_in=usage.get("prompt_tokens", 0), tokens_out=usage.get("completion_tokens", 0))
        kv_set(ck, out)
        return out
    raise LLMUnavailable(f"no {tier} model succeeded: " + ("; ".join(errors) or "no API key configured"))

```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T12:24:37Z

**→ Write**

`~\finn\backend\finn\push\results.py`

```
"""Quarterly results card: headline numbers this quarter vs last quarter (QoQ) vs same quarter last year (YoY).

Primary path: NSE XBRL (exact rupee values). Fallback: screener.in quarterly table (₹ Cr).
No LLM is involved; every figure is traceable to a filing.
"""
import datetime as dt

from ..db import kv_get, kv_set
from ..sources import nse, screener

CR = 1e7


def fy_label(d: dt.date) -> str:
    fy = d.year + 1 if d.month >= 4 else d.year
    q = ((d.month - 4) % 12) // 3 + 1
    return f"Q{q} FY{fy % 100:02d}"


def _shift(d: dt.date, months: int) -> dt.date:
    m = d.month - 1 + months
    y, m = d.year + m // 12, m % 12 + 1
    nxt = dt.date(y + (m == 12), m % 12 + 1, 1)
    return nxt - dt.timedelta(days=1)


def _xbrl(url: str) -> dict:
    key = f"xbrl:{url}"
    if (hit := kv_get(key)) is not None:
        return hit
    facts = nse.xbrl_facts(nse.get_bytes(url))
    flat = {k: [[s, e, v] for (s, e), v in d.items()] for k, d in facts.items()}
    kv_set(key, flat)
    return flat


def _quarter_values(flat: dict, qe: dt.date) -> dict[str, float]:
    out = {}
    for k, triples in flat.items():
        for s, e, v in triples:
            if s and e == qe.isoformat():
                days = (dt.date.fromisoformat(e) - dt.date.fromisoformat(s)).days
                if 80 <= days <= 100:
                    try:
                        out[k] = float(v)
                    except ValueError:
                        pass
    return out


def _kind(v: dict) -> str:
    if "OperatingProfitBeforeProvisionAndContingencies" in v:
        return "bank"
    rev = v.get("RevenueFromOperations") or 0
    if v.get("InterestEarned", 0) > 0.5 * rev and "ImpairmentOnFinancialInstruments" in v:
        return "lender"
    return "industrial"


def _metrics_xbrl(v: dict, kind: str) -> dict[str, float | None]:
    g = lambda k: v.get(k)
    if kind == "bank":
        pat = g("ProfitLossAfterTaxesMinorityInterestAndShareOfProfitLossOfAssociates") or g("ProfitLossForThePeriod")
        ie, ix = g("InterestEarned"), g("InterestExpended")
        return {"revenue": g("Income") or ((ie or 0) + (g("OtherIncome") or 0)),
                "nii": ie - ix if ie is not None and ix is not None else None,
                "ppop": g("OperatingProfitBeforeProvisionAndContingencies"),
                "provisions": g("ProvisionsOtherThanTaxAndContingencies"),
                "pbt": g("ProfitLossFromOrdinaryActivitiesBeforeTax"), "pat": pat, "tax": g("TaxExpense")}
    pat = g("ProfitLossForPeriod")
    if kind == "lender":
        ie, fc, imp, pbt = g("InterestEarned"), g("FinanceCosts"), g("ImpairmentOnFinancialInstruments"), g("ProfitBeforeTax")
        return {"revenue": g("RevenueFromOperations"), "nii": ie - fc if ie is not None and fc is not None else None,
                "ppop": pbt + imp if pbt is not None and imp is not None else None, "provisions": imp,
                "pbt": pbt, "pat": pat, "tax": g("TaxExpense")}
    rev, exp, fc, da = g("RevenueFromOperations"), g("Expenses"), g("FinanceCosts"), g("DepreciationDepletionAndAmortisationExpense")
    ebitda = rev - (exp - (fc or 0) - (da or 0)) if rev is not None and exp is not None else None
    return {"revenue": rev, "ebitda": ebitda, "pbt": g("ProfitBeforeTax"), "pat": pat, "tax": g("TaxExpense"),
            "other_income": g("OtherIncome"), "exceptional": g("ExceptionalItemsBeforeTax")}


LABELS = {"revenue": "Revenue", "ebitda": "EBITDA", "nii": "Net interest income", "ppop": "Pre-provision profit",
          "provisions": "Provisions", "pbt": "PBT", "pat": "PAT"}
HEADLINE = {"industrial": ["revenue", "ebitda", "pbt", "pat"],
            "bank": ["revenue", "nii", "ppop", "provisions", "pbt", "pat"],
            "lender": ["revenue", "nii", "ppop", "provisions", "pbt", "pat"]}


def _pct(a, b):
    if a is None or b is None or b == 0:
        return None
    return round((a - b) / abs(b) * 100, 1)


def _build(kind, cur, prev, yoy, qe, basis, source, links, notes):
    rows = []
    for k in HEADLINE[kind]:
        c, p, y = (m.get(k) if m else None for m in (cur, prev, yoy))
        rows.append({"key": k, "label": LABELS[k], "cur": _cr(c), "prev": _cr(p), "yoy": _cr(y),
                     "qoq_pct": _pct(c, p), "yoy_pct": _pct(c, y)})
    margins = []
    num = "ebitda" if kind == "industrial" else "ppop"
    for k, label in [(num, "EBITDA margin" if kind == "industrial" else "Pre-provision margin"), ("pat", "PAT margin")]:
        vals = [(m[k] / m["revenue"] * 100) if m and m.get(k) is not None and m.get("revenue") else None for m in (cur, prev, yoy)]
        margins.append({"label": label, "cur": _r(vals[0]), "prev": _r(vals[1]), "yoy": _r(vals[2]),
                        "qoq_bps": _bps(vals[0], vals[1]), "yoy_bps": _bps(vals[0], vals[2])})
    checks = []
    if cur and cur.get("pbt") is not None and cur.get("tax") is not None and cur.get("pat") is not None:
        implied = cur["pbt"] - cur["tax"]
        diff = abs(implied - cur["pat"]) / max(abs(cur["pat"]), 1)
        checks.append({"name": "PBT − tax ≈ PAT", "ok": diff < 0.05,
                       "detail": f"PBT−tax = {_cr(implied)} Cr vs PAT {_cr(cur['pat'])} Cr"
                                 + ("" if diff < 0.05 else " (gap = associates/minority/discontinued items)")})
    if kind == "industrial" and cur and cur.get("exceptional"):
        notes.append(f"Includes an exceptional item of ₹{_cr(cur['exceptional'])} Cr in PBT.")
    if kind == "industrial" and cur and cur.get("other_income") and cur.get("pbt") and cur["other_income"] > 0.3 * cur["pbt"]:
        notes.append(f"Other income is {cur['other_income'] / cur['pbt'] * 100:.0f}% of PBT — profit quality depends on non-operating income.")
    return {"kind": kind, "basis": basis, "source": source, "links": links, "notes": notes, "checks": checks,
            "quarter": {"label": fy_label(qe), "end": qe.isoformat()},
            "prev_label": fy_label(_shift(qe, -3)), "yoy_label": fy_label(_shift(qe, -12)),
            "rows": rows, "margins": margins, "headline": _headline(rows, margins)}


def _cr(v):
    return None if v is None else round(v / CR, 1)


def _r(v):
    return None if v is None else round(v, 1)


def _bps(a, b):
    return None if a is None or b is None else round((a - b) * 100)


def _headline(rows, margins) -> str:
    parts = []
    for r in rows:
        if r["key"] in ("revenue", "pat") and r["yoy_pct"] is not None:
            parts.append(f"{r['label']} {r['yoy_pct']:+.0f}% YoY")
    m = margins[0]
    if m["yoy_bps"] is not None:
        parts.append(f"{m['label']} {m['yoy_bps']:+d} bps YoY")
    return " · ".join(parts)


def from_xbrl(symbol: str, qe: dt.date | None = None) -> dict | None:
    filings = nse.results_filings(symbol)
    if not filings:
        return None
    qe = qe or filings[0]["qe"]
    targets = [qe, _shift(qe, -3), _shift(qe, -12)]
    for consolidated in (True, False):
        by_q = {f["qe"]: f for f in filings if f["consolidated"] == consolidated}
        if qe not in by_q:
            continue
        vals = []
        for t in targets:
            f = by_q.get(t)
            vals.append(_quarter_values(_xbrl(f["xbrl"]), t) if f else None)
        if not vals[0]:
            continue
        kind = _kind(vals[0])
        ms = [_metrics_xbrl(v, kind) if v else None for v in vals]
        links = [by_q[t]["xbrl"] for t in targets if t in by_q]
        notes = [] if all(vals) else ["Comparative quarter not available in XBRL; growth shown only where both periods exist."]
        return _build(kind, *ms, qe, "consolidated" if consolidated else "standalone", "NSE XBRL", links, notes)
    return None


def _q_end(label: str) -> dt.date | None:
    try:
        d = dt.datetime.strptime(label, "%b %Y").date()
    except ValueError:
        return None
    return _shift(d.replace(day=1), 0)


def from_screener(symbol: str) -> dict | None:
    c = screener.company(symbol)
    t = c["tables"].get("quarters")
    if not t:
        return None
    ends = [_q_end(x) for x in t["cols"]]
    rows = t["rows"]
    lender = screener.is_lender(c)

    def at(qe):
        if qe not in ends:
            return None
        i = ends.index(qe)
        g = lambda k: None if rows.get(k) is None or rows[k][i] is None else rows[k][i] * CR
        if lender:
            return {"revenue": g("Revenue"), "nii": (g("Revenue") - g("Interest")) if g("Revenue") and g("Interest") else None,
                    "ppop": g("Financing Profit"), "provisions": None, "pbt": g("Profit before tax"), "pat": g("Net Profit"),
                    "tax": None}
        return {"revenue": g("Sales"), "ebitda": g("Operating Profit"), "pbt": g("Profit before tax"), "pat": g("Net Profit"), "tax": None}

    qe = ends[-1]
    cur = at(qe)
    if not cur:
        return None
    kind = "lender" if lender else "industrial"
    out = _build(kind, cur, at(_shift(qe, -3)), at(_shift(qe, -12)), qe, c["basis"], "screener.in", [c["url"]],
                 ["Figures from screener.in's standardised table; exchange XBRL not yet available."])
    if lender:
        out["notes"].append("Screener's 'Financing Profit' is used as pre-provision profit; provisions not broken out.")
    return out


def results_card(symbol: str) -> dict | None:
    """XBRL first, screener fallback, with a cross-check whenever both cover the same quarter."""
    xb = sc = None
    try:
        xb = from_xbrl(symbol)
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — degrade to the secondary source
        print("xbrl failed", symbol, e)
    try:
        sc = from_screener(symbol)
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        print("screener failed", symbol, e)
    if xb and sc and sc["quarter"]["end"] > xb["quarter"]["end"]:
        primary, other = sc, xb  # exchange XBRL lags for this company
    else:
        primary, other = xb or sc, sc if xb else None
    if primary and other and primary["quarter"]["end"] == other["quarter"]["end"]:
        checks = []
        for key in ("revenue", "pat"):
            a = next((r["cur"] for r in primary["rows"] if r["key"] == key), None)
            b = next((r["cur"] for r in other["rows"] if r["key"] == key), None)
            if a is not None and b is not None:
                ok = abs(a - b) <= max(abs(a), 1) * 0.02
                checks.append({"name": f"{LABELS[key]} matches {other['source']}", "ok": ok, "detail": f"{a:,} vs {b:,} Cr"})
        primary["checks"] += checks
    return primary

```

"""Announcement classification: exchange category → reference category, refined by keyword rules.

The LLM is only consulted for the catch-all buckets ("General Updates", "Updates", "Others") when no rule
matches — measured on two weeks of market-wide NSE filings, rules alone cover the vast majority (see tests).
"""
import re

CATEGORIES = ["results", "M&A", "fund_raise", "management_change", "litigation", "insider_trading", "earnings_call",
              "investor_meetings", "credit_rating", "dividend", "bonus_split", "capex", "press_release",
              "postal_ballot", "agm_egm", "pledging", "unclassified"]

DIRECT = {
    "Integrated Filing- Financial": "results",
    "Financial Result Updates": "results",
    "Investor Presentation": "results",
    "Reply to Clarification- Financial results": "results",
    "Clarification - Financial Results": "results",
    "Reasons for Delayed/Non-submission of Financial Results": "results",
    "Appointment": "management_change", "Change in Management": "management_change",
    "Resignation of Director/KMP/SMP": "management_change", "Resignation": "management_change",
    "Change in Director(s)": "management_change", "Cessation": "management_change", "Retirement": "management_change",
    "Demise": "management_change", "Change in Company Secretary/Compliance Officer": "management_change",
    "Re-appointment": "management_change",
    "Acquisition": "M&A", "Amalgamation/Merger": "M&A", "Scheme of Arrangement": "M&A", "Demerger": "M&A",
    "Sale or disposal": "M&A", "Diversification/Disinvestment": "M&A", "Other Restructuring": "M&A",
    "Open Offer": "M&A", "Public Announcement-Open Offer": "M&A",
    "Buyback": "fund_raise", "Public Announcement - Buyback of Shares": "fund_raise",
    "Post Buyback Public Announcement": "fund_raise", "Closure of Buy Back": "fund_raise", "Rights Issue": "fund_raise",
    "Preferential issue": "fund_raise", "Issue of Securities": "fund_raise", "Qualified Institutional Placement": "fund_raise",
    "Credit Rating": "credit_rating", "Credit Rating- Revision": "credit_rating", "Credit Rating- New": "credit_rating",
    "Credit Rating- Others": "credit_rating",
    "Corporate Insolvency Resolution Process": "litigation", "Action(s) taken or orders passed": "litigation",
    "Action(s) initiated or orders passed": "litigation",
    "Pendency of Litigation(s)/dispute(s) or the outcome impacting the Company": "litigation",
    "Fraud/Default/Arrest": "litigation", "Defaults on Payment of Interest/Principal": "litigation",
    "Initiation of Forensic Audit": "litigation", "Final  forensic  audit  report": "litigation",
    "Delay/default in the payment of fines/penalties/dues etc. to authority": "litigation",
    "Dividend": "dividend", "Bonus": "bonus_split", "Stock split": "bonus_split",
    "Capacity addition": "capex", "Adoption of new line(s) of business": "capex",
    "Commencement of commercial production/operations": "capex",
    "Postponement of commercial production/operations": "capex",
    "Bagging/Receiving of orders/contracts": "press_release", "Awarding of order(s)/contract(s)": "press_release",
    "Product launch": "press_release", "Agreements": "press_release", "Memorandum of Understanding/Agreements": "press_release",
    "Arrangements for strategic, technical, manufacturing, or marketing tie up": "press_release",
    "Monthly Business Updates": "press_release", "News Verification": "press_release",
    "Rumour Verification - Regulation 30(11)": "press_release", "Spurt in Volume": "press_release",
    "Price movement": "press_release", "Disruption of Operations": "press_release",
    "Strikes/Lockouts/Disturbances": "press_release", "Closure of operations": "press_release",
    "Granting/withdrawal/surrender/cancellation/suspension of key licenses/ regulatory approvals": "press_release",
    "Effect(s) on listed entity due to changed regulatory  framework applicable": "press_release",
    "Rescission/termination(s)": "press_release",
    "Disclosure under SEBI Takeover Regulations": "insider_trading", "Offer for sale": "insider_trading",
    "Insider Trading": "insider_trading",
}

ROUTINE = {"Copy of Newspaper Publication", "Trading Window", "ESOP/ESOS/ESPS", "Options to purchase securities",
           "Monitoring Agency Report", "Statement of deviation(s) or variation(s) under Reg. 32", "Utilisation of Funds",
           "Certificate under SEBI (Depositories and Participants) Regulations, 2018", "Loss of Share Certificates",
           "Address Change", "Registrar & Share Transfer Agent Update", "Structural Digital Database",
           "Amendment to AOA/MOA", "Committee Meeting Updates", "Name Change", "Name and Symbol Change", "Corrigendum",
           "Addendum", "Integrated Filing- Governance", "Extension  of Financial Year", "Change in Auditors",
           "Resignation of Statutory Auditor", "Related Party Transactions", "Increase in Authorised Capital",
           "Giving guarantees/indemnity/ becoming a surety for third party", "Alteration/revision(s)", "Amendment(s)",
           "Conversion", "Disclosure of material issue"}

# Ordered keyword rules; first match wins. Applied to (exchange category + text) for ambiguous buckets.
RULES = [
    ("pledging", r"\bpledg|encumbranc|regulation 31\b"),
    ("insider_trading", r"regulation 7\(2\)|regulation 29|insider trading|sast\b|acquisition of shares by promoter|inter[- ]se transfer"),
    ("results", r"financial results?|results? for the (quarter|half|year)|quarter (and half year )?ended|unaudited|audited (standalone|consolidated)|earnings release|shareholders'? letter"),
    ("credit_rating", r"credit rating|\brating\b.*(crisil|icra|care|india ratings|brickwork|acuite)"),
    ("M&A", r"acqui(re|sition)|amalgamat|merger|demerg|joint venture|\bjv\b|stake (sale|in)|wholly[- ]owned subsidiary|share purchase agreement|divest"),
    ("fund_raise", r"buy[- ]?back|rights issue|\bqip\b|qualified institutions? placement|preferential (issue|allotment)|warrants|commercial paper|non[- ]convertible debenture|\bncds?\b|fund rais|raising of funds"),
    ("dividend", r"\bdividend"),
    ("bonus_split", r"\bbonus\b|sub[- ]?division|stock split|\bsplit\b"),
    ("management_change", r"appoint|resign|cessation|retire|chief (executive|financial|operating)|\bceo\b|\bcfo\b|managing director|whole[- ]time director|key managerial|\bkmp\b|\bsmp\b|demise"),
    ("litigation", r"\border\b.*(sebi|court|tribunal|nclt|nclat|tax|gst|authority)|penalt|show cause|litigation|insolvency|\bnclt\b|arbitra|demand notice|income tax|search (and|&) seizure|\bfine\b"),
    ("earnings_call", r"transcript|audio recording|recording of|con(ference)?[ .-]?call|earnings call|investor call"),
    ("investor_meetings", r"analyst|investor meet|institutional investor|investors? conference|one[- ]on[- ]one|non[- ]deal roadshow|\bnon deal\b"),
    ("capex", r"capacity (expansion|addition)|new (plant|facility|unit)|greenfield|brownfield|capital expenditure|\bcapex\b|commission(ed|ing) of"),
    ("postal_ballot", r"postal ballot|e-?voting"),
    ("agm_egm", r"annual general meeting|\bagm\b|extra[- ]?ordinary general meeting|\begm\b|shareholders'? meeting"),
    ("press_release", r"press release|order|contract|launch|partnership|tie[- ]?up|mou|clarification|rumou?r|business update"),
    ("unclassified", r"esop|esos|esps|newspaper|trading window|compliance certificate|regulation 74|loss of share|duplicate share|\besg\b|brsr|auditor"),
]
_RULES = [(c, re.compile(p, re.I)) for c, p in RULES]

BROAD = {"General Updates", "Updates", "Others", "Outcome of Board Meeting", "Press Release", "Press Release (Revised)",
         "Analysts/Institutional Investor Meet/Con. Call Updates", "Shareholders meeting", "Record Date",
         "Allotment of Securities", "Copy of Newspaper Publication"}


def rule_classify(exch_category: str, text: str) -> tuple[str | None, str]:
    """Return (category, method). category None means 'needs LLM'."""
    cat = (exch_category or "").strip()
    blob = f"{cat} . {text or ''}"
    if cat in DIRECT:
        return DIRECT[cat], "exchange"
    if cat in ROUTINE and cat != "Copy of Newspaper Publication":
        return "unclassified", "exchange"
    if cat == "Analysts/Institutional Investor Meet/Con. Call Updates":
        return ("earnings_call" if _RULES[CATEGORIES_INDEX["earnings_call"]][1].search(text or "") else "investor_meetings"), "rule"
    if cat == "Shareholders meeting":
        return ("postal_ballot" if re.search(r"postal ballot", text or "", re.I) else "agm_egm"), "rule"
    if cat == "Copy of Newspaper Publication":
        for c in ("results", "postal_ballot", "agm_egm", "dividend"):
            if _RULES[CATEGORIES_INDEX[c]][1].search(text or ""):
                return c, "rule"
        return "unclassified", "rule"
    if cat == "Allotment of Securities":
        return ("unclassified" if re.search(r"esop|esos|esps|employee stock", text or "", re.I) else "fund_raise"), "rule"
    for c, rx in _RULES:
        if rx.search(blob):
            return c, "rule"
    if cat == "Record Date":
        return "unclassified", "rule"
    if cat in ("Press Release", "Press Release (Revised)"):
        return "press_release", "rule"
    return None, "none"


CATEGORIES_INDEX = {c: i for i, (c, _) in enumerate(RULES)}

LLM_SYSTEM = """You classify Indian listed-company exchange filings into exactly one category.
Categories: results, M&A, fund_raise, management_change, litigation, insider_trading, earnings_call, investor_meetings,
credit_rating, dividend, bonus_split, capex, press_release, postal_ballot, agm_egm, pledging, unclassified.
Use "unclassified" for routine compliance paperwork that would not move the stock.
Reply as JSON: {"category": "<one category>"}"""


def classify(exch_category: str, text: str, use_llm: bool = True) -> tuple[str, str]:
    cat, how = rule_classify(exch_category, text)
    if cat:
        return cat, how
    if use_llm:
        from ..llm import LLMUnavailable, chat_json
        try:
            out = chat_json("classify", "fast", LLM_SYSTEM, f"Exchange category: {exch_category}\nText: {text[:1200]}", 50)
            if out.get("category") in CATEGORIES:
                return out["category"], "llm"
        except LLMUnavailable:
            pass
    return "unclassified", "default"

```
