```
"""Materiality score (0–100) with human-readable reasons. Deterministic so every rank is explainable."""
import re

BASE = {
    "results": 80, "M&A": 72, "fund_raise": 65, "management_change": 45, "litigation": 68, "insider_trading": 45,
    "earnings_call": 25, "investor_meetings": 12, "credit_rating": 40, "dividend": 38, "bonus_split": 55, "capex": 60,
    "press_release": 35, "postal_ballot": 18, "agm_egm": 18, "pledging": 62, "unclassified": 6,
}

# (category or "*", regex, adjustment, reason). Applied in order; all matches count.
ADJUST = [
    ("results", r"newspaper|publication", -62, "Newspaper ad of results already filed"),
    ("results", r"investor presentation|earnings presentation", -35, "Results deck (numbers are in the main filing)"),
    ("results", r"corrigendum|revised|clarification", -40, "Correction / clarification to results"),
    ("results", r"intimation|to consider|will be held|is scheduled", -55, "Only a notice of an upcoming board meeting"),
    ("management_change", r"chief executive|\bceo\b|managing director|\bmd\b|chief financial|\bcfo\b|chairman|whole[- ]time director|chief operating|\bcoo\b",
     35, "Top-management role (CEO/MD/CFO/Chair)"),
    ("management_change", r"demise", 40, "Death of a key person"),
    ("management_change", r"independent director|non[- ]executive", -10, "Board-level, non-executive role"),
    ("management_change", r"company secretary|compliance officer|senior management personnel|\bsmp\b", -20, "Junior KMP change"),
    ("management_change", r"re-?appoint", -15, "Re-appointment, not a change"),
    ("credit_rating", r"downgrad|negative|watch with negative", 45, "Rating downgrade / negative outlook"),
    ("credit_rating", r"upgrad|positive", 25, "Rating upgrade / positive outlook"),
    ("credit_rating", r"reaffirm|re-affirm|retained", -20, "Rating reaffirmed — no change"),
    ("credit_rating", r"withdraw", -15, "Rating withdrawn (often debt repaid)"),
    ("litigation", r"insolvency|nclt|fraud|forensic|default|arrest|search|seizure", 22, "Insolvency / fraud / default signal"),
    ("litigation", r"penalty of rs\.? ?[0-9,]{1,7}(\.\d+)?/?-? |no material impact|not material", -30, "Company says impact is immaterial"),
    ("M&A", r"incorporat|name change|strike[- ]off", -45, "Subsidiary housekeeping, not a deal"),
    ("M&A", r"completion|completed|consummat", -10, "Completion of a previously announced deal"),
    ("M&A", r"open offer|demerg|amalgamat|merger", 10, "Structural change to the company"),
    ("fund_raise", r"buy[- ]?back", 8, "Buyback — capital returned to shareholders"),
    ("fund_raise", r"\bqip\b|qualified institution|preferential|rights issue", 8, "Equity dilution"),
    ("fund_raise", r"commercial paper|\bncd|debenture", -25, "Routine debt issuance"),
    ("earnings_call", r"transcript", 20, "Transcript — updates the Promise Ledger"),
    ("earnings_call", r"audio|recording|link", -10, "Call recording link"),
    ("insider_trading", r"promoter", 12, "Promoter-group transaction"),
    ("insider_trading", r"sold|sale|disposal|offer for sale", 8, "Insider selling"),
    ("pledging", r"release|revoc", -22, "Pledge released"),
    ("pledging", r"creation|invocation|invoked", 15, "New pledge / pledge invoked"),
    ("press_release", r"rumou?r|news (item|verification)|clarification", 20, "Exchange-sought clarification on news"),
    ("press_release", r"spurt in volume|price movement", 10, "Exchange query on unusual trading"),
    ("press_release", r"order|contract", 12, "Order win / contract"),
    ("press_release", r"fire|strike|lockout|disruption|closure|suspension|cancell", 30, "Operational disruption"),
    ("press_release", r"monthly business update|production|sales volume", 5, "Monthly operating data"),
    ("dividend", r"special dividend", 15, "Special dividend"),
    ("unclassified", r"resignation of statutory auditor|auditor.*resign", 69, "Auditor resigned mid-term — governance red flag"),
    ("unclassified", r"related party", 20, "Material related-party transaction"),
    ("unclassified", r"deviation|variation", 10, "Statement of deviation in use of funds"),
]
_ADJ = [(c, re.compile(p, re.I), a, r) for c, p, a, r in ADJUST]


def tier(score: int) -> str:
    return "critical" if score >= 75 else "notable" if score >= 45 else "routine"


def score(category: str, exch_category: str, text: str, weight: float = 0.0, price_move_pct: float | None = None,
          move_threshold: float = 4.0) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    s = BASE.get(category, 10)
    reasons = [f"{category.replace('_', ' ')} base {s}"]
    blob = f"{exch_category} {text}"
    for c, rx, adj, why in _ADJ:
        if c == category and rx.search(blob):
            s += adj
            reasons.append(f"{why} ({adj:+d})")
    if weight and s >= 30:
        bump = round(min(8, weight * 0.8))
        if bump:
            s += bump
            reasons.append(f"{weight:.1f}% of portfolio (+{bump})")
    if price_move_pct is not None and abs(price_move_pct) >= move_threshold and s >= 30:
        s += 10
        reasons.append(f"Stock moved {price_move_pct:+.1f}% that day (+10)")
    return max(0, min(100, s)), reasons

```

```
"""Guards against invented figures: every number in generated prose must exist in the evidence it was given."""
import re

_NUM = re.compile(r"(?<![A-Za-z])[-+]?\d[\d,]*(?:\.\d+)?")
# Numbers that carry no factual claim: years, quarter/FY labels, list ordinals.
_IGNORE = re.compile(r"\b(?:FY|Q[1-4]|H[12])\s?'?\d{2,4}\b|\b(?:19|20)\d{2}\b", re.I)


def numbers(text: str) -> list[float]:
    text = _IGNORE.sub(" ", text or "")
    out = []
    for m in _NUM.finditer(text):
        try:
            out.append(float(m.group().replace(",", "")))
        except ValueError:
            pass
    return out


def ungrounded(generated: str, evidence: str, tol: float = 0.011) -> list[float]:
    """Numbers in `generated` that do not appear (± rounding) in `evidence`. Small integers (≤10) are allowed."""
    ev = numbers(evidence)
    bad = []
    for n in numbers(generated):
        if abs(n) <= 10 and n == int(n):
            continue
        if not any(abs(abs(n) - abs(e)) <= max(tol * abs(e), 0.051) for e in ev):
            bad.append(n)
    return bad

```

`~\finn\backend\finn\push\enrich.py`

```
"""Per-announcement enrichment, chosen by category and materiality.

results            -> deterministic results card (XBRL / screener), no LLM
management_change  -> who/role/change + profile, extracted from the filing itself (SEBI requires a brief profile)
other material     -> one-line "why it matters", number-grounded against the filing text
"""
import datetime as dt
import re

from ..grounding import ungrounded
from ..llm import LLMUnavailable, chat_json
from ..sources import docs
from . import results

_BOILER = re.compile(r"^.*?has (informed|submitted to) the Exchange (regarding|about|that|,)?\s*", re.I)


def clean_text(text: str) -> str:
    t = _BOILER.sub("", text or "").strip(" .:-")
    return (t[:1].upper() + t[1:]) if t else ""


def _expected_qe(ts: dt.datetime) -> dt.date:
    """Quarter a results filing on `ts` most plausibly reports (latest quarter-end ≥ 10 days before)."""
    d = ts.date() - dt.timedelta(days=10)
    m = ((d.month - 1) // 3) * 3  # quarter start month - 1
    if m == 0:
        return dt.date(d.year - 1, 12, 31)
    return results._shift(dt.date(d.year, m, 1), 0)


def enrich_results(a) -> dict:
    card = results.results_card(a.symbol)
    if not card:
        return {"type": "results", "status": "unavailable"}
    exp = _expected_qe(a.ts).isoformat()
    if card["quarter"]["end"] < exp:
        return {"type": "results", "status": "pending", "expected_quarter": results.fy_label(dt.date.fromisoformat(exp)),
                "note": "Structured numbers for this quarter are not yet published on the exchange; will refresh automatically."}
    return {"type": "results", "status": "ok", "card": card}


MGMT_SYSTEM = """You extract management changes from an Indian listed company's exchange filing.
Use ONLY the filing text. Never add outside knowledge. Copy the profile wording from the filing.
Return JSON:
{"changes":[{"person":"full name as written","role":"designation","change":"appointed|resigned|retired|ceased|re-appointed|demise|redesignated",
"effective_date":"as written or null","reason":"as written or null",
"profile":"the brief profile / background text from the filing, verbatim, max 700 chars, or null",
"highlights":["up to 3 short phrases taken from the profile: prior employers, years of experience, qualifications"]}]}
If there is no change of a person, return {"changes":[]}."""


def enrich_management(a) -> dict:
    if not a.pdf_url:
        return {"type": "management_change", "status": "no_document"}
    doc = docs.pages(a.pdf_url, max_pages=6)
    body = "\n\n".join(f"[page {i + 1}]\n{t}" for i, t in enumerate(doc["pages"]))[:14000]
    try:
        out = chat_json("mgmt_profile", "fast", MGMT_SYSTEM, f"Exchange summary: {a.text}\n\nFiling:\n{body}", 1200)
    except LLMUnavailable as e:
        return {"type": "management_change", "status": "llm_unavailable", "detail": str(e)[:200]}
    flat = docs.norm(" ".join(doc["pages"]))
    changes = []
    for c in out.get("changes", [])[:4]:
        name = (c.get("person") or "").strip()
        surname = docs.norm(name.split()[-1]) if name else ""
        if not surname or surname not in flat:  # reject names the filing does not contain
            continue
        prof = c.get("profile")
        page = docs.find_quote(doc, prof[:160]) if prof else None
        c["profile_verified"] = page is not None
        c["page"] = page
        changes.append(c)
    return {"type": "management_change", "status": "ok" if changes else "empty", "changes": changes,
            "source": {"url": a.pdf_url, "pages": doc["n"]}}


ONE_LINER_SYSTEM = """You write the single most decision-relevant line about an exchange filing for a portfolio manager.
Rules: max 22 words; state the concrete fact (what, how much, when) then the implication in a few words;
use only facts and numbers present in the filing text; no hype, no advice; if the filing is routine say so plainly.
Return JSON: {"line": "..."}"""


def enrich_one_liner(a) -> dict:
    evidence = a.text or ""
    if a.pdf_url:
        try:
            evidence += "\n" + "\n".join(docs.pages(a.pdf_url, max_pages=3)["pages"])
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — a missing PDF should not block the summary
            pass
    try:
        out = chat_json("one_liner", "fast", ONE_LINER_SYSTEM,
                        f"Company: {a.symbol}\nCategory: {a.category}\nFiling text:\n{evidence[:9000]}", 120)
        line = (out.get("line") or "").strip()
    except LLMUnavailable:
        line = ""
    if not line or ungrounded(line, evidence):
        return {"type": "summary", "status": "fallback", "line": clean_text(a.text)[:220]}
    return {"type": "summary", "status": "ok", "line": line}


def enrich(a) -> dict | None:
    if a.category == "results" and a.materiality >= 60:
        return enrich_results(a)
    if a.category == "management_change" and a.materiality >= 45:
        return enrich_management(a)
    if a.materiality >= 45:
        return enrich_one_liner(a)
    return None

```

```
"""EOD prices: yfinance primary, NSE bhavcopy fallback for the latest session."""
import datetime as dt
import io
import zipfile

import requests
import yfinance as yf
from sqlalchemy import select

from ..config import UA
from ..db import PriceEOD, session


def _upsert(rows: list[dict]):
    with session() as s:
        for r in rows:
            s.merge(PriceEOD(**r))


def from_yfinance(symbols: list[str], period: str = "6mo") -> int:
    df = yf.download([f"{x}.NS" for x in symbols], period=period, interval="1d", group_by="ticker",
                     auto_adjust=False, progress=False, threads=True)
    rows = []
    for sym in symbols:
        try:
            sub = df[f"{sym}.NS"].dropna(subset=["Close"])
        except KeyError:
            continue
        for idx, r in sub.iterrows():
            rows.append({"symbol": sym, "date": idx.date(), "open": float(r["Open"]), "high": float(r["High"]),
                         "low": float(r["Low"]), "close": float(r["Close"]), "volume": float(r["Volume"])})
    _upsert(rows)
    return len(rows)


def from_bhavcopy(symbols: list[str], day: dt.date) -> int:
    url = f"https://nsearchives.nseindia.com/content/cm/BhavCopy_NSE_CM_0_0_0_{day:%Y%m%d}_F_0000.csv.zip"
    r = requests.get(url, headers={"User-Agent": UA}, timeout=30)
    if r.status_code != 200:
        return 0
    z = zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(r.content))
    lines = z.read(z.namelist()[0]).decode().splitlines()
    hdr = lines[0].split(",")
    ix = {k: hdr.index(k) for k in ("TckrSymb", "SctySrs", "OpnPric", "HghPric", "LwPric", "ClsPric", "TtlTradgVol")}
    want, rows = set(symbols), []
    for line in lines[1:]:
        f = line.split(",")
        if f[ix["TckrSymb"]] in want and f[ix["SctySrs"]] == "EQ":
            rows.append({"symbol": f[ix["TckrSymb"]], "date": day, "open": float(f[ix["OpnPric"]]),
                         "high": float(f[ix["HghPric"]]), "low": float(f[ix["LwPric"]]),
                         "close": float(f[ix["ClsPric"]]), "volume": float(f[ix["TtlTradgVol"]])})
    _upsert(rows)
    return len(rows)


def update(symbols: list[str]) -> dict:
    try:
        n = from_yfinance(symbols)
        return {"source": "yfinance", "rows": n}
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        day, n = dt.date.today(), 0
        for _ in range(5):
            n = from_bhavcopy(symbols, day)
            if n:
                break
            day -= dt.timedelta(days=1)
        return {"source": "bhavcopy", "rows": n, "yfinance_error": str(e)[:200]}


def history(symbol: str, days: int = 90) -> list[PriceEOD]:
    with session() as s:
        rows = s.scalars(select(PriceEOD).where(PriceEOD.symbol == symbol).order_by(PriceEOD.date.desc()).limit(days))
        return list(reversed(list(rows)))

```

```
"""Triggered alerts, evaluated on read against the user's current thresholds.

Price: |daily return| ≥ X%  (also reports the move in σ of the stock's own recent volatility)
Volume: volume ≥ k × trailing N-day average
Announcement: category in the user's watch-list and materiality ≥ threshold
Price/volume alerts are linked to a same-session filing when one exists — "volume 3× · results filed 15:42".
"""
import datetime as dt
import statistics

from sqlalchemy import select

from ..db import Announcement, Company, session
from ..sources.prices import history

MARKET_CLOSE = dt.time(15, 30)


def _session_date(ts: dt.datetime, trading_days: list[dt.date]) -> dt.date | None:
    """The trading session in which the market could first react to a filing made at `ts`."""
    d = ts.date() if ts.time() < MARKET_CLOSE else ts.date() + dt.timedelta(days=1)
    for t in trading_days:
        if t >= d:
            return t
    return None


def eod_moves(symbol: str, settings: dict, days: int = 7) -> list[dict]:
    override = (settings.get("overrides") or {}).get(symbol, {})
    pct_th = override.get("price_move_pct", settings["price_move_pct"])
    vol_th = override.get("volume_multiple", settings["volume_multiple"])
    look = settings["volume_lookback_days"]
    rows = history(symbol, look + days + 5)
    out = []
    for i in range(max(look + 1, len(rows) - days), len(rows)):
        cur, prev = rows[i], rows[i - 1]
        window = rows[i - look:i]
        rets = [window[j].close / window[j - 1].close - 1 for j in range(1, len(window))]
        sigma = statistics.pstdev(rets) if len(rets) > 5 else None
        ret = cur.close / prev.close - 1
        avg_vol = statistics.fmean([w.volume for w in window if w.volume]) if window else None
        mult = cur.volume / avg_vol if avg_vol and cur.volume else None
        z = ret / sigma if sigma else None
        base = {"symbol": symbol, "date": cur.date.isoformat(), "close": round(cur.close, 2),
                "ret_pct": round(ret * 100, 2), "sigma": round(z, 1) if z is not None else None,
                "vol_mult": round(mult, 2) if mult else None, "volume": cur.volume}
        if abs(ret) * 100 >= pct_th:
            out.append({**base, "kind": "price", "threshold": pct_th,
                        "title": f"{symbol} {ret * 100:+.1f}% to ₹{cur.close:,.2f}"
                                 + (f" · {abs(z):.1f}σ move" if z is not None else "")})
        if mult and mult >= vol_th:
            out.append({**base, "kind": "volume", "threshold": vol_th,
                        "title": f"{symbol} volume {mult:.1f}× its {look}-day average"
                                 + f" · price {ret * 100:+.1f}%"})
    return out


def compute(settings: dict, days: int = 7) -> list[dict]:
    with session() as s:
        symbols = list(s.scalars(select(Company.symbol).where(Company.in_portfolio)))
        since = dt.datetime.now() - dt.timedelta(days=days + 3)
        anns = list(s.scalars(select(Announcement).where(Announcement.ts >= since, Announcement.symbol.in_(symbols))))
    alerts = []
    for sym in symbols:
        trading = [r.date for r in history(sym, 40)]
        moves = eod_moves(sym, settings, days)
        sym_anns = [a for a in anns if a.symbol == sym and a.materiality >= 25]
        for m in moves:
            d = dt.date.fromisoformat(m["date"])
            linked = [a for a in sym_anns if _session_date(a.ts, trading) == d]
            linked.sort(key=lambda a: -a.materiality)
            if linked:
                a = linked[0]
                m["linked"] = {"id": a.id, "category": a.category, "ts": a.ts.isoformat(), "text": a.summary or a.text}
            m["id"] = f"{m['kind']}:{sym}:{m['date']}"
            alerts.append(m)
    cutoff = dt.datetime.now() - dt.timedelta(days=days)
    for a in anns:
        if a.ts >= cutoff and a.category in settings["alert_categories"] and a.materiality >= settings["min_alert_materiality"]:
            alerts.append({"id": f"ann:{a.id}", "kind": "announcement", "symbol": a.symbol, "date": a.ts.date().isoformat(),
                           "ts": a.ts.isoformat(), "category": a.category, "materiality": a.materiality,
                           "title": f"{a.symbol} · {a.category.replace('_', ' ')}", "announcement_id": a.id,
                           "text": a.summary or a.text})
    alerts.sort(key=lambda x: (x["date"], x.get("ts", "")), reverse=True)
    return alerts

```

```
"""Push pipeline: fetch → classify → score → enrich. Idempotent; safe to run on a schedule."""
import datetime as dt
import json

from sqlalchemy import select

from ..config import BACKEND
from ..db import Announcement, Company, get_settings, now, portfolio_symbols, session
from ..sources import docs, nse, prices, screener
from . import classify, enrich, score
from .alerts import _session_date

PORTFOLIO_FILE = BACKEND / "portfolio.json"


def load_portfolio() -> list[str]:
    spec = json.loads(PORTFOLIO_FILE.read_text())
    with session() as s:
        for row in s.scalars(select(Company)):
            row.in_portfolio = False
        for p in spec["holdings"]:
            c = s.get(Company, p["symbol"]) or Company(symbol=p["symbol"])
            c.in_portfolio, c.weight = True, p["weight"]
            c.sector = p.get("sector") or c.sector
            s.merge(c)
    for p in spec["holdings"]:
        with session() as s:
            c = s.get(Company, p["symbol"])
            if not c.name:
                try:
                    sc = screener.company(p["symbol"])
                    c.name, c.bse_code = sc["name"], sc["bse_code"]
                except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
                    print("screener meta failed", p["symbol"], e)
    return [p["symbol"] for p in spec["holdings"]]


def _price_move(symbol: str, ts: dt.datetime) -> float | None:
    rows = prices.history(symbol, 60)
    days = [r.date for r in rows]
    d = _session_date(ts, days)
    if d is None:
        return None
    i = days.index(d)
    return (rows[i].close / rows[i - 1].close - 1) * 100 if i > 0 else None


def process(a: Announcement, weight: float, settings: dict, use_llm: bool = True):
    cat, how = classify.rule_classify(a.exch_category, a.text)
    if cat is None and a.pdf_url:  # peek at the document before paying for an LLM call
        try:
            first = " ".join(docs.pages(a.pdf_url, max_pages=2)["pages"])[:4000]
            cat, how = classify.rule_classify(a.exch_category, f"{a.text} {first}")
            how = "rule+pdf" if cat else how
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
            pass
    if cat is None:
        cat, how = classify.classify(a.exch_category, a.text, use_llm=use_llm)
    a.category, a.classified_by = cat, how
    a.materiality, a.reasons = score.score(cat, a.exch_category, a.text, weight, _price_move(a.symbol, a.ts),
                                           settings["price_move_pct"])


def ingest_announcements(days: int = 3, symbols: list[str] | None = None, use_llm: bool = True) -> dict:
    settings = get_settings()
    symbols = symbols or portfolio_symbols()
    to, frm = dt.date.today(), dt.date.today() - dt.timedelta(days=days)
    stats = {"fetched": 0, "new": 0, "enriched": 0, "errors": []}
    for sym in symbols:
        try:
            rows = nse.announcements(sym, frm, to)
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            stats["errors"].append(f"{sym}: {e}")
            continue
        stats["fetched"] += len(rows)
        with session() as s:
            weight = (s.get(Company, sym).weight if s.get(Company, sym) else 0) or 0
            for r in rows:
                if s.get(Announcement, r["id"]):
                    continue
                a = Announcement(**r)
                process(a, weight, settings, use_llm)
                s.add(a)
                stats["new"] += 1
    stats["enriched"] = enrich_pending(use_llm=use_llm)
    return stats


def enrich_pending(limit: int = 60, use_llm: bool = True) -> int:
    """Enrich material announcements that have no enrichment yet, or whose results numbers were pending."""
    with session() as s:
        cands = list(s.scalars(select(Announcement).where(Announcement.materiality >= 45)
                               .order_by(Announcement.ts.desc()).limit(400)))
    n = 0
    for a in cands:
        e = a.enrichment or {}
        needs = (a.enriched_at is None or e.get("status") in ("pending", "llm_unavailable", "fallback")
                 and (now() - a.enriched_at) > dt.timedelta(minutes=30))
        if not needs or (not use_llm and a.category != "results"):
            continue
        try:
            out = enrich.enrich(a)
        except Exception as ex:  # noqa: BLE001
            out = {"type": "error", "status": "error", "detail": str(ex)[:200]}
        with session() as s:
            row = s.get(Announcement, a.id)
            row.enrichment, row.enriched_at = out, now()
            if out and out.get("type") == "summary":
                row.summary = out.get("line")
        n += 1
        if n >= limit:
            break
    return n


def ingest_prices() -> dict:
    return prices.update(portfolio_symbols())

```

```
"""python -m finn.cli <command>

  init                 create tables + load portfolio.json
  prices               update EOD prices for the portfolio
  ingest [days]        fetch, classify, score and enrich announcements (default 2 days)
  enrich               retry pending / un-enriched material announcements
  research SYMBOL      build (or refresh) the research memo for any NSE symbol
  all [days]           prices + ingest — what the scheduler runs
"""
import json
import sys

from .db import init_db


def main(argv: list[str]):
    cmd = argv[0] if argv else "all"
    init_db()
    from .push import ingest
    if cmd == "init":
        print(ingest.load_portfolio())
    elif cmd == "prices":
        print(ingest.ingest_prices())
    elif cmd == "ingest":
        print(json.dumps(ingest.ingest_announcements(int(argv[1]) if len(argv) > 1 else 2), indent=1))
    elif cmd == "enrich":
        print(ingest.enrich_pending())
    elif cmd == "research":
        from .pull import research
        for sym in argv[1:]:
            r = research.build(sym.upper(), force="--force" in argv)
            print(sym, r.get("status"), [k for k, v in r.get("sections", {}).items() if v])
    elif cmd == "all":
        ingest.load_portfolio()
        print(ingest.ingest_prices())
        print(json.dumps(ingest.ingest_announcements(int(argv[1]) if len(argv) > 1 else 2), indent=1))
    else:
        print(__doc__)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main(sys.argv[1:])

```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T12:33:06Z

**→ Bash**

_Add penalty-vs-revenue materiality and reprocess_
```
cat >> finn/push/score.py <<'EOF'


_PENALTY = re.compile(r"penalt(?:y|ies)[^.]{0,90}?(?:rs\.?|inr|₹)\s?([\d,]+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(crores?|cr\b|lakhs?|lacs?|million)?", re.I)


def penalty_cr(text: str) -> float | None:
    """Largest penalty amount stated in the text, in ₹ crore."""
    best = None
    for m in _PENALTY.finditer(text or ""):
        try:
            v = float(m.group(1).replace(",", ""))
        except ValueError:
            continue
        unit = (m.group(2) or "").lower()
        cr = v if unit.startswith("cr") else v / 100 if unit.startswith(("lakh", "lac")) else v / 10 if unit == "million" else v / 1e7
        best = cr if best is None else max(best, cr)
    return best


def penalty_adjustment(text: str, annual_revenue_cr: float | None) -> tuple[int, str] | None:
    p = penalty_cr(text)
    if p is None or not annual_revenue_cr:
        return None
    share = p / annual_revenue_cr * 100
    amount = f"₹{p * 100:,.2f} lakh" if p < 1 else f"₹{p:,.1f} Cr"
    if share < 0.05:
        return -45, f"Penalty {amount} = {share:.4f}% of annual revenue — immaterial"
    if share > 1:
        return 15, f"Penalty {amount} = {share:.1f}% of annual revenue"
    return 0, f"Penalty {amount} = {share:.2f}% of annual revenue"
EOF
.venv/Scripts/python.exe - <<'PYEOF'

          move_threshold: float = 4.0) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:''', '''def score(category: str, exch_category: str, text: str, weight: float = 0.0, price_move_pct: float | None = None,
          move_threshold: float = 4.0, annual_revenue_cr: float | None = None) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:''')
s = s.replace('''    if weight and s >= 30:''', '''    if category == "litigation" and (pen := penalty_adjustment(blob, annual_revenue_cr)):
        s += pen[0]
        reasons.append(f"{pen[1]} ({pen[0]:+d})")

open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(s)
```

`~\finn\backend\finn\pull\financials.py`

```
"""Deterministic research sections from standardised statements. No LLM.

Every number is registered in a fact table with a stable id (e.g. "A.Sales.Mar 2026", "Q.OPM %.Sep 2026") so
LLM-written sections can cite facts instead of producing figures.
"""
import re

from ..sources import screener


class Facts:
    def __init__(self, source: str, url: str):
        self.source, self.url, self.items = source, url, {}

    def add(self, fid: str, label: str, value, unit: str, period: str) -> str:
        if value is not None:
            self.items[fid] = {"id": fid, "label": label, "value": value, "unit": unit, "period": period,
                               "source": self.source, "url": self.url}
        return fid

    def get(self, fid):
        return self.items.get(fid, {}).get("value")


def _series(tbl, row):
    if not tbl or row not in tbl["rows"]:
        return []
    return [(c, v) for c, v in zip(tbl["cols"], tbl["rows"][row])]


def _cagr(a, b, years):
    if a is None or b is None or a <= 0 or b <= 0 or years <= 0:
        return None
    return round(((b / a) ** (1 / years) - 1) * 100, 1)


def _fy(col: str) -> str:
    m = re.match(r"(\w{3}) (\d{4})", col)
    return f"FY{m.group(2)[2:]}" if m and m.group(1) == "Mar" else col


def build(symbol: str) -> dict:
    c = screener.company(symbol)
    t = c["tables"]
    lender = screener.is_lender(c)
    F = Facts("screener.in standardised statements", c["url"])
    A, Q, B, CF, R, SH = (t.get(k) for k in ("annual", "quarters", "balance", "cashflow", "ratios", "shareholding"))
    top_line = "Revenue" if lender else "Sales"
    op_row, margin_row = ("Financing Profit", "Financing Margin %") if lender else ("Operating Profit", "OPM %")

    # ---- register facts ----
    for tbl, prefix, rows in [(A, "A", [top_line, op_row, margin_row, "Net Profit", "Interest", "Depreciation", "Other Income", "Profit before tax", "EPS in Rs", "Dividend Payout %"]),
                              (Q, "Q", [top_line, op_row, margin_row, "Net Profit", "Profit before tax", "Other Income", "Gross NPA %", "Net NPA %"]),
                              (B, "B", ["Equity Capital", "Reserves", "Borrowings", "Borrowing", "Deposits", "Total Liabilities", "Fixed Assets", "CWIP", "Investments", "Other Assets"]),
                              (CF, "CF", ["Cash from Operating Activity", "Cash from Investing Activity", "Cash from Financing Activity", "Free Cash Flow"]),
                              (R, "R", ["Debtor Days", "Inventory Days", "Days Payable", "Cash Conversion Cycle", "Working Capital Days", "ROCE %", "ROE %"]),
                              (SH, "SH", ["Promoters", "FIIs", "DIIs", "Public"])]:
        for row in rows:
            unit = "%" if row.endswith("%") or prefix == "SH" else "days" if "Days" in row or "Cycle" in row else "₹" if row == "EPS in Rs" else "₹ Cr"
            for col, v in _series(tbl, row):
                F.add(f"{prefix}.{row}.{col}", row, v, unit, col)

    # ---- trajectory ----
    ann = [(col, v) for col, v in _series(A, top_line) if col != "TTM"]
    pat = dict(_series(A, "Net Profit"))
    traj_rows = []
    for col, rev in ann[-10:]:
        traj_rows.append({"period": _fy(col), "col": col, "revenue": rev, "op_profit": dict(_series(A, op_row)).get(col),
                          "margin": dict(_series(A, margin_row)).get(col), "pat": pat.get(col)})
    cagr = {}
    for yrs in (3, 5, 10):
        if len(ann) > yrs:
            a0, a1 = ann[-1 - yrs], ann[-1]
            cagr[f"revenue_{yrs}y"] = F.add(f"calc.revenue_cagr_{yrs}y", f"Revenue CAGR {yrs}y", _cagr(a0[1], a1[1], yrs), "%", f"{_fy(a0[0])}–{_fy(a1[0])}")
            cagr[f"pat_{yrs}y"] = F.add(f"calc.pat_cagr_{yrs}y", f"PAT CAGR {yrs}y", _cagr(pat.get(a0[0]), pat.get(a1[0]), yrs), "%", f"{_fy(a0[0])}–{_fy(a1[0])}")

    qrows = []
    qs = _series(Q, top_line)
    qpat = dict(_series(Q, "Net Profit"))
    qmar = dict(_series(Q, margin_row))
    for i, (col, rev) in enumerate(qs):
        yoy_col = qs[i - 4][0] if i >= 4 else None
        g = round((rev / qs[i - 4][1] - 1) * 100, 1) if yoy_col and rev and qs[i - 4][1] else None
        pg = round((qpat[col] / qpat[yoy_col] - 1) * 100, 1) if yoy_col and qpat.get(col) and qpat.get(yoy_col) and qpat[yoy_col] > 0 else None
        F.add(f"calc.Q.revenue_yoy.{col}", "Revenue growth YoY", g, "%", col)
        F.add(f"calc.Q.pat_yoy.{col}", "PAT growth YoY", pg, "%", col)
        qrows.append({"period": col, "revenue": rev, "revenue_yoy": g, "margin": qmar.get(col), "pat": qpat.get(col), "pat_yoy": pg})

    # Year-to-date growth for the current fiscal year (what full-year guidance is usually tracked against)
    ytd = None
    if qs:
        last = qs[-1][0]
        m = re.match(r"(\w{3}) (\d{4})", last)
        if m:
            fy_start_year = int(m.group(2)) if m.group(1) != "Mar" else int(m.group(2)) - 1
            if m.group(1) in ("Jan", "Feb", "Mar"):
                fy_start_year = int(m.group(2)) - 1
            cur = [(col, v) for col, v in qs if _in_fy(col, fy_start_year)]
            prev = [(col, v) for col, v in qs if _in_fy(col, fy_start_year - 1)][:len(cur)]
            if cur and len(prev) == len(cur) and all(v for _, v in cur + prev):
                s1, s0 = sum(v for _, v in cur), sum(v for _, v in prev)
                label = f"FY{(fy_start_year + 1) % 100:02d} YTD ({len(cur)}Q)"
                ytd = {"label": label, "revenue": round(s1, 1), "prior": round(s0, 1), "growth": round((s1 / s0 - 1) * 100, 1)}
                F.add("calc.ytd_revenue_growth", f"Revenue growth {label} vs prior-year period", ytd["growth"], "%", label)
                m_cur = [qmar.get(col) for col, _ in cur if qmar.get(col) is not None]
                if m_cur:
                    F.add("calc.ytd_margin", f"Average {margin_row} {label}", round(sum(m_cur) / len(m_cur), 1), "%", label)

    # ---- balance sheet ----
    bal = []
    borrow_row = "Borrowings" if B and "Borrowings" in B["rows"] else "Borrowing"
    for col, eq in _series(B, "Equity Capital"):
        res = dict(_series(B, "Reserves")).get(col)
        debt = dict(_series(B, borrow_row)).get(col)
        nw = (eq or 0) + (res or 0)
        de = round(debt / nw, 2) if debt is not None and nw else None
        F.add(f"calc.net_worth.{col}", "Net worth", round(nw, 1), "₹ Cr", col)
        F.add(f"calc.de.{col}", "Debt / equity", de, "x", col)
        bal.append({"period": col, "net_worth": round(nw, 1), "debt": debt, "de": de,
                    "deposits": dict(_series(B, "Deposits")).get(col)})
    wc = []
    for col, d in _series(R, "Debtor Days"):
        wc.append({"period": col, "debtor_days": d, "inventory_days": dict(_series(R, "Inventory Days")).get(col),
                   "payable_days": dict(_series(R, "Days Payable")).get(col),
                   "ccc": dict(_series(R, "Cash Conversion Cycle")).get(col), "wc_days": dict(_series(R, "Working Capital Days")).get(col),
                   "roce": dict(_series(R, "ROCE %")).get(col)})

    # ---- cash quality ----
    cash = []
    for col, cfo in _series(CF, "Cash from Operating Activity"):
        fcf = dict(_series(CF, "Free Cash Flow")).get(col)
        p = pat.get(col)
        capex = round(cfo - fcf, 1) if cfo is not None and fcf is not None else None
        conv = round(cfo / p * 100) if cfo is not None and p and p > 0 else None
        F.add(f"calc.capex.{col}", "Capex (CFO − FCF)", capex, "₹ Cr", col)
        F.add(f"calc.cfo_pat.{col}", "CFO / PAT", conv, "%", col)
        cash.append({"period": col, "cfo": cfo, "fcf": fcf, "capex": capex, "pat": p, "cfo_pat": conv})
    last5 = cash[-5:]
    conv5 = None
    if last5 and all(x["cfo"] is not None and x["pat"] for x in last5):
        conv5 = round(sum(x["cfo"] for x in last5) / sum(x["pat"] for x in last5) * 100)
        F.add("calc.cfo_pat_5y", "Cumulative 5y CFO / PAT", conv5, "%", f"{last5[0]['period']}–{last5[-1]['period']}")
        F.add("calc.fcf_5y", "Cumulative 5y free cash flow", round(sum(x["fcf"] or 0 for x in last5), 1), "₹ Cr",
              f"{last5[0]['period']}–{last5[-1]['period']}")

    signals = _signals(F, traj_rows, qrows, bal, wc, cash, conv5, lender, SH)
    top = c["top"]
    snapshot = {
        "name": c["name"], "symbol": symbol, "sector": c["sector"], "basis": c["basis"], "lender": lender,
        "market_cap": top.get("Market Cap"), "price": top.get("Current Price"), "pe": top.get("Stock P/E"),
        "roce": top.get("ROCE"), "roe": top.get("ROE"), "dividend_yield": top.get("Dividend Yield"),
        "book_value": top.get("Book Value"), "high_low": top.get("High / Low"),
        "ttm_revenue": dict(_series(A, top_line)).get("TTM"), "ttm_pat": pat.get("TTM"),
        "ttm_margin": dict(_series(A, margin_row)).get("TTM"),
        "about": c["about"], "url": c["url"], "bse_code": c["bse_code"],
    }
    return {"snapshot": snapshot, "trajectory": {"annual": traj_rows, "quarterly": qrows[-8:], "cagr": {k: F.get(v) for k, v in cagr.items()}, "ytd": ytd,
                                                 "margin_label": margin_row.replace(" %", "")},
            "balance_sheet": {"rows": bal, "working_capital": wc, "borrow_label": borrow_row},
            "cash": {"rows": cash, "cfo_pat_5y": conv5},
            "signals": signals, "facts": F.items, "concalls": c["concalls"], "annual_reports": c["annual_reports"]}


def _in_fy(col: str, start_year: int) -> bool:
    m = re.match(r"(\w{3}) (\d{4})", col)
    if not m:
        return False
    mon, yr = m.group(1), int(m.group(2))
    return (yr == start_year and mon in ("Jun", "Sep", "Dec")) or (yr == start_year + 1 and mon == "Mar")


def _signals(F, traj, qrows, bal, wc, cash, conv5, lender, SH) -> list[dict]:
    """Plain, computed observations, each tagged good/bad/neutral and tied to fact ids."""
    out = []

    def add(tone, text, facts):
        out.append({"tone": tone, "text": text, "facts": [f for f in facts if f in F.items]})

    if len(traj) >= 4:
        a, b = traj[-4], traj[-1]
        if a["margin"] is not None and b["margin"] is not None and abs(b["margin"] - a["margin"]) >= 2:
            tone = "good" if b["margin"] > a["margin"] else "bad"
            label = "Financing margin" if lender else "Operating margin"
            add(tone, f"{label} {'expanded' if tone == 'good' else 'compressed'} from {a['margin']}% ({a['period']}) to {b['margin']}% ({b['period']}).",
                [f"A.{'Financing Margin %' if lender else 'OPM %'}.{a['col']}", f"A.{'Financing Margin %' if lender else 'OPM %'}.{b['col']}"])
    r5, p5 = F.get("calc.revenue_cagr_5y"), F.get("calc.pat_cagr_5y")
    if r5 is not None and p5 is not None:
        tone = "good" if p5 >= r5 else "bad" if p5 < r5 - 5 else "neutral"
        add(tone, f"5-year revenue CAGR {r5}% vs PAT CAGR {p5}% — profits {'outgrew' if p5 > r5 else 'lagged'} sales.",
            ["calc.revenue_cagr_5y", "calc.pat_cagr_5y"])
    recent = [q for q in qrows if q["revenue_yoy"] is not None][-4:]
    if len(recent) >= 3:
        gs = [q["revenue_yoy"] for q in recent]
        if gs[-1] < gs[0] - 5:
            add("bad", f"Revenue growth decelerating: {gs[0]}% YoY ({recent[0]['period']}) → {gs[-1]}% ({recent[-1]['period']}).",
                [f"calc.Q.revenue_yoy.{recent[0]['period']}", f"calc.Q.revenue_yoy.{recent[-1]['period']}"])
        elif gs[-1] > gs[0] + 5:
            add("good", f"Revenue growth accelerating: {gs[0]}% YoY ({recent[0]['period']}) → {gs[-1]}% ({recent[-1]['period']}).",
                [f"calc.Q.revenue_yoy.{recent[0]['period']}", f"calc.Q.revenue_yoy.{recent[-1]['period']}"])
    if conv5 is not None and not lender:
        tone = "good" if conv5 >= 90 else "bad" if conv5 < 70 else "neutral"
        add(tone, f"Over 5 years, operating cash flow was {conv5}% of reported profit"
            + (" — profits are converting to cash." if tone == "good" else " — a meaningful share of profit is not turning into cash." if tone == "bad" else "."),
            ["calc.cfo_pat_5y"])
```

```
"""Concall transcripts → structured, quote-verified guidance (one long-context call per transcript, cached)."""
import datetime as dt
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

from ..llm import chat_json
from ..push.results import fy_label
from ..sources import docs

MAX_CHARS = 90_000

SYSTEM = """You are an equity analyst reading an Indian listed company's earnings-call transcript.
Extract only what MANAGEMENT said about the FUTURE: explicit targets, ranges, expectations and plans.
Ignore historical results, analyst opinions, and safe-harbour boilerplate. Use only this transcript.

Return JSON:
{"guidance":[{
  "metric":"revenue_growth|revenue|margin|ebitda|profit|volume|order_book|order_inflow|capex|capacity|debt|working_capital|market_share|segment|cost|return_ratio|launch|other",
  "subject":"what exactly, e.g. 'consolidated revenue', 'EBITDA margin', 'mobile segment volume'",
  "statement":"management's words copied EXACTLY from the transcript (one or two sentences, max 280 chars)",
  "target":"the target as stated, e.g. '15-17%', '₹1,000 crore', 'double-digit', 'margin expansion'",
  "low":number or null,"high":number or null,"unit":"%|Cr|units|x|other",
  "horizon":"period it applies to, e.g. 'FY27', 'H2 FY27', 'Q3 FY27', 'by FY28', 'next 2-3 years', 'medium term'",
  "kind":"quantitative|directional",
  "speaker":"name or role",
  "page":page number from the [[p.N]] marker}],
 "business":{"summary":"max 60 words: what the company does and how it makes money, in management's framing",
             "segments":[{"name":"segment","detail":"max 20 words, include any revenue-share figure management stated"}]},
 "analyst_focus":[{"topic":"max 8 words","quote":"analyst's words copied exactly, max 200 chars","page":number}]}

Rules: at most 14 guidance items, headline numbers first (growth, margins, profit, capex, orders).
analyst_focus: at most 4 — the issues analysts pressed hardest on. Copy text exactly; never paraphrase inside "statement" or "quote"."""


def quarter_discussed(call_date: dt.date) -> str:
    """A call in Nov discusses the Sep quarter; Feb → Dec; May → Mar; Aug → Jun."""
    m = call_date.month
    qe_month = {1: 12, 2: 12, 3: 12, 4: 3, 5: 3, 6: 3, 7: 6, 8: 6, 9: 6, 10: 9, 11: 9, 12: 9}[m]
    year = call_date.year - (1 if qe_month == 12 and m <= 3 else 0)
    day = 31 if qe_month in (3, 12) else 30
    return fy_label(dt.date(year, qe_month, day))


def pick(concalls: list[dict], n: int = 5) -> list[dict]:
    out, seen = [], set()
    for c in concalls:
        if not c.get("transcript") or not c.get("date"):
            continue
        q = quarter_discussed(dt.date.fromisoformat(c["date"]))
        if q in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(q)
        out.append({**c, "quarter": q})
        if len(out) == n:
            break
    return out


def extract(call: dict, idx: int) -> dict:
    doc = docs.pages(call["transcript"])
    body = "".join(f"\n[[p.{i + 1}]]\n{t}" for i, t in enumerate(doc["pages"]))[:MAX_CHARS]
    raw = chat_json(f"transcript:{call['quarter']}", "long", SYSTEM,
                    f"Transcript of the earnings call discussing {call['quarter']}:\n{body}", max_tokens=6000)
    kept, dropped = [], 0
    for j, g in enumerate(raw.get("guidance") or []):
        page = docs.find_quote(doc, g.get("statement") or "", g.get("page"))
        if page is None:
            dropped += 1
            continue
        g.update({"id": f"G{idx}.{j + 1}", "page": page, "call": call["quarter"], "call_label": call["label"],
                  "url": call["transcript"]})
        kept.append(g)
    focus = []
    for f in raw.get("analyst_focus") or []:
        page = docs.find_quote(doc, f.get("quote") or "", f.get("page"))
        if page:
            focus.append({**f, "page": page, "url": call["transcript"], "call": call["quarter"]})
    return {"call": call["quarter"], "label": call["label"], "url": call["transcript"], "pages": doc["n"],
            "guidance": kept, "dropped_unverified": dropped, "business": raw.get("business"), "analyst_focus": focus}


def extract_all(concalls: list[dict], n: int = 5) -> list[dict]:
    calls = pick(concalls, n)
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=3) as ex:
        futs = [ex.submit(extract, c, len(calls) - i) for i, c in enumerate(calls)]  # G1 = oldest
        out = []
        for c, f in zip(calls, futs):
            try:
                out.append(f.result())
            except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — one unreadable transcript should not sink the memo
                out.append({"call": c["quarter"], "label": c["label"], "url": c["transcript"], "error": str(e)[:200],
                            "guidance": [], "analyst_focus": []})
    return out

```

```
"""Promise Ledger: what management guided vs what the reported numbers show.

Reasoning runs over (a) quote-verified guidance items and (b) the deterministic fact table — never raw
documents — so the model judges, but cannot invent, evidence. Output ids and numbers are validated.
"""
import json

from ..grounding import ungrounded
from ..llm import chat_json

VERDICTS = ["delivered", "missed", "on_track", "at_risk", "goalposts_moved", "dropped", "too_early", "unverifiable"]

SYSTEM = """You audit management credibility for a public-markets investor: compare what management PROMISED on earnings
calls with what the company actually REPORTED. You receive GUIDANCE items (verbatim, with ids) from successive calls,
oldest first, and ACTUALS (reported figures, with fact ids). Use nothing else — no outside knowledge.

Group guidance about the same thing across calls into one row (e.g. FY27 revenue growth guided in three calls).
For each row decide a verdict:
- delivered: the horizon has ended and actuals meet the target
- missed: the horizon has ended and actuals fall short
- on_track / at_risk: horizon still running; judge from year-to-date or latest-quarter actuals
- goalposts_moved: the target was lowered, softened or pushed out in a later call
- dropped: a specific promise that later calls stop mentioning, with no explanation
- too_early: horizon running and no actual yet bears on it
- unverifiable: actuals given cannot test it (e.g. non-financial promise with no later evidence)

Rules:
- Quote numbers exactly as they appear in GUIDANCE or ACTUALS. Do not compute new numbers (no differences, no sums).
- cite guidance_ids and fact_ids that support each row; fact_ids must be ids from ACTUALS.
- "trajectory": one entry per call where this promise was discussed: stance = set|reiterated|raised|lowered|softened.
- Prefer headline promises (revenue growth, margins, profit, capex, orders); at most 8 rows, most material first.

Return JSON:
{"rows":[{"subject":"short label, e.g. 'FY27 revenue growth'","metric":"metric type","promise":"max 18 words: target + horizon",
  "guidance_ids":["G1.2"],"trajectory":[{"call":"Q4 FY26","guidance_id":"G1.2","stance":"set"}],
  "delivered":"max 25 words: what actually happened","fact_ids":["calc.ytd_revenue_growth"],
  "verdict":"one verdict","why":"max 30 words"}],
 "credibility":"max 45 words: overall read on whether this management's guidance can be relied on, and in which areas"}"""


def _actuals_text(facts: dict) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
    keep = []
    for fid, f in facts.items():
        if fid.startswith(("calc.", "Q.", "A.")) and not fid.startswith(("Q.Other", "A.Other", "A.EPS", "A.Interest", "A.Depreciation")):
            keep.append(fid)
    # Most recent periods matter most; cap the prompt size.
    keep = keep[-260:]
    lines = [f"{fid} = {facts[fid]['value']} {facts[fid]['unit']} ({facts[fid]['label']}, {facts[fid]['period']})" for fid in keep]
    return "\n".join(lines), keep


def _guidance_text(calls: list[dict]) -> str:
    out = []
    for c in sorted(calls, key=lambda c: c["guidance"][0]["id"] if c.get("guidance") else "Z"):
        for g in c.get("guidance", []):
            out.append(f"{g['id']} | call {g['call']} | {g['metric']} | {g.get('subject')} | target: {g.get('target')} | "
                       f"horizon: {g.get('horizon')} | \"{g['statement']}\"")
    return "\n".join(out)


def build(calls: list[dict], facts: dict, latest_quarter: str) -> dict:
    guidance = {g["id"]: g for c in calls for g in c.get("guidance", [])}
    if len(guidance) < 2:
        return {"status": "insufficient", "rows": [], "note": "Not enough verifiable guidance in the available transcripts."}
    actuals, fact_ids = _actuals_text(facts)
    gtext = _guidance_text(calls)
    call_order = [c["call"] for c in reversed(calls)]  # oldest → newest
    user = (f"Latest reported quarter: {latest_quarter}. Calls (oldest→newest): {', '.join(call_order)}.\n\n"
            f"GUIDANCE:\n{gtext}\n\nACTUALS:\n{actuals}")
    evidence = gtext + "\n" + actuals
    out = chat_json("ledger", "reason", SYSTEM, user, max_tokens=5000)
    rows, problems = _validate(out, guidance, set(fact_ids), evidence)
    if problems:
        retry = user + "\n\nYour previous answer had these problems; fix them:\n- " + "\n- ".join(problems[:12])
        out2 = chat_json("ledger_retry", "reason", SYSTEM, retry, max_tokens=5000)
        rows2, problems2 = _validate(out2, guidance, set(fact_ids), evidence)
        if len(problems2) < len(problems):
            out, rows, problems = out2, rows2, problems2
    for r in rows:
        stances = {t["call"]: t for t in r.get("trajectory") or [] if t.get("call") in call_order}
        r["timeline"] = [{"call": c, **({"stance": stances[c]["stance"], "guidance_id": stances[c].get("guidance_id")}
                                         if c in stances else {"stance": "silent"})} for c in call_order]
        r["evidence"] = [guidance[g] for g in r["guidance_ids"]]
        r["facts"] = [facts[f] for f in r["fact_ids"]]
    counts = {v: sum(1 for r in rows if r["verdict"] == v) for v in VERDICTS}
    return {"status": "ok", "rows": rows, "credibility": out.get("credibility"), "counts": counts, "calls": call_order,
            "warnings": problems}


def _validate(out: dict, guidance: dict, fact_ids: set, evidence: str):
    rows, problems = [], []
    for i, r in enumerate((out.get("rows") or [])[:8]):
        r["guidance_ids"] = [g for g in r.get("guidance_ids") or [] if g in guidance]
        bad_facts = [f for f in r.get("fact_ids") or [] if f not in fact_ids]
        r["fact_ids"] = [f for f in r.get("fact_ids") or [] if f in fact_ids]
        if not r["guidance_ids"]:
            problems.append(f"row {i + 1} ('{r.get('subject')}') cites no valid guidance id")
            continue
        if bad_facts:
            problems.append(f"row {i + 1} cites unknown fact ids {bad_facts}")
        if r.get("verdict") not in VERDICTS:
            problems.append(f"row {i + 1} has invalid verdict {r.get('verdict')!r}")
            r["verdict"] = "unverifiable"
        text = " ".join(str(r.get(k) or "") for k in ("promise", "delivered", "why"))
        if bad := ungrounded(text, evidence):
            problems.append(f"row {i + 1} uses numbers not in GUIDANCE/ACTUALS: {bad[:5]}")
            r["grounded"] = False
        else:
            r["grounded"] = True
        rows.append(r)
    return rows, problems


def compact(ledger: dict) -> str:
    """Short text form for downstream prompts."""
    return json.dumps([{"id": f"L{i + 1}", "subject": r["subject"], "verdict": r["verdict"], "why": r.get("why")}
                       for i, r in enumerate(ledger.get("rows", []))])

```

```
"""Bull vs bear, written only from computed signals, the Promise Ledger and verified transcript quotes."""
from ..grounding import ungrounded
from ..llm import chat_json
from .ledger import compact

SYSTEM = """You write the tightest possible bull and bear case on a listed company for a portfolio manager.
You may use ONLY the EVIDENCE provided (computed signals S*, promise-ledger rows L*, management guidance G*,
analyst questions A*, facts by id). No outside knowledge, no news, no valuation opinions beyond the facts given.
Quote numbers exactly as given; do not compute new ones.

Return JSON:
{"bull":[{"point":"max 28 words","refs":["S1","L2","G4.3","calc.cfo_pat_5y"]}],
 "bear":[{"point":"max 28 words","refs":[...]}],
 "watch":{"point":"max 25 words: the one thing in next quarter's numbers that would settle the debate","refs":[...]}}
Exactly 3 bull and 3 bear points; every point must cite at least one ref."""


def build(fin: dict, calls: list[dict], ledger: dict) -> dict:
    sig = {f"S{i + 1}": s for i, s in enumerate(fin["signals"])}
    focus = {f"A{i + 1}": f for i, f in enumerate([f for c in calls for f in c.get("analyst_focus", [])][:8])}
    latest = next((c for c in calls if c.get("guidance")), None)
    guid = {g["id"]: g for g in (latest or {}).get("guidance", [])}
    led = {f"L{i + 1}": r for i, r in enumerate(ledger.get("rows", []))}
    key_facts = {k: v for k, v in fin["facts"].items() if k.startswith("calc.") and not k.startswith("calc.Q.")}
    ev = ["SIGNALS:"] + [f"{k}: [{s['tone']}] {s['text']}" for k, s in sig.items()]
    ev += ["PROMISE LEDGER:", compact(ledger)]
    ev += ["LATEST GUIDANCE:"] + [f"{k}: {g.get('subject')} — {g.get('target')} ({g.get('horizon')}): \"{g['statement']}\"" for k, g in guid.items()]
    ev += ["ANALYST QUESTIONS:"] + [f"{k}: {f['topic']} — \"{f['quote']}\"" for k, f in focus.items()]
    ev += ["FACTS:"] + [f"{k} = {v['value']} {v['unit']} ({v['label']}, {v['period']})" for k, v in key_facts.items()]
    evidence = "\n".join(ev)
    out = chat_json("thesis", "reason", SYSTEM, evidence, max_tokens=1800)
    valid = set(sig) | set(focus) | set(guid) | set(led) | set(key_facts)
    lookup = {**{k: {"type": "signal", **v} for k, v in sig.items()}, **{k: {"type": "analyst", **v} for k, v in focus.items()},
              **{k: {"type": "guidance", **v} for k, v in guid.items()},
              **{k: {"type": "ledger", "subject": v["subject"], "verdict": v["verdict"]} for k, v in led.items()},
              **{k: {"type": "fact", **v} for k, v in key_facts.items()}}

    def clean(p):
        refs = [r for r in p.get("refs") or [] if r in valid]
        return {"point": p.get("point", ""), "refs": refs, "evidence": [lookup[r] for r in refs],
                "grounded": not ungrounded(p.get("point", ""), evidence) and bool(refs)}

    return {"bull": [clean(p) for p in (out.get("bull") or [])[:3]],
            "bear": [clean(p) for p in (out.get("bear") or [])[:3]],
            "watch": clean(out["watch"]) if isinstance(out.get("watch"), dict) else None}

```

```
"""Research memo orchestration for any NSE symbol.

Stage 1 (seconds, free): deterministic sections from standardised statements.
Stage 2 (one-time per transcript, cached): quote-verified guidance extraction.
Stage 3 (two reasoning calls): Promise Ledger + bull/bear.
State is persisted after each stage so the UI renders progressively by polling.
"""
import datetime as dt
import threading
import traceback

from sqlalchemy import func, select

from ..db import LLMCall, kv_get, kv_set, now, session
from ..llm import LLMUnavailable, available
from . import financials, ledger, thesis, transcripts

_running: set[str] = set()
_lock = threading.Lock()


def _key(sym: str) -> str:
    return f"research:{sym}"


def get(sym: str) -> dict | None:
    return kv_get(_key(sym))


def _usage(since: dt.datetime) -> dict:
    with session() as s:
        rows = s.execute(select(LLMCall.provider, LLMCall.model, LLMCall.cache_hit, func.count(), func.sum(LLMCall.tokens_in),
                                func.sum(LLMCall.tokens_out)).where(LLMCall.ts >= since, LLMCall.ok)
                         .group_by(LLMCall.provider, LLMCall.model, LLMCall.cache_hit)).all()
    out = {"calls": 0, "cache_hits": 0, "tokens_in": 0, "tokens_out": 0, "models": []}
    for p, m, hit, n, ti, to in rows:
        out["calls"] += n
        if hit:
            out["cache_hits"] += n
        else:
            out["tokens_in"] += ti or 0
            out["tokens_out"] += to or 0
        if f"{p}:{m}" not in out["models"]:
            out["models"].append(f"{p}:{m}")
    return out


def build(sym: str, force: bool = False) -> dict:
    sym = sym.upper().strip()
    prev = get(sym)
    if prev and not force and prev.get("status") in ("done", "partial"):
        age = now() - dt.datetime.fromisoformat(prev["updated"])
        if age < dt.timedelta(hours=24):
            return prev
    started = now()
    state = {"symbol": sym, "status": "running", "stage": "Reading financial statements", "started": started.isoformat(),
             "updated": started.isoformat(), "sections": {}, "errors": []}

    def save(**kw):
        state.update(kw)
        state["updated"] = now().isoformat()
        state["usage"] = _usage(started)
        kv_set(_key(sym), state)

    try:
        fin = financials.build(sym)
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        save(status="error", stage="", errors=[f"Could not load financial statements for {sym}: {e}"])
        return state
    secs = state["sections"]
    secs.update({k: fin[k] for k in ("snapshot", "trajectory", "balance_sheet", "cash", "signals")})
    state["facts"] = fin["facts"]
    secs["sources"] = {"statements": fin["snapshot"]["url"], "annual_reports": fin["annual_reports"][:3],
                       "concalls": [c for c in fin["concalls"] if c.get("transcript")][:5]}
    if not any(available().values()):
        save(status="partial", stage="", errors=["AI sections need an LLM API key (GEMINI_API_KEY or GROQ_API_KEY)."])
        return state
    save(stage="Reading the last 5 earnings-call transcripts")

    try:
        calls = transcripts.extract_all(fin["concalls"], 5)
        good = [c for c in calls if c.get("guidance")]
        secs["guidance"] = {"calls": [{k: c.get(k) for k in ("call", "label", "url", "pages", "dropped_unverified", "error")}
                                      | {"n": len(c.get("guidance", []))} for c in calls],
                            "items": [g for c in calls for g in c.get("guidance", [])]}
        latest_biz = next((c["business"] for c in calls if c.get("business")), None)
        secs["business"] = {"from_transcript": latest_biz, "source_call": next((c["call"] for c in calls if c.get("business")), None),
                            "source_url": next((c["url"] for c in calls if c.get("business")), None),
                            "analyst_focus": [f for c in calls[:2] for f in c.get("analyst_focus", [])][:6]}
        if not good:
            save(status="partial", stage="", errors=state["errors"] + ["No transcript could be read; ledger skipped."])
            return state
        save(stage="Auditing guidance against reported numbers")
        q = fin["trajectory"]["quarterly"]
        latest_q = q[-1]["period"] if q else "unknown"
        secs["ledger"] = ledger.build(calls, fin["facts"], latest_q)
        save(stage="Writing the bull and bear case")
        secs["thesis"] = thesis.build(fin, calls, secs["ledger"])
        save(status="done", stage="")
    except LLMUnavailable as e:
        save(status="partial", stage="", errors=state["errors"] + [f"AI unavailable: {e}"[:300]])
    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
        traceback.print_exc()
        save(status="partial", stage="", errors=state["errors"] + [f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"[:300]])
    return state


def start(sym: str, force: bool = False) -> dict:
    """Kick off a background build; return current state immediately."""
    sym = sym.upper().strip()
    prev = get(sym)
    with _lock:
        if sym in _running:
            return prev or {"symbol": sym, "status": "running", "stage": "Starting", "sections": {}}
        fresh = prev and prev.get("status") in ("done", "partial") and \
            now() - dt.datetime.fromisoformat(prev["updated"]) < dt.timedelta(hours=24)
        if fresh and not force:
            return prev
        _running.add(sym)

    def run():
        try:
            build(sym, force=True)
        finally:
            with _lock:
                _running.discard(sym)

    threading.Thread(target=run, daemon=True).start()
    return {"symbol": sym, "status": "running", "stage": "Starting", "sections": (prev or {}).get("sections", {})}

```

`~\finn\backend\.cache\fix_forward.py`

```
import re
p = "finn/pull/transcripts.py"
s = open(p, encoding="utf-8").read()
start = s.index("FORWARD = re.compile(")
end = s.index("re.I)", start) + len("re.I)")
new = '''FORWARD = re.compile(r"\\b(expect|will|would|shall|target|guid|aim|plan|going forward|anticipat|should|outlook|next|"
                     r"intend|envisag|confident|likely|looking (at|to)|on track|trajectory|by (fy|20)|over the (next|coming)|"
                     r"medium[- ]term|long[- ]term|aspir|ambition|endeavou?r|hope|estimate|project|"
                     r"see (a|the|this|it|that|growth|margin)|going to|in the range|upcoming|future|ahead)", re.I)'''
s = s[:start] + new + s[end:]
open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(s)

```

```
import csv
import datetime as dt
import io
import threading

import requests
from fastapi import BackgroundTasks, FastAPI, Header, HTTPException, Query
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.middleware.gzip import GZipMiddleware
from sqlalchemy import func, select

from .config import CORS_ORIGINS, DEFAULT_SETTINGS, INGEST_TOKEN, UA
from .db import Announcement, Company, LLMCall, get_settings, init_db, kv_get, kv_set, now, session
from .llm import available
from .push import alerts as alerts_mod
from .push import results
from .push.enrich import clean_text
from .push.score import tier
from .pull import research
from .sources import prices

app = FastAPI(title="FINN API", version="1.0")
app.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware, allow_origins=CORS_ORIGINS, allow_methods=["*"], allow_headers=["*"])
app.add_middleware(GZipMiddleware, minimum_size=1000)


@app.on_event("startup")
def _startup():
    init_db()
    with session() as s:
        empty = s.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(Company)) == 0
    if empty:
        from .push.ingest import load_portfolio
        threading.Thread(target=load_portfolio, daemon=True).start()


@app.get("/api/health")
def health():
    with session() as s:
        last = s.scalar(select(func.max(Announcement.ts)))
        n = s.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(Announcement))
    return {"ok": True, "llm": available(), "announcements": n, "latest_filing": last,
            "last_ingest": kv_get("last_ingest"), "server_time": now().isoformat()}


# ---------------- push ----------------

def _title(a: Announcement) -> str:
    e = a.enrichment or {}
    if a.category == "results" and e.get("status") == "ok":
        return f"{e['card']['quarter']['label']} results"
    if a.category == "management_change" and e.get("changes"):
        c = e["changes"][0]
        return f"{c.get('person')} — {c.get('change')} as {c.get('role')}"
    t = clean_text(a.text) or a.exch_category or ""
    return t if len(t) <= 140 else t[:137].rsplit(" ", 1)[0] + "…"


def _story(group: list[Announcement], names: dict, reaction: dict) -> dict:
    group.sort(key=lambda a: (-a.materiality, -(1 if a.enrichment and a.enrichment.get("status") == "ok" else 0), a.ts))
    p = group[0]
    return {"id": p.id, "symbol": p.symbol, "name": names.get(p.symbol, p.symbol), "category": p.category,
            "tier": tier(p.materiality), "materiality": p.materiality, "reasons": p.reasons, "ts": p.ts.isoformat(),
            "title": _title(p), "summary": p.summary, "text": clean_text(p.text), "exch_category": p.exch_category,
            "pdf_url": p.pdf_url, "enrichment": p.enrichment, "reaction": reaction.get((p.symbol, p.ts.date())),
            "related": [{"id": a.id, "ts": a.ts.isoformat(), "exch_category": a.exch_category, "text": clean_text(a.text)[:160],
                         "pdf_url": a.pdf_url} for a in sorted(group[1:], key=lambda a: a.ts)]}


def _cluster(rows: list[Announcement]) -> list[list[Announcement]]:
    """Same company + same category within 36h = one story (e.g. results outcome + press release + deck)."""
    groups: list[list[Announcement]] = []
    for a in sorted(rows, key=lambda a: a.ts):
        for g in groups:
            if g[0].symbol == a.symbol and g[0].category == a.category and a.category != "unclassified" \
                    and abs((a.ts - g[-1].ts).total_seconds()) < 36 * 3600:
                g.append(a)
                break
        else:
            groups.append([a])
    return groups


def _reactions(symbols: set[str]) -> dict:
    out = {}
    for sym in symbols:
        h = prices.history(sym, 30)
        for i in range(1, len(h)):
            out[(sym, h[i].date)] = round((h[i].close / h[i - 1].close - 1) * 100, 2)
    return out


@app.get("/api/digest")
def digest(hours: int = Query(24, ge=1, le=24 * 30)):
    since = dt.datetime.now() - dt.timedelta(hours=hours)
    with session() as s:
        names = {c.symbol: c.name or c.symbol for c in s.scalars(select(Company))}
        port = {c.symbol for c in s.scalars(select(Company).where(Company.in_portfolio))}
        rows = list(s.scalars(select(Announcement).where(Announcement.ts >= since, Announcement.symbol.in_(port))))
    stories = [_story(g, names, _reactions({g[0].symbol})) for g in _cluster(rows)]
    stories.sort(key=lambda x: (-x["materiality"], x["ts"]), reverse=False)
    stories.sort(key=lambda x: -x["materiality"])
    material = [s for s in stories if s["tier"] != "routine"]
    routine = [s for s in stories if s["tier"] == "routine"]
    by_cat: dict = {}
    for r in routine:
        by_cat.setdefault(r["category"], []).append(r)
    return {"window_hours": hours, "since": since.isoformat(), "generated": dt.datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "counts": {"filings": len(rows), "stories": len(stories), "critical": sum(s["tier"] == "critical" for s in stories),
                       "notable": sum(s["tier"] == "notable" for s in stories), "routine": len(routine)},
            "stories": material,
            "routine": [{"category": k, "count": len(v), "items": v} for k, v in sorted(by_cat.items(), key=lambda kv: -len(kv[1]))]}


@app.get("/api/alerts")
def get_alerts(days: int = Query(7, ge=1, le=30)):
    return {"settings": get_settings(), "alerts": alerts_mod.compute(get_settings(), days)}


@app.get("/api/settings")
def read_settings():
    return get_settings()


@app.put("/api/settings")
def write_settings(body: dict):
    cur = kv_get("settings") or {}
    for k, v in body.items():
        if k not in DEFAULT_SETTINGS and k != "overrides":
            continue
        if k in ("price_move_pct", "price_move_sigma", "volume_multiple") and not (0 < float(v) <= 50):
            raise HTTPException(400, f"{k} out of range")
        cur[k] = v
    kv_set("settings", cur)
    return get_settings()


@app.get("/api/portfolio")
def portfolio():
    with session() as s:
        comps = list(s.scalars(select(Company).where(Company.in_portfolio).order_by(Company.weight.desc())))
        since = dt.datetime.now() - dt.timedelta(days=7)
        counts = dict(s.execute(select(Announcement.symbol, func.count()).where(Announcement.ts >= since, Announcement.materiality >= 45)
                                .group_by(Announcement.symbol)).all())
    out = []
    for c in comps:
        h = prices.history(c.symbol, 30)
        last, prev = (h[-1], h[-2]) if len(h) >= 2 else (None, None)
        out.append({"symbol": c.symbol, "name": c.name, "sector": c.sector, "weight": c.weight,
                    "close": last.close if last else None, "date": last.date.isoformat() if last else None,
                    "change_pct": round((last.close / prev.close - 1) * 100, 2) if last and prev else None,
                    "spark": [round(r.close, 2) for r in h], "material_7d": counts.get(c.symbol, 0)})
    return out


@app.get("/api/company/{symbol}")
def company(symbol: str):
    symbol = symbol.upper()
    with session() as s:
        c = s.get(Company, symbol)
        anns = list(s.scalars(select(Announcement).where(Announcement.symbol == symbol).order_by(Announcement.ts.desc()).limit(40)))
    card = kv_get(f"results_card:{symbol}", dt.timedelta(hours=6))
    if card is None:
        try:
            card = results.results_card(symbol)
            kv_set(f"results_card:{symbol}", card)
        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
            card = None
    h = prices.history(symbol, 120)
    return {"symbol": symbol, "name": c.name if c else None, "in_portfolio": bool(c and c.in_portfolio),
            "weight": c.weight if c else None, "results": card,
            "prices": [{"d": r.date.isoformat(), "c": round(r.close, 2), "v": r.volume} for r in h],
            "announcements": [{"id": a.id, "ts": a.ts.isoformat(), "category": a.category, "tier": tier(a.materiality),
                               "materiality": a.materiality, "title": _title(a), "pdf_url": a.pdf_url} for a in anns]}


@app.get("/api/announcement/{aid:path}")
def announcement(aid: str):
    with session() as s:
        a = s.get(Announcement, aid)
        if not a:
            raise HTTPException(404)
        names = {a.symbol: (s.get(Company, a.symbol).name if s.get(Company, a.symbol) else a.symbol)}
    return _story([a], names, _reactions({a.symbol}))


# ---------------- pull ----------------

def _public(state: dict) -> dict:
    out = {k: v for k, v in state.items() if k != "facts"}
    facts = state.get("facts") or {}
    secs = out.get("sections", {})
    for sig in secs.get("signals") or []:
        sig["evidence"] = [facts[f] for f in sig.get("facts", []) if f in facts]
    return out


@app.post("/api/research/{symbol}")
def research_start(symbol: str, force: bool = False):
    return _public(research.start(symbol, force))


@app.get("/api/research/{symbol}")
def research_get(symbol: str):
    st = research.get(symbol.upper())
    if not st:
        raise HTTPException(404, "not started")
    return _public(st)


@app.get("/api/research")
def research_list():
    with session() as s:
        from .db import KV
        keys = list(s.scalars(select(KV.key).where(KV.key.like("research:%"))))
    out = []
    for k in keys:
        st = kv_get(k) or {}
        snap = (st.get("sections") or {}).get("snapshot") or {}
        out.append({"symbol": st.get("symbol"), "name": snap.get("name"), "status": st.get("status"), "updated": st.get("updated"),
                    "verdicts": ((st.get("sections") or {}).get("ledger") or {}).get("counts")})
    return sorted(out, key=lambda x: x.get("updated") or "", reverse=True)


def _equity_list() -> list[dict]:
    cached = kv_get("nse_equity_list", dt.timedelta(days=7))
    if cached:
        return cached
    r = requests.get("https://nsearchives.nseindia.com/content/equities/EQUITY_L.csv", headers={"User-Agent": UA}, timeout=30)
    r.raise_for_status()
    rows = [{"symbol": x["SYMBOL"].strip(), "name": x["NAME OF COMPANY"].strip()} for x in csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(r.text))]
    kv_set("nse_equity_list", rows)
    return rows


@app.get("/api/search")
def search(q: str = Query(..., min_length=1)):
    q = q.strip().upper()
    try:
        rows = _equity_list()
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — fall back to known companies
        with session() as s:
            rows = [{"symbol": c.symbol, "name": c.name or c.symbol} for c in s.scalars(select(Company))]
    starts = [r for r in rows if r["symbol"].startswith(q)]
    contains = [r for r in rows if r not in starts and (q in r["symbol"] or q in r["name"].upper())]
    return (starts + contains)[:12]


# ---------------- ops ----------------

@app.post("/api/ingest")
def ingest(bg: BackgroundTasks, x_finn_token: str = Header(default=""), days: int = 2):
    if not INGEST_TOKEN or x_finn_token != INGEST_TOKEN:
        raise HTTPException(401)

    def run():
        from .push import ingest as ing
        out = {"prices": ing.ingest_prices(), "announcements": ing.ingest_announcements(days), "at": now().isoformat()}
        kv_set("last_ingest", out)
    bg.add_task(run)
    return {"started": True}


@app.get("/api/usage")
def usage():
    with session() as s:
        rows = s.execute(select(LLMCall.task, LLMCall.provider, LLMCall.model, LLMCall.cache_hit, func.count(),
                                func.sum(LLMCall.tokens_in), func.sum(LLMCall.tokens_out), func.avg(LLMCall.ms))
                         .where(LLMCall.ok).group_by(LLMCall.task, LLMCall.provider, LLMCall.model, LLMCall.cache_hit)).all()
        by_cls = dict(s.execute(select(Announcement.classified_by, func.count()).group_by(Announcement.classified_by)).all())
    tasks: dict = {}
    for task, p, m, hit, n, ti, to, ms in rows:
        key = task.split(":")[0]
        t = tasks.setdefault(key, {"task": key, "calls": 0, "cache_hits": 0, "tokens_in": 0, "tokens_out": 0, "models": set()})
        t["calls"] += n
        t["cache_hits"] += n if hit else 0
        if not hit:
            t["tokens_in"] += ti
```

```
  --bg: #0d0f11;

  --note: #f2c14e;

  --up: #3dd68c;
  --down: #ff6b5e;
  color-scheme: dark;
}

@theme inline {
  --color-bg: var(--bg);
  --color-card: var(--card);
  --color-sunk: var(--sunk);
  --color-ink: var(--ink);
  --color-ink2: var(--ink2);
  --color-ink3: var(--ink3);

  --color-accent-soft: var(--accent-soft);
  --color-crit: var(--crit);

  --color-note: var(--note);

  --color-up: var(--up);
  --color-down: var(--down);
  --font-sans: "Inter", ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif;
  --font-serif: "Newsreader", ui-serif, Georgia, serif;
}

html, body {
  background: var(--bg);
  color: var(--ink);
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
  overscroll-behavior-y: none;
}

body { font-family: var(--font-sans); font-feature-settings: "cv11", "ss01"; }

.num { font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; font-feature-settings: "tnum"; }

.no-scrollbar::-webkit-scrollbar { display: none; }
.no-scrollbar { scrollbar-width: none; }

.safe-bottom { padding-bottom: calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom) + 64px); }

@keyframes sheet-in { from { transform: translateY(100%); } to { transform: translateY(0); } }
@keyframes fade-in { from { opacity: 0; } to { opacity: 1; } }
@keyframes shimmer { 0% { background-position: -200px 0; } 100% { background-position: 200px 0; } }

.sheet { animation: sheet-in 260ms cubic-bezier(.2,.8,.2,1); }
.fade { animation: fade-in 200ms ease-out; }
.skeleton {
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--sunk) 0px, var(--line) 80px, var(--sunk) 160px);
  background-size: 400px 100%;
  animation: shimmer 1.2s infinite linear;
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .sheet, .fade, .skeleton { animation: none; }
}
```

`~\finn\frontend\src\lib\api.ts`

```
const BASE = (import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL as string | undefined)?.replace(/\/$/, '') ?? ''

export async function api<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
  const r = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    ...init,
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...(init?.headers ?? {}) },
  })
  if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`${r.status} ${await r.text().catch(() => '')}`.slice(0, 200))
  return r.json() as Promise<T>
}

export type Tier = 'critical' | 'notable' | 'routine'

export interface ResultsRow { key: string; label: string; cur: number | null; prev: number | null; yoy: number | null; qoq_pct: number | null; yoy_pct: number | null }
export interface MarginRow { label: string; cur: number | null; prev: number | null; yoy: number | null; qoq_bps: number | null; yoy_bps: number | null }
export interface ResultsCard {
  kind: 'industrial' | 'bank' | 'lender'; basis: string; source: string; links: string[]; notes: string[]
  checks: { name: string; ok: boolean; detail: string }[]
  quarter: { label: string; end: string }; prev_label: string; yoy_label: string
  rows: ResultsRow[]; margins: MarginRow[]; headline: string
}
export interface MgmtChange {
  person: string; role: string; change: string; effective_date?: string | null; reason?: string | null
  profile?: string | null; highlights?: string[]; profile_verified?: boolean; page?: number | null
}

  type: string; status: string; card?: ResultsCard; changes?: MgmtChange[]; line?: string
  expected_quarter?: string; note?: string; source?: { url: string; pages: number }
}
export interface Story {
  id: string; symbol: string; name: string; category: string; tier: Tier; materiality: number; reasons: string[]
  ts: string; title: string; subject?: string | null; summary?: string | null; text: string; exch_category: string
  pdf_url?: string | null; enrichment?: Enrichment | null; reaction?: { date: string; pct: number } | null

}

  counts: { filings: number; stories: number; critical: number; notable: number; routine: number }
  stories: Story[]; routine: { category: string; count: number; items: Story[] }[]
}

  change_pct: number | null; spark: number[]; material_7d: number

  ret_pct?: number; sigma?: number | null; vol_mult?: number | null; close?: number; threshold?: number

  text?: string; announcement_id?: string; ts?: string
}
export interface Settings {
  window_hours: number; price_move_pct: number; price_move_sigma: number; volume_multiple: number
  volume_lookback_days: number; alert_categories: string[]; min_alert_materiality: number
}

export interface Guidance {

  speaker?: string; page: number; call: string; call_label: string; url: string
}

export interface LedgerRow {

  sections: {
    snapshot?: {

      pe?: string; roce?: string; roe?: string; dividend_yield?: string; book_value?: string; high_low?: string
      ttm_revenue?: number; ttm_pat?: number; ttm_margin?: number | null; about?: string; url: string
    }
    trajectory?: {
      annual: { period: string; col: string; revenue: number | null; op_profit: number | null; margin: number | null; pat: number | null }[]
      quarterly: { period: string; revenue: number | null; revenue_yoy: number | null; margin: number | null; pat: number | null; pat_yoy: number | null }[]
      cagr: Record<string, number | null>; ytd: { label: string; revenue: number; prior: number; growth: number } | null; margin_label: string
    }
    balance_sheet?: {
      rows: { period: string; net_worth: number; debt: number | null; de: number | null; deposits: number | null }[]
      working_capital: { period: string; debtor_days: number | null; inventory_days: number | null; payable_days: number | null; ccc: number | null; wc_days: number | null; roce: number | null }[]
      borrow_label: string
    }
    cash?: { rows: { period: string; cfo: number | null; fcf: number | null; capex: number | null; pat: number | null; cfo_pat: number | null }[]; cfo_pat_5y: number | null }
    signals?: { tone: 'good' | 'bad' | 'neutral'; text: string; facts: string[]; evidence?: Fact[] }[]
    guidance?: { calls: { call: string; label: string; url: string; pages?: number; n: number; dropped_unverified?: number; error?: string }[]; items: Guidance[] }
    business?: {
      from_transcript?: { summary?: string; segments?: { name: string; detail: string }[] } | null
      source_call?: string; source_url?: string
      analyst_focus?: { topic: string; quote: string; page: number; url: string; call: string }[]
    }
    ledger?: { status: string; rows: LedgerRow[]; credibility?: string; counts?: Record<Verdict, number>; calls?: string[]; warnings?: string[]; note?: string }
    thesis?: { bull: ThesisPoint[]; bear: ThesisPoint[]; watch: ThesisPoint | null }
    sources?: { statements: string; annual_reports: { year: number; url: string }[]; concalls: { label: string; transcript: string; ppt?: string }[] }
  }
}

  prices: { d: string; c: number; v: number }[]

}
```
