export const CATEGORY_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  results: 'Results', 'M&A': 'M&A', fund_raise: 'Fund raise', management_change: 'Management',
  litigation: 'Legal & regulatory', insider_trading: 'Insider trade', earnings_call: 'Earnings call',
  investor_meetings: 'Investor meets', credit_rating: 'Credit rating', dividend: 'Dividend',
  bonus_split: 'Bonus / split', capex: 'Capex', press_release: 'Company news', postal_ballot: 'Postal ballot',
  agm_egm: 'AGM / EGM', pledging: 'Pledging', unclassified: 'Routine paperwork',
}

export const cat = (c: string) => CATEGORY_LABEL[c] ?? c.replace(/_/g, ' ')

export function crore(v: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (v == null) return '—'
  const a = Math.abs(v)
  if (a >= 100000) return `${(v / 100000).toFixed(2)}L Cr`
  if (a >= 1000) return `${Math.round(v).toLocaleString('en-IN')} Cr`
  return `${v.toFixed(a < 10 ? 1 : 0)} Cr`
}

export function pct(v: number | null | undefined, digits = 1, sign = true): string {
  if (v == null || Number.isNaN(v)) return '—'
  return `${sign && v > 0 ? '+' : ''}${v.toFixed(digits)}%`
}

export function bps(v: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (v == null) return '—'
  return `${v > 0 ? '+' : ''}${v} bps`
}

export function rupee(v: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (v == null) return '—'
  return `₹${v.toLocaleString('en-IN', { maximumFractionDigits: v < 100 ? 2 : 1 })}`
}

export function ago(iso: string): string {
  const t = new Date(iso).getTime()
  const m = Math.round((Date.now() - t) / 60000)
  if (m < 1) return 'now'
  if (m < 60) return `${m}m`
  const h = Math.round(m / 60)
  if (h < 24) return `${h}h`
  const d = Math.round(h / 24)
  return d < 7 ? `${d}d` : new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })
}

export function clock(iso: string): string {
  const d = new Date(iso)
  return d.toLocaleString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hour12: false })
}

export const tone = (v: number | null | undefined) => (v == null ? 'text-ink3' : v > 0 ? 'text-up' : v < 0 ? 'text-down' : 'text-ink2')

export function pageLink(url: string, page?: number | null) {
  return page ? `${url}#page=${page}` : url
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react'
import { api } from './api'

const cache = new Map<string, unknown>()

/** Fetch with stale-while-revalidate from an in-memory cache, so tab switches feel instant. */
export function useApi<T>(path: string | null, deps: unknown[] = []) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<T | undefined>(() => (path ? (cache.get(path) as T | undefined) : undefined))
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const load = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!path) return
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      const d = await api<T>(path)
      cache.set(path, d)
      setData(d)
      setError(null)
    } catch (e) {
      setError(String((e as Error).message ?? e))
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [path, ...deps])
  useEffect(() => {
    if (path && cache.has(path)) setData(cache.get(path) as T)
    load()
  }, [load, path])
  return { data, error, loading, reload: load, setData }
}

// ---- tiny persistent store (per-viewer conveniences only) ----
function read<T>(key: string, fallback: T): T {
  try {
    const v = localStorage.getItem(key)
    return v ? (JSON.parse(v) as T) : fallback
  } catch {
    return fallback
  }
}
const listeners = new Set<() => void>()
const memo = new Map<string, unknown>()

export function usePersisted<T>(key: string, fallback: T): [T, (v: T) => void] {
  const get = () => {
    if (!memo.has(key)) memo.set(key, read(key, fallback))
    return memo.get(key) as T
  }
  const value = useSyncExternalStore((cb) => { listeners.add(cb); return () => listeners.delete(cb) }, get, get)
  const set = (v: T) => {
    memo.set(key, v)
    try { localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(v)) } catch { /* private mode */ }
    listeners.forEach((l) => l())
  }
  return [value, set]
}

export function useHashRoute(): [string[], (to: string) => void] {
  const parse = () => (location.hash.replace(/^#\/?/, '') || 'brief').split('/').map(decodeURIComponent)
  const [route, setRoute] = useState(parse)
  useEffect(() => {
    const on = () => { setRoute(parse()); window.scrollTo(0, 0) }
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', on)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('hashchange', on)
  }, [])
  return [route, (to: string) => { location.hash = `/${to}` }]
}

export function useInterval(fn: () => void, ms: number | null) {
  const ref = useRef(fn)
  ref.current = fn
  useEffect(() => {
    if (ms == null) return
    const id = setInterval(() => ref.current(), ms)
    return () => clearInterval(id)
  }, [ms])
}

```

import { useEffect, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type { Tier } from '../lib/api'

type IconName = 'brief' | 'bell' | 'search' | 'folio' | 'gear' | 'out' | 'chev' | 'check' | 'x' | 'doc' | 'undo' | 'spark' | 'back'

const PATHS: Record<IconName, string> = {
  brief: 'M4 5h16M4 10h16M4 15h10M4 20h7',
  bell: 'M6 16V11a6 6 0 1 1 12 0v5l1.5 2h-15L6 16Zm4 4a2 2 0 0 0 4 0',
  search: 'M11 18a7 7 0 1 0 0-14 7 7 0 0 0 0 14Zm5-2 4 4',
  folio: 'M4 19V9m5 10V5m5 14v-7m5 7V8',
  gear: 'M12 15a3 3 0 1 0 0-6 3 3 0 0 0 0 6Zm7.4-3a7.4 7.4 0 0 0-.1-1.2l2-1.6-2-3.4-2.4 1a7.5 7.5 0 0 0-2-1.2L14.5 2h-5l-.4 2.6a7.5 7.5 0 0 0-2 1.2l-2.4-1-2 3.4 2 1.6a7.4 7.4 0 0 0 0 2.4l-2 1.6 2 3.4 2.4-1a7.5 7.5 0 0 0 2 1.2l.4 2.6h5l.4-2.6a7.5 7.5 0 0 0 2-1.2l2.4 1 2-3.4-2-1.6c.1-.4.1-.8.1-1.2Z',
  out: 'M14 4h6v6m0-6-9 9M18 14v5a1 1 0 0 1-1 1H5a1 1 0 0 1-1-1V7a1 1 0 0 1 1-1h5',
  chev: 'm9 6 6 6-6 6',
  check: 'm5 12 5 5 9-10',
  x: 'M6 6l12 12M18 6 6 18',
  doc: 'M7 3h7l5 5v12a1 1 0 0 1-1 1H7a1 1 0 0 1-1-1V4a1 1 0 0 1 1-1Zm7 0v5h5M9 13h6M9 17h6',
  undo: 'M9 14 4 9l5-5M4 9h10a6 6 0 0 1 0 12h-3',
  spark: 'M12 3v4m0 10v4M3 12h4m10 0h4M6 6l2.5 2.5M15.5 15.5 18 18M6 18l2.5-2.5M15.5 8.5 18 6',
  back: 'm15 6-6 6 6 6',
}

export function Icon({ name, className = 'size-5', strokeWidth = 1.8 }: { name: IconName; className?: string; strokeWidth?: number }) {
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={strokeWidth} strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" className={className} aria-hidden>
      <path d={PATHS[name]} />
    </svg>
  )
}

export const tierColor: Record<Tier, string> = { critical: 'bg-crit', notable: 'bg-note', routine: 'bg-ink3' }

export function TierBar({ tier }: { tier: Tier }) {
  return <span className={`absolute left-0 top-3 bottom-3 w-[3px] rounded-r ${tierColor[tier]}`} aria-label={tier} />
}

export function Chip({ active, onClick, children, className = '' }: { active?: boolean; onClick?: () => void; children: ReactNode; className?: string }) {
  return (
    <button
      onClick={onClick}
      className={`h-8 shrink-0 rounded-full px-3 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors ${active ? 'bg-ink text-bg' : 'bg-sunk text-ink2 active:bg-line'} ${className}`}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  )
}

export function Pill({ children, className = '' }: { children: ReactNode; className?: string }) {
  return <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide ${className}`}>{children}</span>
}

export function Spark({ data, w = 64, h = 22, className = '' }: { data: number[]; w?: number; h?: number; className?: string }) {
  if (data.length < 2) return <svg width={w} height={h} />
  const min = Math.min(...data), max = Math.max(...data)
  const y = (v: number) => h - 2 - ((v - min) / (max - min || 1)) * (h - 4)
  const d = data.map((v, i) => `${i ? 'L' : 'M'}${((i / (data.length - 1)) * w).toFixed(1)},${y(v).toFixed(1)}`).join('')
  const up = data[data.length - 1] >= data[0]
  return (
    <svg width={w} height={h} viewBox={`0 0 ${w} ${h}`} className={className} aria-hidden>
      <path d={d} fill="none" stroke={up ? 'var(--up)' : 'var(--down)'} strokeWidth={1.5} strokeLinejoin="round" />
    </svg>
  )
}

export function Sheet({ open, onClose, title, children }: { open: boolean; onClose: () => void; title?: ReactNode; children: ReactNode }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return
    const prev = document.body.style.overflow
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => e.key === 'Escape' && onClose()
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)
    return () => { document.body.style.overflow = prev; window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey) }
  }, [open, onClose])
  if (!open) return null
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-end justify-center" role="dialog" aria-modal>
      <div className="fade absolute inset-0 bg-black/40" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="sheet relative max-h-[86vh] w-full max-w-xl overflow-y-auto rounded-t-2xl bg-card pb-[calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom)+20px)] shadow-2xl">
        <div className="sticky top-0 z-10 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line bg-card/95 px-4 py-3 backdrop-blur">
          <div className="text-[15px] font-semibold">{title}</div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="-mr-1 rounded-full p-1.5 text-ink2 active:bg-sunk" aria-label="Close"><Icon name="x" /></button>
        </div>
        <div className="px-4 pt-3">{children}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function SectionTitle({ children, right, id }: { children: ReactNode; right?: ReactNode; id?: string }) {
  return (
    <div id={id} className="mb-2 mt-6 flex scroll-mt-28 items-baseline justify-between px-1">
      <h2 className="text-[12px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.08em] text-ink3">{children}</h2>
      {right}
    </div>
  )
}

export function Card({ children, className = '', onClick }: { children: ReactNode; className?: string; onClick?: () => void }) {
  return <div onClick={onClick} className={`relative rounded-2xl border border-line bg-card ${className}`}>{children}</div>
}

export function Skeleton({ className = '' }: { className?: string }) {
  return <div className={`skeleton rounded-lg ${className}`} />
}

export function ExtLink({ href, children, className = '' }: { href: string; children: ReactNode; className?: string }) {
  return (
    <a href={href} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-accent underline-offset-2 hover:underline ${className}`}>
      {children}<Icon name="out" className="size-3.5" />
    </a>
  )
}

export function Empty({ title, children }: { title: string; children?: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="px-6 py-14 text-center">
      <div className="font-serif text-2xl">{title}</div>
      {children && <div className="mt-2 text-sm text-ink2">{children}</div>}
    </div>
  )
}

export function TabBar({ route, go, alertCount }: { route: string; go: (r: string) => void; alertCount: number }) {
  const tabs: { id: string; label: string; icon: IconName }[] = [
    { id: 'brief', label: 'Brief', icon: 'brief' },
    { id: 'alerts', label: 'Alerts', icon: 'bell' },
    { id: 'research', label: 'Research', icon: 'search' },
    { id: 'portfolio', label: 'Portfolio', icon: 'folio' },
  ]
  return (
    <nav className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-40 border-t border-line bg-card/90 pb-[env(safe-area-inset-bottom)] backdrop-blur-lg">
      <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-xl">
        {tabs.map((t) => {
          const on = route === t.id || (t.id === 'research' && route === 'memo') || (t.id === 'portfolio' && route === 'company')
          return (
            <button key={t.id} onClick={() => go(t.id)} className={`relative flex h-14 flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium ${on ? 'text-ink' : 'text-ink3'}`}>
              <Icon name={t.icon} strokeWidth={on ? 2.2 : 1.8} />
              {t.label}
              {t.id === 'alerts' && alertCount > 0 && (
                <span className="num absolute right-[calc(50%-20px)] top-1.5 min-w-4 rounded-full bg-crit px-1 text-[10px] font-bold leading-4 text-white">{alertCount}</span>
              )}
            </button>
          )
        })}
      </div>
    </nav>
  )
}

```

import { useRef, useState } from 'react'
import type { MgmtChange, ResultsCard, Story } from '../lib/api'
import { ago, bps, cat, clock, crore, pageLink, pct, tone } from '../lib/format'
import { Card, ExtLink, Icon, Pill, Sheet, TierBar } from './ui'

export function ResultsTable({ card, compact = false }: { card: ResultsCard; compact?: boolean }) {
  const rows = compact ? card.rows.filter((r) => ['revenue', 'ebitda', 'nii', 'pbt', 'pat'].includes(r.key)) : card.rows
  const failed = card.checks.filter((c) => !c.ok)
  return (
    <div>
      <table className="num w-full text-[13px]">
        <thead>
          <tr className="text-[11px] text-ink3">
            <th className="py-1 text-left font-medium">{card.quarter.label}</th>
            <th className="py-1 text-right font-medium">₹ Cr</th>
            <th className="py-1 text-right font-medium">QoQ</th>
            <th className="py-1 text-right font-medium">YoY</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {rows.map((r) => (
            <tr key={r.key} className="border-t border-line/70">
              <td className="py-1.5 text-ink2">{r.label}</td>
              <td className="py-1.5 text-right font-medium">{crore(r.cur).replace(' Cr', '')}</td>
              <td className={`py-1.5 text-right ${tone(r.qoq_pct)}`}>{pct(r.qoq_pct, 0)}</td>
              <td className={`py-1.5 text-right font-semibold ${tone(r.yoy_pct)}`}>{pct(r.yoy_pct, 0)}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
          {card.margins.slice(0, compact ? 1 : 2).map((m) => (
            <tr key={m.label} className="border-t border-line/70">
              <td className="py-1.5 text-ink2">{m.label}</td>
              <td className="py-1.5 text-right font-medium">{m.cur == null ? '—' : `${m.cur}%`}</td>
              <td className={`py-1.5 text-right ${tone(m.qoq_bps)}`}>{bps(m.qoq_bps).replace(' bps', '')}</td>
              <td className={`py-1.5 text-right font-semibold ${tone(m.yoy_bps)}`}>{bps(m.yoy_bps)}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      {card.notes.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mt-2 rounded-lg bg-note-soft px-2.5 py-2 text-[12.5px] leading-snug text-ink">
          {card.notes.map((n) => <p key={n}>{n}</p>)}
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-[11.5px] text-ink3">
        <span>{card.basis} · {card.source}</span>
        {failed.length === 0 && card.checks.length > 0 && (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 text-up"><Icon name="check" className="size-3.5" />{card.checks.length} checks passed</span>
        )}
        {failed.map((c) => <span key={c.name} className="text-note" title={c.detail}>⚠ {c.name.replace('matches', '≠')}: {c.detail}</span>)}
        {card.kind !== 'industrial' && <span>{card.kind === 'bank' ? 'Bank' : 'Lender'} format — EBITDA not meaningful</span>}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function MgmtBlock({ changes, src }: { changes: MgmtChange[]; src?: { url: string } }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {changes.map((c) => (
        <div key={c.person + c.role}>
          <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
            <div className="grid size-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-accent-soft text-[13px] font-semibold text-accent">
              {c.person.replace(/^(mr|ms|mrs|dr|shri|smt)\.?\s+/i, '').split(/\s+/).map((p) => p[0]).slice(0, 2).join('')}
            </div>
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="text-[14px] font-semibold leading-tight">{c.person}</div>
              <div className="text-[12.5px] text-ink2">
                <span className="capitalize">{c.change}</span> · {c.role}{c.effective_date ? ` · w.e.f. ${c.effective_date}` : ''}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          {!!c.highlights?.length && (
            <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1 pl-[46px] text-[13px] text-ink">
              {c.highlights.slice(0, 3).map((h) => <li key={h} className="relative before:absolute before:-left-3 before:text-ink3 before:content-['–']">{h}</li>)}
            </ul>
          )}
          {c.profile && (
            <div className="mt-2 pl-[46px]">
              <p className={`text-[12.5px] leading-relaxed text-ink2 ${open ? '' : 'line-clamp-2'}`}>“{c.profile}”</p>
              <div className="mt-1 flex gap-3 text-[11.5px]">
                <button className="text-accent" onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); setOpen(!open) }}>{open ? 'Less' : 'Full profile'}</button>
                {src && <ExtLink href={pageLink(src.url, c.page)}>{c.profile_verified ? `Verbatim from filing p.${c.page}` : 'Filing'}</ExtLink>}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
          {c.reason && <p className="mt-1 pl-[46px] text-[12.5px] text-ink2">Reason given: {c.reason}</p>}
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

function Body({ s }: { s: Story }) {
  const e = s.enrichment
  if (e?.type === 'results' && e.status === 'ok' && e.card) return <ResultsTable card={e.card} compact />
  if (e?.type === 'results' && e.status === 'pending') return <p className="text-[13px] text-ink2">{e.note}</p>
  if (e?.type === 'management_change' && e.status === 'ok' && e.changes?.length) return <MgmtBlock changes={e.changes} src={e.source} />
  const line = s.summary || (s.subject && s.subject !== s.title ? s.subject : null) || (s.text !== s.title ? s.text : null)
  return line ? <p className="line-clamp-3 text-[13.5px] leading-snug text-ink2">{line}</p> : null
}

export function StoryCard({ s, rank, onClear, onResearch }: { s: Story; rank?: number; onClear?: () => void; onResearch: () => void }) {
  const [why, setWhy] = useState(false)
  const [dx, setDx] = useState(0)
  const start = useRef<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null)
  const compact = s.tier === 'notable'

  const onDown = (e: React.PointerEvent) => { start.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY } }
  const onMove = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
    if (!start.current || !onClear) return
    const ddx = e.clientX - start.current.x, ddy = e.clientY - start.current.y
    if (Math.abs(ddx) > 12 && Math.abs(ddx) > Math.abs(ddy) * 1.5) setDx(Math.min(0, ddx))
  }
  const onUp = () => {
    if (dx < -110 && onClear) onClear()
    setDx(0)
    start.current = null
  }

  return (
    <div className="relative">
      {onClear && dx < 0 && (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-end rounded-2xl bg-accent-soft pr-5 text-[13px] font-semibold text-accent">
          <Icon name="check" className="mr-1 size-4" />Done
        </div>
      )}
      <Card className="touch-pan-y overflow-hidden transition-transform" >
        <div onPointerDown={onDown} onPointerMove={onMove} onPointerUp={onUp} onPointerCancel={onUp}
          style={{ transform: `translateX(${dx}px)`, transition: dx ? 'none' : 'transform 180ms ease-out' }}
          className="bg-card px-4 pb-3 pt-3">
          <TierBar tier={s.tier} />
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12px]">
            <button onClick={onResearch} className="font-bold tracking-wide text-ink">{s.symbol}</button>
            <span className="text-ink3">·</span>
            <span className="text-ink2">{cat(s.category)}</span>
            <span className="text-ink3">·</span>
            <span className="text-ink3" title={clock(s.ts)}>{ago(s.ts)}</span>
            {s.reaction && (
              <span className={`num ml-auto rounded-md px-1.5 py-0.5 font-semibold ${s.reaction.pct >= 0 ? 'bg-up/10 text-up' : 'bg-down/10 text-down'}`}
                title={`Close-to-close move in the first session after the filing (${s.reaction.date})`}>
                {pct(s.reaction.pct)}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
          <h3 className={`mt-1 font-semibold leading-snug text-ink ${compact ? 'text-[14.5px]' : 'text-[16px]'}`}>{s.title}</h3>
          {s.enrichment?.type === 'results' && s.enrichment.card?.headline && !compact && (
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-[12.5px] text-ink2">{s.enrichment.card.headline}</p>
          )}
          <div className="mt-2.5"><Body s={s} /></div>
          <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-1 text-[12px] text-ink3">
            <button onClick={() => setWhy(true)} className="num rounded-md bg-sunk px-2 py-1 font-medium text-ink2 active:bg-line">
              {rank ? `#${rank} · ` : ''}score {s.materiality}
            </button>
            {s.related.length > 0 && <span className="px-1.5">+{s.related.length} related filing{s.related.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}</span>}
            <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-3">
              {s.pdf_url && <ExtLink href={s.pdf_url} className="text-[12px]">Filing</ExtLink>}
              <button onClick={onResearch} className="inline-flex items-center font-medium text-ink">Research<Icon name="chev" className="size-4" /></button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>
      <Sheet open={why} onClose={() => setWhy(false)} title={`Why ${s.symbol} ranks here`}>
        <ol className="space-y-2 text-[14px]">
          {s.reasons.map((r, i) => (
            <li key={r} className="flex gap-2"><span className="num w-5 text-ink3">{i + 1}.</span><span>{r}</span></li>
          ))}
        </ol>
        <p className="mt-3 text-[13px] text-ink2">Score {s.materiality}/100 → <b className="capitalize">{s.tier}</b>. Rules are deterministic: the same filing always gets the same rank, and each adjustment is listed above.</p>
        <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-sunk p-3 text-[12.5px] text-ink2">
          <div className="font-medium text-ink">Exchange record</div>
          <div className="mt-1">{s.exch_category} · {clock(s.ts)}</div>
          {s.text && <div className="mt-1">{s.text}</div>}
        </div>
        {s.related.length > 0 && (
          <div className="mt-4">
            <div className="text-[12px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink3">Grouped into this story</div>
            {s.related.map((r) => (
              <div key={r.id} className="mt-2 border-t border-line pt-2 text-[13px]">
                <div className="text-ink2">{r.exch_category} · {clock(r.ts)}</div>
                <div>{r.text}</div>
                {r.pdf_url && <ExtLink href={r.pdf_url} className="text-[12px]">Filing</ExtLink>}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </Sheet>
    </div>
  )
}

export function RoutineGroup({ category, items }: { category: string; items: Story[] }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  return (
    <div className="border-t border-line first:border-t-0">
      <button onClick={() => setOpen(!open)} className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 text-left text-[14px]">
        <span className="flex-1 text-ink2">{cat(category)}</span>
        <span className="num text-ink3">{items.length}</span>
        <Icon name="chev" className={`size-4 text-ink3 transition-transform ${open ? 'rotate-90' : ''}`} />
      </button>
      {open && (
        <div className="space-y-2 px-4 pb-3">
          {items.map((s) => (
            <div key={s.id} className="flex items-start gap-2 text-[13px]">
              <span className="w-[86px] shrink-0 font-semibold">{s.symbol}</span>
              <span className="flex-1 text-ink2">{s.title}</span>
              {s.pdf_url && <a href={s.pdf_url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="text-ink3"><Icon name="doc" className="size-4" /></a>}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export { Pill }

import { useMemo } from 'react'
import { RoutineGroup, StoryCard } from '../components/Story'
import { Card, Chip, Empty, Icon, SectionTitle, Skeleton, Spark } from '../components/ui'
import type { Digest, Holding, Story } from '../lib/api'
import { cat, pct, tone } from '../lib/format'
import { useApi, usePersisted } from '../lib/hooks'

const WINDOWS = [{ h: 24, label: '24h' }, { h: 72, label: '3 days' }, { h: 168, label: 'Week' }, { h: 336, label: '2 weeks' }]

function headline(stories: Story[], routine: number, hours: number): string {
  const crit = stories.filter((s) => s.tier === 'critical')
  if (!stories.length) return routine ? `Nothing material. ${routine} routine filing${routine > 1 ? 's' : ''} folded away.` : 'A quiet tape. Nothing filed.'
  const names = [...new Set(crit.map((s) => s.symbol))]
  const lead = names.length
    ? `${names.slice(0, 3).join(', ')}${names.length > 3 ? ` and ${names.length - 3} more` : ''} need${names.length === 1 ? 's' : ''} you`
    : `${stories.length} thing${stories.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} worth a look`
  const span = hours <= 24 ? 'today' : hours <= 72 ? 'in the last three days' : hours <= 168 ? 'this week' : 'in the last two weeks'
  return `${lead} ${span}.`
}

function greeting() {
  const h = new Date().getHours()
  return h < 12 ? 'Morning brief' : h < 17 ? 'Afternoon brief' : 'Evening brief'
}

export default function Brief({ go, openSettings }: { go: (r: string) => void; openSettings: () => void }) {
  const [hours, setHours] = usePersisted('finn.window', 24)
  const [cleared, setCleared] = usePersisted<string[]>('finn.cleared', [])
  const { data, error, loading } = useApi<Digest>(`/api/digest?hours=${hours}`)
  const { data: folio } = useApi<Holding[]>('/api/portfolio')

  const visible = useMemo(() => (data?.stories ?? []).filter((s) => !cleared.includes(s.id)), [data, cleared])
  const done = (data?.stories ?? []).filter((s) => cleared.includes(s.id))
  const crit = visible.filter((s) => s.tier === 'critical')
  const notable = visible.filter((s) => s.tier === 'notable')
  const clear = (id: string) => setCleared([...cleared, id].slice(-400))
  const research = (sym: string) => go(`memo/${sym}`)
  const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long' })

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-xl px-4 pt-[calc(env(safe-area-inset-top)+12px)]">
      <header className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="grid size-7 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-ink font-serif text-[15px] font-semibold text-bg">F</div>
          <div className="text-[13px] text-ink2">{today}</div>
        </div>
        <button onClick={openSettings} className="rounded-full p-2 text-ink2 active:bg-sunk" aria-label="Alert settings"><Icon name="gear" /></button>
      </header>

      <div className="mt-5">
        <div className="text-[12px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.1em] text-accent">{greeting()}</div>
        {data ? (
          <h1 className="mt-1 font-serif text-[30px] leading-[1.1] tracking-tight">{headline(visible, data.counts.routine, hours)}</h1>
        ) : <Skeleton className="mt-2 h-16 w-4/5" />}
        {data && (
          <p className="num mt-2 text-[13px] text-ink2">
            {data.counts.filings} filings across your 19 holdings → {data.counts.critical + data.counts.notable} worth reading, {data.counts.routine} folded.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="no-scrollbar -mx-4 mt-4 flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-4">
        {WINDOWS.map((w) => <Chip key={w.h} active={hours === w.h} onClick={() => setHours(w.h)}>{w.label}</Chip>)}
      </div>

      {folio && (
        <div className="no-scrollbar -mx-4 mt-4 flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-4 pb-1">
          {folio.map((h) => (
            <button key={h.symbol} onClick={() => go(`company/${h.symbol}`)}
              className="shrink-0 rounded-xl border border-line bg-card px-2.5 py-1.5 text-left active:bg-sunk">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="text-[11.5px] font-bold">{h.symbol}</span>
                {h.material_7d > 0 && <span className="size-1.5 rounded-full bg-crit" />}
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-end gap-1.5">
                <span className={`num text-[12px] font-semibold ${tone(h.change_pct)}`}>{pct(h.change_pct)}</span>
                <Spark data={h.spark.slice(-15)} w={36} h={14} />
              </div>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {error && <Card className="mt-6 p-4 text-sm text-down">Couldn’t load the brief: {error}</Card>}
      {!data && loading && <div className="mt-6 space-y-3">{[0, 1, 2].map((i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-40" />)}</div>}

      {data && (
        <>
          {crit.length > 0 && <SectionTitle right={<span className="text-[11.5px] text-ink3">swipe left when done</span>}>Needs you</SectionTitle>}
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {crit.map((s, i) => <StoryCard key={s.id} s={s} rank={i + 1} onClear={() => clear(s.id)} onResearch={() => research(s.symbol)} />)}
          </div>
          {notable.length > 0 && <SectionTitle>Worth a look</SectionTitle>}
          <div className="space-y-2.5">
            {notable.map((s) => <StoryCard key={s.id} s={s} onClear={() => clear(s.id)} onResearch={() => research(s.symbol)} />)}
          </div>
          {!visible.length && data.stories.length > 0 && (
            <Empty title="You’re through.">Nothing else in your portfolio needs attention in this window.</Empty>
          )}
          {data.routine.length > 0 && (
            <>
              <SectionTitle right={<span className="num text-[11.5px] text-ink3">{data.counts.routine} items</span>}>Folded — routine</SectionTitle>
              <Card>{data.routine.map((g) => <RoutineGroup key={g.category} category={g.category} items={g.items} />)}</Card>
              <p className="mt-2 px-1 text-[11.5px] text-ink3">
                Folded: {data.routine.slice(0, 4).map((g) => cat(g.category).toLowerCase()).join(', ')}. Filings stay one tap away.
              </p>
            </>
          )}
          {done.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl bg-sunk px-3 py-2.5 text-[13px] text-ink2">
              <span>{done.length} cleared</span>
              <button className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-medium text-ink" onClick={() => setCleared(cleared.filter((id) => !done.some((d) => d.id === id)))}>
                <Icon name="undo" className="size-4" />Restore
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
      <div className="h-24" />
    </div>
  )
}

import { useState } from 'react'
import { Card, Chip, Empty, Icon, SectionTitle, Skeleton } from '../components/ui'
import type { Alert, Settings } from '../lib/api'
import { cat, clock, pct } from '../lib/format'
import { useApi } from '../lib/hooks'

const KIND = { price: 'Price', volume: 'Volume', announcement: 'Filing' } as const

function AlertRow({ a, go }: { a: Alert; go: (r: string) => void }) {
  const icon = a.kind === 'announcement' ? '◆' : a.kind === 'volume' ? '▮' : (a.ret_pct ?? 0) >= 0 ? '▲' : '▼'
  const color = a.kind === 'announcement' ? 'text-crit' : a.kind === 'volume' ? 'text-accent' : (a.ret_pct ?? 0) >= 0 ? 'text-up' : 'text-down'
  return (
    <button onClick={() => go(`company/${a.symbol}`)} className="flex w-full gap-3 border-t border-line px-4 py-3 text-left first:border-t-0 active:bg-sunk">
      <span className={`mt-0.5 w-4 text-center text-[13px] ${color}`}>{icon}</span>
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <div className="text-[14px] font-semibold leading-snug">{a.title}</div>
        {a.kind !== 'announcement' && (
          <div className="num mt-0.5 text-[12px] text-ink3">
            {a.kind === 'price' ? `threshold ±${a.threshold}%` : `threshold ${a.threshold}×`}
            {a.sigma != null && a.kind === 'price' ? '' : a.sigma != null ? ` · ${Math.abs(a.sigma)}σ price move` : ''}
          </div>
        )}
        {a.kind === 'announcement' && a.text && <div className="mt-0.5 line-clamp-2 text-[12.5px] text-ink2">{a.text}</div>}
        {a.linked && (
          <div className="mt-1.5 rounded-lg bg-sunk px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[12px] text-ink2">
            <span className="font-medium text-ink">Likely why:</span> {cat(a.linked.category)} filed {clock(a.linked.ts)} — <span className="line-clamp-1 inline">{a.linked.text}</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      <Icon name="chev" className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0 text-ink3" />
    </button>
  )
}

export default function Alerts({ go, openSettings }: { go: (r: string) => void; openSettings: () => void }) {
  const [days, setDays] = useState(7)
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<'all' | Alert['kind']>('all')
  const { data, loading } = useApi<{ settings: Settings; alerts: Alert[] }>(`/api/alerts?days=${days}`)
  const s = data?.settings
  const list = (data?.alerts ?? []).filter((a) => kind === 'all' || a.kind === kind)
  const byDay = list.reduce<Record<string, Alert[]>>((acc, a) => { (acc[a.date] ??= []).push(a); return acc }, {})

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-xl px-4 pt-[calc(env(safe-area-inset-top)+16px)]">
      <h1 className="font-serif text-[30px] leading-tight">Alerts</h1>
      <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink2">You decide what “unusual” means. FINN checks every end-of-day print against it and tells you what was filed that day.</p>

      <Card className="mt-4 p-3.5" onClick={openSettings}>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="text-[12px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink3">Your thresholds</div>
          <span className="text-[13px] font-medium text-accent">Edit</span>
        </div>
        {s ? (
          <div className="num mt-2 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-center">
            <div className="rounded-lg bg-sunk py-2"><div className="text-[17px] font-semibold">±{s.price_move_pct}%</div><div className="text-[11px] text-ink3">daily move</div></div>
            <div className="rounded-lg bg-sunk py-2"><div className="text-[17px] font-semibold">{s.volume_multiple}×</div><div className="text-[11px] text-ink3">{s.volume_lookback_days}-day volume</div></div>
            <div className="rounded-lg bg-sunk py-2"><div className="text-[17px] font-semibold">≥{s.min_alert_materiality}</div><div className="text-[11px] text-ink3">filing score</div></div>
          </div>
        ) : <Skeleton className="mt-2 h-14" />}
        {s && <div className="mt-2 text-[12px] text-ink3">Filing alerts for: {s.alert_categories.map(cat).join(', ')}</div>}
      </Card>

      <div className="no-scrollbar -mx-4 mt-4 flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-4">
        {(['all', 'price', 'volume', 'announcement'] as const).map((k) => (
          <Chip key={k} active={kind === k} onClick={() => setKind(k)}>{k === 'all' ? 'All' : KIND[k]}</Chip>
        ))}
        <span className="w-2 shrink-0" />
        {[3, 7, 14].map((d) => <Chip key={d} active={days === d} onClick={() => setDays(d)}>{d}d</Chip>)}
      </div>

      {loading && !data && <Skeleton className="mt-4 h-60" />}
      {data && !list.length && <Empty title="Nothing crossed your lines.">Loosen a threshold in settings, or widen the window.</Empty>}
      {Object.entries(byDay).map(([day, items]) => (
        <div key={day}>
          <SectionTitle right={<span className="num text-[11.5px] text-ink3">{items.length}</span>}>
            {new Date(day).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}
          </SectionTitle>
          <Card>{items.map((a) => <AlertRow key={a.id} a={a} go={go} />)}</Card>
        </div>
      ))}
      {data && list.some((a) => a.kind === 'price') && (
        <p className="mt-3 px-1 text-[11.5px] text-ink3">σ = the move measured in the stock’s own recent daily volatility, so a 3% move in a utility and in a small-cap don’t look the same. Moves: {pct(null)} uses close-to-close prices.</p>
      )}
      <div className="h-24" />
    </div>
  )
}

```
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Chip, Sheet } from '../components/ui'
import { api, type Settings } from '../lib/api'
import { cat } from '../lib/format'

const ALL = ['results', 'M&A', 'management_change', 'fund_raise', 'credit_rating', 'litigation', 'pledging', 'insider_trading',
  'capex', 'dividend', 'bonus_split', 'press_release', 'earnings_call']

function Stepper({ label, hint, value, step, min, max, unit, onChange }: {
  label: string; hint: string; value: number; step: number; min: number; max: number; unit: string; onChange: (v: number) => void
}) {
  const set = (v: number) => onChange(Math.min(max, Math.max(min, Math.round(v * 100) / 100)))
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-line py-3 first:border-t-0">
      <div>
        <div className="text-[14px] font-medium">{label}</div>
        <div className="text-[12px] text-ink3">{hint}</div>
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
        <button onClick={() => set(value - step)} className="size-9 rounded-full bg-sunk text-lg active:bg-line" aria-label={`decrease ${label}`}>−</button>
        <span className="num w-16 text-center text-[16px] font-semibold">{value}{unit}</span>
        <button onClick={() => set(value + step)} className="size-9 rounded-full bg-sunk text-lg active:bg-line" aria-label={`increase ${label}`}>+</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export default function SettingsSheet({ open, onClose, onSaved }: { open: boolean; onClose: () => void; onSaved: () => void }) {
  const [s, setS] = useState<Settings | null>(null)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  useEffect(() => { if (open) api<Settings>('/api/settings').then(setS).catch(() => setS(null)) }, [open])
  const save = async () => {
    if (!s) return
    setSaving(true)
    try {
      await api('/api/settings', { method: 'PUT', body: JSON.stringify(s) })
      onSaved()
      onClose()
    } finally {
      setSaving(false)
    }
  }
  const toggle = (c: string) => s && setS({ ...s, alert_categories: s.alert_categories.includes(c) ? s.alert_categories.filter((x) => x !== c) : [...s.alert_categories, c] })

  return (
    <Sheet open={open} onClose={onClose} title="What counts as unusual">
      {!s ? <div className="py-10 text-center text-ink3">Loading…</div> : (
        <>
          <Stepper label="Price move" hint="Alert when a holding closes beyond ±" value={s.price_move_pct} step={0.5} min={0.5} max={20} unit="%" onChange={(v) => setS({ ...s, price_move_pct: v })} />
          <Stepper label="Volume spike" hint="× the trailing average volume" value={s.volume_multiple} step={0.25} min={1.25} max={10} unit="×" onChange={(v) => setS({ ...s, volume_multiple: v })} />
          <Stepper label="Volume lookback" hint="Days in the trailing average" value={s.volume_lookback_days} step={5} min={5} max={60} unit="d" onChange={(v) => setS({ ...s, volume_lookback_days: v })} />
          <Stepper label="Filing score" hint="Minimum materiality to alert (0–100)" value={s.min_alert_materiality} step={5} min={30} max={95} unit="" onChange={(v) => setS({ ...s, min_alert_materiality: v })} />
          <div className="border-t border-line py-3">
            <div className="text-[14px] font-medium">Alert me about</div>
            <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {ALL.map((c) => <Chip key={c} active={s.alert_categories.includes(c)} onClick={() => toggle(c)}>{cat(c)}</Chip>)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={save} disabled={saving} className="mt-2 h-12 w-full rounded-xl bg-ink text-[15px] font-semibold text-bg disabled:opacity-60">
            {saving ? 'Saving…' : 'Save — applies to past days too'}
          </button>
          <p className="mt-2 pb-2 text-center text-[11.5px] text-ink3">Alerts are recomputed from stored prices and filings on every view.</p>
        </>
      )}
    </Sheet>
  )
}

import { useState } from 'react'

const W = 340

/** Bars (₹ Cr) with an optional line (%) on a second scale. */
export function BarLine({ labels, bars, line, barLabel, lineLabel, h = 150 }: {
  labels: string[]; bars: (number | null)[]; line?: (number | null)[]; barLabel: string; lineLabel?: string; h?: number
}) {
  const [hover, setHover] = useState<number | null>(null)
  const n = labels.length
  const top = 14, bottom = 20, plot = h - top - bottom
  const bmax = Math.max(1, ...bars.map((b) => b ?? 0))
  const lv = (line ?? []).filter((v): v is number => v != null)
  const lmin = Math.min(0, ...lv), lmax = Math.max(1, ...lv)
  const bw = (W / n) * 0.62
  const x = (i: number) => (W / n) * (i + 0.5)
  const by = (v: number) => top + plot - (Math.max(0, v) / bmax) * plot
  const ly = (v: number) => top + plot - ((v - lmin) / (lmax - lmin || 1)) * plot * 0.9
  const path = line?.map((v, i) => (v == null ? '' : `${i && line[i - 1] != null ? 'L' : 'M'}${x(i)},${ly(v)}`)).join('')
  const i = hover ?? n - 1
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="num mb-1 flex items-baseline gap-3 text-[12px]">
        <span className="font-semibold">{labels[i]}</span>
        <span className="text-ink2"><span className="mr-1 inline-block size-2 rounded-sm bg-ink3/50" />{barLabel} {bars[i]?.toLocaleString('en-IN') ?? '—'} Cr</span>
        {line && <span className="text-accent"><span className="mr-1 inline-block h-0.5 w-3 bg-accent align-middle" />{lineLabel} {line[i] ?? '—'}%</span>}
      </div>
      <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${W} ${h}`} className="w-full touch-none" onPointerLeave={() => setHover(null)}
        onPointerMove={(e) => {
          const r = (e.currentTarget as SVGElement).getBoundingClientRect()
          setHover(Math.max(0, Math.min(n - 1, Math.floor(((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width) * n))))
        }}>
        <line x1={0} x2={W} y1={top + plot} y2={top + plot} stroke="var(--line)" />
        {bars.map((v, k) => v != null && (
          <rect key={k} x={x(k) - bw / 2} y={by(v)} width={bw} height={top + plot - by(v)} rx={2}
            fill={k === i ? 'var(--ink2)' : 'var(--ink3)'} opacity={k === i ? 0.9 : 0.45} />
        ))}
        {path && <path d={path} fill="none" stroke="var(--accent)" strokeWidth={2} strokeLinejoin="round" />}
        {line?.map((v, k) => v != null && <circle key={k} cx={x(k)} cy={ly(v)} r={k === i ? 3.5 : 2} fill="var(--accent)" />)}
        {labels.map((l, k) => (n <= 8 || k % 2 === (n - 1) % 2) && (
          <text key={k} x={x(k)} y={h - 5} textAnchor="middle" fontSize={10} fill="var(--ink3)">{l}</text>
        ))}
      </svg>
    </div>
  )
}

/** Two series side by side per period (e.g. operating cash flow vs profit). */
export function PairBars({ labels, a, b, aLabel, bLabel, h = 140 }: {
  labels: string[]; a: (number | null)[]; b: (number | null)[]; aLabel: string; bLabel: string; h?: number
}) {
  const n = labels.length
  const top = 8, bottom = 20, plot = h - top - bottom
  const vals = [...a, ...b].filter((v): v is number => v != null)
  const max = Math.max(1, ...vals), min = Math.min(0, ...vals)
  const y = (v: number) => top + ((max - v) / (max - min)) * plot
  const slot = W / n, bw = slot * 0.32
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-1 flex gap-3 text-[12px] text-ink2">
        <span><span className="mr-1 inline-block size-2 rounded-sm bg-accent" />{aLabel}</span>
        <span><span className="mr-1 inline-block size-2 rounded-sm bg-ink3/60" />{bLabel}</span>
      </div>
      <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${W} ${h}`} className="w-full">
        <line x1={0} x2={W} y1={y(0)} y2={y(0)} stroke="var(--line)" />
        {labels.map((l, k) => {
          const cx = slot * (k + 0.5)
          return (
            <g key={l}>
              {a[k] != null && <rect x={cx - bw - 1} y={Math.min(y(a[k]!), y(0))} width={bw} height={Math.abs(y(a[k]!) - y(0))} rx={2} fill="var(--accent)" />}
              {b[k] != null && <rect x={cx + 1} y={Math.min(y(b[k]!), y(0))} width={bw} height={Math.abs(y(b[k]!) - y(0))} rx={2} fill="var(--ink3)" opacity={0.6} />}
              <text x={cx} y={h - 5} textAnchor="middle" fontSize={10} fill="var(--ink3)">{l}</text>
            </g>
          )
        })}
      </svg>
    </div>
  )
}

/** Close-price line with filing markers coloured by tier. */
export function PriceChart({ prices, marks, h = 170 }: {
  prices: { d: string; c: number }[]; marks: { d: string; tier: string; title: string }[]; h?: number
}) {
  const [hover, setHover] = useState<number | null>(null)
  if (prices.length < 2) return null
  const top = 10, bottom = 18, plot = h - top - bottom
  const cs = prices.map((p) => p.c)
  const min = Math.min(...cs), max = Math.max(...cs)
  const x = (i: number) => (i / (prices.length - 1)) * W
  const y = (v: number) => top + ((max - v) / (max - min || 1)) * plot
  const d = prices.map((p, i) => `${i ? 'L' : 'M'}${x(i).toFixed(1)},${y(p.c).toFixed(1)}`).join('')
  const idx = new Map(prices.map((p, i) => [p.d, i]))
  const i = hover ?? prices.length - 1
  const color: Record<string, string> = { critical: 'var(--crit)', notable: 'var(--note)', routine: 'var(--ink3)' }
  const nearest = (date: string) => {
    if (idx.has(date)) return idx.get(date)!
    const k = prices.findIndex((p) => p.d >= date)
    return k < 0 ? undefined : k
  }
  const markAt = marks.filter((m) => m.tier !== 'routine').map((m) => ({ ...m, i: nearest(m.d) })).filter((m) => m.i !== undefined)
  const here = markAt.filter((m) => m.i === i)
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="num mb-1 flex items-baseline justify-between text-[12px]">
        <span className="text-ink2">{new Date(prices[i].d).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}</span>
        <span className="font-semibold">₹{prices[i].c.toLocaleString('en-IN')}</span>
      </div>
      <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${W} ${h}`} className="w-full touch-none" onPointerLeave={() => setHover(null)}
        onPointerMove={(e) => {
          const r = (e.currentTarget as SVGElement).getBoundingClientRect()
          setHover(Math.round(((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width) * (prices.length - 1)))
        }}>
        <path d={d} fill="none" stroke="var(--ink)" strokeWidth={1.6} strokeLinejoin="round" />
        {markAt.map((m, k) => <circle key={k} cx={x(m.i!)} cy={y(prices[m.i!].c)} r={4} fill={color[m.tier]} stroke="var(--card)" strokeWidth={1.5} />)}
        <line x1={x(i)} x2={x(i)} y1={top} y2={top + plot} stroke="var(--ink3)" strokeDasharray="2 3" />
      </svg>
      <div className="min-h-5 text-[12px] text-ink2">{here.map((m) => m.title).join(' · ')}</div>
    </div>
  )
}

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Card, Icon, SectionTitle } from '../components/ui'
import { api } from '../lib/api'
import { ago } from '../lib/format'
import { useApi } from '../lib/hooks'

const SUGGESTED = [
  { symbol: 'DIXON', why: 'Revenue +47%, profit −45% — what did management promise?' },
  { symbol: 'ASIANPAINT', why: 'Three flat years — is the recovery story landing?' },
  { symbol: 'PIDILITIND', why: 'Outside the portfolio: a clean compounder' },
  { symbol: 'KPITTECH', why: 'Outside the portfolio: guidance-heavy IT services' },
  { symbol: 'HDFCBANK', why: 'Bank format: NII, provisions, NPAs' },
]

export default function Research({ go }: { go: (r: string) => void }) {
  const [q, setQ] = useState('')
  const [hits, setHits] = useState<{ symbol: string; name: string }[]>([])
  const { data: recent } = useApi<{ symbol: string; name?: string; status: string; updated: string; verdicts?: Record<string, number> }[]>('/api/research')

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!q.trim()) { setHits([]); return }
    const t = setTimeout(() => api<{ symbol: string; name: string }[]>(`/api/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`).then(setHits).catch(() => setHits([])), 180)
    return () => clearTimeout(t)
  }, [q])

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-xl px-4 pt-[calc(env(safe-area-inset-top)+16px)]">
      <h1 className="font-serif text-[30px] leading-tight">Research</h1>
      <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink2">Any NSE company. Built only from its filings, statements and earnings-call transcripts — every claim links to its source.</p>

      <div className="relative mt-4">
        <Icon name="search" className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 size-5 -translate-y-1/2 text-ink3" />
        <input value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} placeholder="Company or NSE symbol"
          onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter' && (hits[0] || q.trim())) go(`memo/${(hits[0]?.symbol ?? q.trim()).toUpperCase()}`) }}
          className="h-12 w-full rounded-xl border border-line bg-card pl-10 pr-3 text-[16px] outline-none placeholder:text-ink3 focus:border-ink3"
          autoCapitalize="characters" autoCorrect="off" spellCheck={false} />
        {hits.length > 0 && (
          <Card className="absolute inset-x-0 top-14 z-20 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto shadow-lg">
            {hits.map((h) => (
              <button key={h.symbol} onClick={() => go(`memo/${h.symbol}`)} className="flex w-full items-baseline gap-3 border-t border-line px-4 py-2.5 text-left first:border-t-0 active:bg-sunk">
                <span className="w-24 shrink-0 text-[13px] font-bold">{h.symbol}</span>
                <span className="truncate text-[13px] text-ink2">{h.name}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </Card>
        )}
      </div>

      {!!recent?.length && (
        <>
          <SectionTitle>Your memos</SectionTitle>
          <Card>
            {recent.map((r) => (
              <button key={r.symbol} onClick={() => go(`memo/${r.symbol}`)} className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 border-t border-line px-4 py-3 text-left first:border-t-0 active:bg-sunk">
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="text-[14px] font-semibold">{r.symbol} <span className="font-normal text-ink3">· {r.name}</span></div>
                  {r.verdicts && (
                    <div className="num mt-0.5 text-[12px] text-ink2">
                      {r.verdicts.delivered ?? 0} delivered · {(r.verdicts.missed ?? 0) + (r.verdicts.goalposts_moved ?? 0) + (r.verdicts.dropped ?? 0)} missed, moved or dropped
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
                <span className="text-[12px] text-ink3">{r.status === 'running' ? 'building…' : ago(r.updated)}</span>
                <Icon name="chev" className="size-4 text-ink3" />
              </button>
            ))}
          </Card>
        </>
      )}

      <SectionTitle>Start here</SectionTitle>
      <Card>
        {SUGGESTED.map((s) => (
          <button key={s.symbol} onClick={() => go(`memo/${s.symbol}`)} className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 border-t border-line px-4 py-3 text-left first:border-t-0 active:bg-sunk">
            <div className="flex-1">
              <div className="text-[14px] font-semibold">{s.symbol}</div>
              <div className="text-[12.5px] text-ink2">{s.why}</div>
            </div>
            <Icon name="chev" className="size-4 text-ink3" />
          </button>
        ))}
      </Card>
      <div className="h-24" />
    </div>
  )
}

```

import { useState } from 'react'
import { BarLine, PairBars } from '../components/Charts'
import { Card, Chip, ExtLink, Icon, SectionTitle, Sheet, Skeleton } from '../components/ui'
import { api, type Fact, type LedgerRow, type Research, type ThesisPoint, type Verdict } from '../lib/api'
import { crore, pageLink, pct, tone } from '../lib/format'
import { useApi, useInterval } from '../lib/hooks'

const VERDICT: Record<Verdict, { label: string; cls: string; bar: string }> = {
  delivered: { label: 'Delivered', cls: 'bg-up/12 text-up', bar: 'var(--up)' },
  on_track: { label: 'On track', cls: 'bg-accent-soft text-accent', bar: 'var(--accent)' },
  at_risk: { label: 'At risk', cls: 'bg-note-soft text-note', bar: 'var(--note)' },
  goalposts_moved: { label: 'Goalposts moved', cls: 'bg-note-soft text-note', bar: '#d97706' },
  missed: { label: 'Missed', cls: 'bg-down/12 text-down', bar: 'var(--down)' },
  dropped: { label: 'Quietly dropped', cls: 'bg-crit-soft text-crit', bar: 'var(--crit)' },
  too_early: { label: 'Too early', cls: 'bg-sunk text-ink2', bar: 'var(--ink3)' },
  unverifiable: { label: 'Can’t verify', cls: 'bg-sunk text-ink3', bar: 'var(--line)' },
}

const STANCE: Record<string, { g: string; c: string; t: string }> = {
  set: { g: '●', c: 'text-ink', t: 'First guided' },
  reiterated: { g: '●', c: 'text-ink2', t: 'Reiterated' },
  raised: { g: '▲', c: 'text-up', t: 'Raised' },
  lowered: { g: '▼', c: 'text-down', t: 'Lowered' },
  softened: { g: '◐', c: 'text-note', t: 'Softened' },
  silent: { g: '○', c: 'text-ink3/50', t: 'Not mentioned' },
}

const shortCall = (c: string) => c.replace(' FY', "·")

function FactLine({ f }: { f: Fact }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3 border-t border-line py-2 text-[13px] first:border-t-0">
      <span className="text-ink2">{f.label} <span className="text-ink3">· {f.period}</span></span>
      <span className="num shrink-0 font-semibold">{f.unit === '₹ Cr' ? crore(f.value) : `${f.value}${f.unit === '%' ? '%' : f.unit === 'x' ? '×' : ` ${f.unit}`}`}</span>
    </div>
  )
}

function Quote({ text, call, page, url, speaker }: { text: string; call?: string; page?: number; url?: string; speaker?: string }) {
  return (
    <figure className="border-l-2 border-accent/60 pl-3">
      <blockquote className="font-serif text-[15.5px] leading-snug">“{text}”</blockquote>
      <figcaption className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-ink3">
        {[speaker, call && `${call} call`].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')}
        {url && <> · <ExtLink href={pageLink(url, page)}>transcript p.{page}</ExtLink></>}
      </figcaption>
    </figure>
  )
}

function LedgerSection({ r, open }: { r: Research; open: (row: LedgerRow) => void }) {
  const L = r.sections.ledger
  const g = r.sections.guidance
  if (!L) return null
  if (L.status !== 'ok') return <Card className="p-4 text-[13.5px] text-ink2">{L.note ?? 'Not enough verifiable guidance to audit.'}</Card>
  const counts = L.counts ?? ({} as Record<Verdict, number>)
  const total = Object.values(counts).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) || 1
  const dropped = g?.calls.reduce((a, c) => a + (c.dropped_unverified ?? 0), 0) ?? 0
  const downgraded = L.rows.filter((x) => x.downgraded_from).length
  return (
    <>
      {L.credibility && <p className="px-1 font-serif text-[19px] leading-snug">{L.credibility}</p>}
      <div className="mt-3 flex h-2.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-sunk">
        {(Object.keys(VERDICT) as Verdict[]).map((v) => counts[v] ? <div key={v} style={{ width: `${(counts[v] / total) * 100}%`, background: VERDICT[v].bar }} title={`${VERDICT[v].label}: ${counts[v]}`} /> : null)}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-x-3 gap-y-1 px-1 text-[12px] text-ink2">
        {(Object.keys(VERDICT) as Verdict[]).filter((v) => counts[v]).map((v) => (
          <span key={v} className="num"><span className="mr-1 inline-block size-2 rounded-full align-middle" style={{ background: VERDICT[v].bar }} />{VERDICT[v].label} {counts[v]}</span>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 space-y-2.5">
        {L.rows.map((row) => (
          <Card key={row.subject} className="p-3.5 active:bg-sunk" onClick={() => open(row)}>
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
              <div className="text-[14.5px] font-semibold leading-snug">{row.subject}</div>
              <span className={`shrink-0 rounded-md px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold ${VERDICT[row.verdict].cls}`}>{VERDICT[row.verdict].label}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink2"><span className="text-ink3">Promised:</span> {row.promise}</div>
            {row.delivered && <div className="mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink"><span className="text-ink3">Actual:</span> {row.delivered}</div>}
            <div className="mt-2.5 flex items-center gap-0.5">
              {row.timeline.map((t, i) => (
                <div key={t.call} className="flex flex-1 flex-col items-center" title={`${t.call}: ${STANCE[t.stance]?.t ?? t.stance}`}>
                  <div className="flex w-full items-center">
                    <span className={`h-px flex-1 ${i === 0 ? 'opacity-0' : 'bg-line'}`} />
                    <span className={`text-[13px] leading-none ${STANCE[t.stance]?.c ?? 'text-ink3'}`}>{STANCE[t.stance]?.g ?? '●'}</span>
                    <span className={`h-px flex-1 ${i === row.timeline.length - 1 ? 'opacity-0' : 'bg-line'}`} />
                  </div>
                  <span className="mt-1 text-[10px] text-ink3">{shortCall(t.call)}</span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
            {!row.grounded && <div className="mt-2 text-[11.5px] text-note">⚠ Wording contains a figure FINN couldn’t trace to the sources — check the evidence.</div>}
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>
      <p className="mt-3 px-1 text-[11.5px] leading-relaxed text-ink3">
        ● guided · ▲ raised · ▼ lowered · ◐ softened · ○ not mentioned on that call. Verdicts compare management’s verbatim guidance with reported figures only.
        {dropped > 0 && ` ${dropped} extracted statement${dropped > 1 ? 's were' : ' was'} discarded because the wording couldn’t be found verbatim in the transcript or wasn’t forward-looking.`}
        {downgraded > 0 && ` ${downgraded} verdict${downgraded > 1 ? 's were' : ' was'} downgraded to “can’t verify” for lack of evidence.`}
      </p>
    </>
  )
}

function ThesisCol({ title, points, cls, onRef }: { title: string; points: ThesisPoint[]; cls: string; onRef: (p: ThesisPoint) => void }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <div className={`mb-2 text-[12px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider ${cls}`}>{title}</div>
      <ol className="space-y-2.5">
        {points.map((p, i) => (
          <li key={i} className="flex gap-2.5 text-[14px] leading-snug">
            <span className={`num mt-px text-[12px] font-bold ${cls}`}>{i + 1}</span>
            <div>
              {p.point}
              <button onClick={() => onRef(p)} className="ml-1.5 inline-flex rounded bg-sunk px-1.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-ink2 active:bg-line">
                {p.refs.length} source{p.refs.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}
              </button>
              {!p.grounded && <span className="ml-1 text-[11px] text-note">⚠ unverified</span>}
            </div>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
    </div>
  )
}

function Evidence({ items }: { items: ThesisPoint['evidence'] }) {
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {items.map((e, i) => {
        const x = e as Record<string, never>
        if (e.type === 'guidance') return <Quote key={i} text={x.statement} call={x.call} page={x.page} url={x.url} speaker={x.speaker} />
        if (e.type === 'analyst') return <div key={i}><div className="mb-1 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-ink3">Analyst question · {x.topic}</div><Quote text={x.quote} call={x.call} page={x.page} url={x.url} /></div>
        if (e.type === 'signal') return <div key={i} className="rounded-lg bg-sunk p-2.5 text-[13px]">{x.text}</div>
        if (e.type === 'ledger') return <div key={i} className="rounded-lg bg-sunk p-2.5 text-[13px]">Promise Ledger: <b>{x.subject}</b> — {VERDICT[x.verdict as Verdict]?.label}</div>
        if (e.type === 'fact') return <FactLine key={i} f={e as unknown as Fact} />
        return null
      })}
    </div>
  )
}

const NAV = [['ledger', 'Promise Ledger'], ['thesis', 'Bull / Bear'], ['snapshot', 'Snapshot'], ['trajectory', 'Trajectory'], ['balance', 'Balance sheet'], ['cash', 'Cash'], ['sources', 'Sources']]

export default function Memo({ symbol, go }: { symbol: string; go: (r: string) => void }) {

  return (

import { PriceChart } from '../components/Charts'
import { ResultsTable } from '../components/Story'
import { Card, ExtLink, Icon, SectionTitle, Skeleton, Spark, tierColor } from '../components/ui'
import type { CompanyView, Holding } from '../lib/api'
import { ago, cat, pct, rupee, tone } from '../lib/format'
import { useApi } from '../lib/hooks'

export function Portfolio({ go }: { go: (r: string) => void }) {
  const { data } = useApi<Holding[]>('/api/portfolio')
  const day = data?.filter((h) => h.change_pct != null).reduce((a, h) => a + (h.change_pct! * h.weight) / 100, 0)
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-xl px-4 pt-[calc(env(safe-area-inset-top)+16px)]">
      <h1 className="font-serif text-[30px] leading-tight">Portfolio</h1>
      {day != null && (
        <p className="num mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink2">
          Weighted day move <b className={tone(day)}>{pct(day, 2)}</b> · {data?.length} holdings · last close {data?.[0]?.date}
        </p>
      )}
      <SectionTitle>Holdings by weight</SectionTitle>
      {!data ? <Skeleton className="h-96" /> : (
        <Card>
          {data.map((h) => (
            <button key={h.symbol} onClick={() => go(`company/${h.symbol}`)} className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 border-t border-line px-4 py-2.5 text-left first:border-t-0 active:bg-sunk">
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <span className="text-[14px] font-bold">{h.symbol}</span>
                  {h.material_7d > 0 && <span className="num rounded bg-crit-soft px-1 text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-crit">{h.material_7d}</span>}
                </div>
                <div className="truncate text-[12px] text-ink3">{h.sector} · {h.weight}%</div>
              </div>
              <Spark data={h.spark} w={60} h={24} />
              <div className="num w-[78px] text-right">
                <div className="text-[13.5px] font-semibold">{rupee(h.close)}</div>
                <div className={`text-[12px] ${tone(h.change_pct)}`}>{pct(h.change_pct)}</div>
              </div>
            </button>
          ))}
        </Card>
      )}
      <p className="mt-2 px-1 text-[11.5px] text-ink3">Red badge = material filings in the last 7 days. Mock weights; end-of-day prices.</p>
      <div className="h-24" />
    </div>
  )
}

export function Company({ symbol, go }: { symbol: string; go: (r: string) => void }) {
  const { data } = useApi<CompanyView>(`/api/company/${symbol}`)
  const last = data?.prices.at(-1), first = data?.prices[0]
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-xl px-4 pt-[calc(env(safe-area-inset-top)+8px)]">
      <button onClick={() => history.length > 1 ? history.back() : go('portfolio')} className="-ml-2 rounded-full p-2 text-ink2 active:bg-sunk" aria-label="Back"><Icon name="back" /></button>
      <h1 className="font-serif text-[28px] leading-tight">{data?.name ?? symbol}</h1>
      {last && first && (
        <div className="num mt-0.5 text-[13px] text-ink2">{rupee(last.c)} · <span className={tone(last.c - first.c)}>{pct((last.c / first.c - 1) * 100)}</span> over {data!.prices.length} sessions{data?.weight ? ` · ${data.weight}% of portfolio` : ''}</div>
      )}
      <button onClick={() => go(`memo/${symbol}`)} className="mt-3 flex h-11 w-full items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-xl bg-ink text-[14px] font-semibold text-bg">
        Open research memo<Icon name="chev" className="size-4" />
      </button>

      {!data ? <Skeleton className="mt-4 h-60" /> : (
        <>
          <SectionTitle>Price & filings</SectionTitle>
          <Card className="p-4">
            <PriceChart prices={data.prices} marks={data.announcements.map((a) => ({ d: a.ts.slice(0, 10), tier: a.tier, title: a.title }))} />
            <div className="mt-1 flex gap-3 text-[11px] text-ink3">
              <span><span className="mr-1 inline-block size-2 rounded-full bg-crit" />critical filing</span>
              <span><span className="mr-1 inline-block size-2 rounded-full bg-note" />notable</span>
            </div>
          </Card>
          {data.results && (
            <>
              <SectionTitle right={<span className="text-[11.5px] text-ink3">vs {data.results.prev_label} · {data.results.yoy_label}</span>}>Latest results</SectionTitle>
              <Card className="p-4">
                <ResultsTable card={data.results} />
                {data.results.links[0] && <div className="mt-2 text-[12px]"><ExtLink href={data.results.links[0]}>{data.results.source} source</ExtLink></div>}
              </Card>
            </>
          )}
          <SectionTitle>Filings</SectionTitle>
          <Card>
            {data.announcements.map((a) => (
              <div key={a.id} className="flex items-start gap-2.5 border-t border-line px-4 py-2.5 first:border-t-0">
                <span className={`mt-1.5 size-2 shrink-0 rounded-full ${tierColor[a.tier]}`} />
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="text-[13.5px] leading-snug">{a.title}</div>
                  <div className="text-[11.5px] text-ink3">{cat(a.category)} · {ago(a.ts)} · score {a.materiality}</div>
                </div>
                {a.pdf_url && <a href={a.pdf_url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className="mt-0.5 text-ink3"><Icon name="doc" className="size-4" /></a>}
              </div>
            ))}
          </Card>
        </>
      )}
      <div className="h-24" />
    </div>
  )
}

import { useState } from 'react'
import { TabBar } from './components/ui'
import type { Alert } from './lib/api'
import { useApi, useHashRoute } from './lib/hooks'
import Alerts from './screens/Alerts'
import Brief from './screens/Brief'
import Memo from './screens/Memo'
import { Company, Portfolio } from './screens/Portfolio'
import Research from './screens/Research'
import SettingsSheet from './screens/SettingsSheet'

export default function App() {
  const [route, go] = useHashRoute()
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState(false)
  const [version, setVersion] = useState(0)
  const { data: alerts } = useApi<{ alerts: Alert[] }>('/api/alerts?days=1', [version])
  const [screen, arg] = route
  const open = () => setSettings(true)

  return (
    <div className="min-h-dvh">
      <main key={`${screen}/${arg ?? ''}/${version}`} className="fade">
        {screen === 'brief' && <Brief go={go} openSettings={open} />}
        {screen === 'alerts' && <Alerts go={go} openSettings={open} />}
        {screen === 'research' && <Research go={go} />}
        {screen === 'memo' && arg && <Memo symbol={arg.toUpperCase()} go={go} />}
        {screen === 'portfolio' && <Portfolio go={go} />}
        {screen === 'company' && arg && <Company symbol={arg.toUpperCase()} go={go} />}
      </main>
      <TabBar route={screen} go={go} alertCount={alerts?.alerts.length ?? 0} />
      <SettingsSheet open={settings} onClose={() => setSettings(false)} onSaved={() => setVersion((v) => v + 1)} />
    </div>
  )
}

```

// Minimal offline shell: cache the app on install, serve API from network with a cached fallback.
const SHELL = 'finn-shell-v1'
const DATA = 'finn-data-v1'

self.addEventListener('install', (e) => {
  e.waitUntil(caches.open(SHELL).then((c) => c.addAll(['/', '/manifest.webmanifest', '/icon.svg'])))
  self.skipWaiting()
})

self.addEventListener('activate', (e) => {
  e.waitUntil(caches.keys().then((keys) => Promise.all(keys.filter((k) => ![SHELL, DATA].includes(k)).map((k) => caches.delete(k)))))
  self.clients.claim()
})

self.addEventListener('fetch', (e) => {
  const url = new URL(e.request.url)
  if (e.request.method !== 'GET') return
  if (url.pathname.startsWith('/api/')) {
    e.respondWith(
      fetch(e.request)
        .then((r) => { const copy = r.clone(); caches.open(DATA).then((c) => c.put(e.request, copy)); return r })
        .catch(() => caches.match(e.request)),
    )
    return
  }
  if (url.origin === location.origin) {
    e.respondWith(caches.match(e.request).then((hit) => hit || fetch(e.request).then((r) => {
      const copy = r.clone(); caches.open(SHELL).then((c) => c.put(e.request, copy)); return r
    })))
  }
})

self.addEventListener('push', (e) => {
  const d = e.data ? e.data.json() : { title: 'FINN', body: 'Something in your portfolio needs a look.' }
  e.waitUntil(self.registration.showNotification(d.title, { body: d.body, icon: '/icon-192.png', badge: '/icon-192.png', data: d.url || '/#/alerts' }))
})

self.addEventListener('notificationclick', (e) => {
  e.notification.close()
  e.waitUntil(self.clients.openWindow(e.notification.data || '/#/alerts'))
})

```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T12:55:10Z

**→ Bash**

_Set up Playwright with system Chrome and capture screens_
```
cd ~/finn/backend && uv pip install --python .venv/Scripts/python.exe playwright 2>&1 | tail -1 && mkdir -p ../scripts && cat > ../scripts/screens.py <<'EOF'
"""Capture phone-sized screenshots of the running app (uses the locally installed Chrome)."""
import sys
from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright

BASE = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "http://127.0.0.1:5173"
OUT = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "docs/screens"
PAGES = sys.argv[3].split(",") if len(sys.argv) > 3 else ["brief", "alerts", "research", "portfolio", "company/DIXON", "memo/DIXON"]

with sync_playwright() as p:
    b = p.chromium.launch(channel="chrome")
    ctx = b.new_context(viewport={"width": 390, "height": 844}, device_scale_factor=2, is_mobile=True, has_touch=True,
                        color_scheme=sys.argv[4] if len(sys.argv) > 4 else "light")
    for route in PAGES:
        pg = ctx.new_page()
        pg.goto(f"{BASE}/#/{route}")
        pg.wait_for_timeout(4500)
        name = route.replace("/", "_")
        pg.screenshot(path=f"{OUT}/{name}.png", full_page=True)
        print("saved", name, pg.evaluate("document.documentElement.scrollWidth"))
        pg.close()
    b.close()
EOF
cd .. && backend/.venv/Scripts/python.exe scripts/screens.py http://127.0.0.1:5173 docs/screens brief,alerts,portfolio
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T12:55:59Z

**→ Write**

`~\finn\backend\finn\push\present.py`

```
"""How a filing is named wherever it appears (feed, alerts, company page)."""
from .enrich import clean_text


def title(a) -> str:
    e = a.enrichment or {}
    if a.category == "results" and e.get("status") == "ok":
        return f"{e['card']['quarter']['label']} results · {e['card']['headline']}"
    if a.category == "results" and e.get("status") == "pending":
        return f"{e['expected_quarter']} results — numbers awaiting exchange XBRL"
    if a.category == "management_change" and e.get("changes"):
        c = e["changes"][0]
        return f"{c.get('person')} — {c.get('change')} as {c.get('role')}"
    if a.summary:
        return a.summary
    t = a.subject if a.subject and len(clean_text(a.text)) < 80 else (clean_text(a.text) or a.exch_category or "")
    return t if len(t) <= 140 else t[:137].rsplit(" ", 1)[0] + "…"


def short_title(a) -> str:
    """Feed headline: results cards carry their numbers in the body, so keep the headline short."""
    t = title(a)
    return t.split(" · ")[0] if a.category == "results" else t

```

p = "finn/api.py"

            out.append({**base, "kind": "price", "threshold": pct_th,
                        "title": f"{symbol} {ret * 100:+.1f}% to ₹{cur.close:,.2f}"
                                 + (f" · {abs(z):.1f}σ move" if z is not None else "")})
        if mult and mult >= vol_th:
            out.append({**base, "kind": "volume", "threshold": vol_th,
                        "title": f"{symbol} volume {mult:.1f}× its {look}-day average"
                                 + f" · price {ret * 100:+.1f}%"})''', '''        price_hit = abs(ret) * 100 >= pct_th
        vol_hit = bool(mult and mult >= vol_th)
        if not (price_hit or vol_hit):
            continue
        # One line per stock per day: a big move on heavy volume is one event, not two.
        parts = [f"{ret * 100:+.1f}% to ₹{cur.close:,.2f}" if price_hit else f"volume {mult:.1f}× its {look}-day average"]
        if price_hit and vol_hit:
            parts.append(f"on {mult:.1f}× volume")
        elif vol_hit:
            parts.append(f"price {ret * 100:+.1f}%")
        if price_hit and z is not None:
            parts.append(f"{abs(z):.1f}σ")
        out.append({**base, "kind": "price" if price_hit else "volume", "both": price_hit and vol_hit,
                    "threshold": pct_th if price_hit else vol_th, "vol_threshold": vol_th,
                    "title": f"{symbol} " + " · ".join(parts)})''')
s = s.replace('''                m["linked"] = {"id": a.id, "category": a.category, "ts": a.ts.isoformat(), "text": a.summary or a.text}''',
              '''                m["linked"] = {"id": a.id, "category": a.category, "ts": a.ts.isoformat(), "text": filing_title(a)}''')
s = s.replace('''                           "text": a.summary or a.subject or a.text})''', '''                           "text": filing_title(a)})''')

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T12:59:46Z

`~\finn\scripts\screens.py`

```
"""Capture phone-sized screenshots of the running app using the locally installed Chrome.

python scripts/screens.py [base_url] [out_dir] [route[@scrollY],...] [light|dark] [--full]
"""
import sys

from playwright.sync_api import sync_playwright

args = [a for a in sys.argv[1:] if not a.startswith("--")]
BASE = args[0] if len(args) > 0 else "http://127.0.0.1:5173"
OUT = args[1] if len(args) > 1 else "docs/screens"
ROUTES = args[2].split(",") if len(args) > 2 else ["brief", "alerts", "research", "portfolio", "company/DIXON", "memo/DIXON"]
SCHEME = args[3] if len(args) > 3 else "light"
FULL = "--full" in sys.argv

with sync_playwright() as p:
    b = p.chromium.launch(channel="chrome")
    ctx = b.new_context(viewport={"width": 390, "height": 844}, device_scale_factor=2, is_mobile=True, has_touch=True,
                        color_scheme=SCHEME)
    ctx.add_init_script("localStorage.setItem('finn.window', '336')")
    pg = ctx.new_page()
    for spec in ROUTES:
        route, _, y = spec.partition("@")
        pg.goto(f"{BASE}/#/{route}")
        pg.wait_for_timeout(4000)
        if y:
            pg.evaluate(f"window.scrollTo(0, {int(y)})")
            pg.wait_for_timeout(400)
        name = f"{route.replace('/', '_')}{'_' + y if y else ''}{'_dark' if SCHEME == 'dark' else ''}"
        pg.screenshot(path=f"{OUT}/{name}.png", full_page=FULL)
        print("saved", name)
    b.close()

```

`~\finn\.github\workflows\ingest.yml`

```
name: ingest

# Push pipeline on a schedule: EOD prices + new filings → classify → score → enrich.
# Writes straight to the shared Postgres, so the API host never has to scrape NSE itself.
on:
  schedule:
    - cron: "*/30 3-15 * * 1-5"   # every 30 min, 08:30–21:00 IST, Mon–Fri
    - cron: "0 13 * * 1-5"        # 18:30 IST — after the EOD price print
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      days:
        description: "Days of filings to (re)fetch"
        default: "2"

concurrency: { group: ingest, cancel-in-progress: false }

jobs:
  ingest:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 25
    defaults: { run: { working-directory: backend } }
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with: { python-version: "3.12", cache: pip, cache-dependency-path: backend/requirements.txt }
      - run: pip install -r requirements.txt
      - name: Ingest
        env:
          DATABASE_URL: ${{ secrets.DATABASE_URL }}
          GEMINI_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.GEMINI_API_KEY }}
          GROQ_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.GROQ_API_KEY }}
        run: python -m finn.cli all ${{ github.event.inputs.days || '2' }}
      - name: Keep the API warm
        if: always()
        run: curl -fsS --max-time 90 "${{ secrets.API_URL }}/api/health" > /dev/null || true

```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T13:01:25Z

**→ Write**

`~\finn\scripts\export_history.py`

```
"""Export the Claude Code session(s) that built FINN into docs/ai-history/.

Writes the raw JSONL transcript (complete, machine-readable) and a readable Markdown rendering
(user prompts, assistant messages and tool calls; long tool outputs truncated).
"""
import json
import pathlib
import shutil
import sys

SRC = pathlib.Path.home() / ".claude" / "projects" / "C--Users-<user>"
OUT = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "docs" / "ai-history"
SESSIONS = sys.argv[1:] or ["c363bd54-6dbf-4695-9f84-97a9ce39aca0"]


def text_of(content) -> list[str]:
    if isinstance(content, str):
        return [content]
    out = []
    for c in content or []:
        t = c.get("type")
        if t == "text":
            out.append(c["text"])
        elif t == "tool_use":
            inp = json.dumps(c.get("input"), ensure_ascii=False)
            out.append(f"**→ {c.get('name')}** `{inp[:600]}{'…' if len(inp) > 600 else ''}`")
        elif t == "tool_result":
            body = c.get("content")
            body = body if isinstance(body, str) else " ".join(x.get("text", "") for x in body or [] if isinstance(x, dict))
            out.append(f"<details><summary>result</summary>\n\n```\n{body[:1500]}{'…' if len(body) > 1500 else ''}\n```\n</details>")
    return out


OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
for sid in SESSIONS:
    src = SRC / f"{sid}.jsonl"
    shutil.copy(src, OUT / f"{sid}.jsonl")
    lines = [f"# Claude Code build session `{sid}`\n", "Exported from the local Claude Code transcript. Raw JSONL alongside.\n"]
    for raw in src.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        try:
            ev = json.loads(raw)
        except ValueError:
            continue
        msg = ev.get("message") or {}
        role = msg.get("role") or ev.get("type")
        if role not in ("user", "assistant"):
            continue
        parts = text_of(msg.get("content"))
        if not parts:
            continue
        who = "🧑 User" if role == "user" and not any(p.startswith("<details>") for p in parts) else ("🤖 Claude" if role == "assistant" else "⚙️ Tool")
        lines.append(f"\n### {who}\n")
        lines.extend(p if len(p) < 8000 else p[:8000] + "\n…[truncated]" for p in parts)
    (OUT / f"{sid}.md").write_text("\n\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")
    print("exported", sid, (OUT / f"{sid}.md").stat().st_size // 1024, "KB")

```

+ import re

+ # Never publish credentials or personal identifiers that ended up in the transcript.
+ REDACT = [
+     (re.compile(r"AIza[0-9A-Za-z_\-]{30,}"), "[REDACTED_GOOGLE_KEY]"),
+     (re.compile(r"gsk_[0-9A-Za-z]{20,}"), "[REDACTED_GROQ_KEY]"),

+ ]
+
+
+ def redact(s: str) -> str:
+     for rx, rep in REDACT:
+         s = rx.sub(rep, s)
+     return s
