### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T13:02:09Z

**→ Bash**

_Evaluate results engine across the portfolio_
```
cd ~/finn/backend && cat > ../scripts/eval_results.py <<'EOF'
"""Evaluate the results engine on the portfolio: source used, arithmetic check, two-source agreement."""
import json
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, "backend")
from finn.db import init_db  # noqa: E402
from finn.push.results import results_card  # noqa: E402

init_db()
spec = json.load(open("backend/portfolio.json"))
rows, agree, compared, arith = [], 0, 0, 0
for h in spec["holdings"]:
    c = results_card(h["symbol"])
    if not c:
        rows.append((h["symbol"], "none", "", "", ""))
        continue
    xs = [x for x in c["checks"] if "matches" in x["name"]]
    ar = [x for x in c["checks"] if "PBT" in x["name"]]
    compared += bool(xs)
    agree += bool(xs) and all(x["ok"] for x in xs)
    arith += bool(ar) and ar[0]["ok"]
    rows.append((h["symbol"], c["quarter"]["label"], c["source"], c["kind"],
                 "agree" if xs and all(x["ok"] for x in xs) else ("differ: " + "; ".join(x["detail"] for x in xs if not x["ok"]) if xs else "single source")))
for r in rows:
    print(" | ".join(map(str, r)))
print(f"\n{len(rows)} companies · arithmetic check passed {arith} · two sources compared {compared}, agreed {agree}")
EOF
cd .. && PYTHONIOENCODING=utf-8 backend/.venv/Scripts/python.exe scripts/eval_results.py 2>&1 | grep -v "failed" | tail -24
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T13:02:49Z

**→ Write**

`~\finn\README.md`

```
# FINN — Financial Investment Agent

A mobile-first investor tool with two jobs:

- **Push — the Brief.** Every filing across a 19-stock portfolio is classified, scored for materiality and clustered into stories. You see the few that matter, each with the numbers already pulled out. Everything routine is folded away.
- **Pull — the Research memo.** For any NSE company, FINN builds a memo from primary sources only. It leads with the **Promise Ledger**: what management guided on its last five earnings calls, set against what the company actually reported.

> **Live:** _link added after deploy_ · open it on a phone and *Add to Home Screen* to install it.

<p>
<img src="docs/screens/brief.png" width="260" alt="Brief">
<img src="docs/screens/alerts.png" width="260" alt="Alerts">
<img src="docs/screens/company_DIXON.png" width="260" alt="Company">
</p>

## The point of view

An investor's day is triage, so FINN behaves like **an inbox you can clear**, not a dashboard:

- The Brief opens with one sentence on what needs you.
- Material stories are ranked, and every rank can be explained (tap the score).
- You swipe a story away once you've dealt with it.
- Routine paperwork is folded but stays one tap away.
- Every story links straight into research.

You set the alert thresholds. Moves are reported in both % and σ (standard deviations of the stock's own recent volatility), and each move is linked to whatever was filed that session.

## What makes it trustworthy

| Problem | What FINN does |
|---|---|
| LLMs invent numbers | **The model never produces a figure.** Results come from NSE XBRL (exact rupee values), and multi-year data from standardised statements, computed in code. Any number in model-written prose must exist in the evidence the model was given, otherwise it is flagged. |
| Quotes get paraphrased | Every guidance quote is matched **verbatim** against the transcript text, and the page number is recorded. Unmatched or non-forward-looking statements are discarded, and the count is shown. |
| Confident verdicts with no evidence | A delivered / missed / on-track verdict needs a reported figure or a later management statement. Otherwise it is downgraded to "can't verify", and the downgrade is shown. |
| One data source can be wrong or late | Results are cross-checked: XBRL vs screener and PBT − tax ≈ PAT. **19/19 holdings pass the arithmetic check; 18/19 agree across two sources.** The exception (HDFC Bank: screener uses a different PAT aggregation) is shown as an amber flag, not hidden. |
| "CFO resigned" says nothing | SEBI requires a brief profile of any newly appointed director or key manager *inside the filing*. FINN extracts it and links the page, so the bio comes from a primary source, not scraped LinkedIn. |
| Most filings are noise | 11,450 market-wide filings over two weeks: **90.7% are classified by rules with no LLM call.** When a filing's summary is generic, FINN reads its "Sub:" line and opening paragraph instead. Penalties are sized against annual revenue (a ₹1.07 lakh DoT fine for Bharti Airtel is folded automatically). |

## Architecture

```
NSE (announcements, XBRL results, bhavcopy) ─┐
Yahoo Finance (EOD prices) ──────────────────┼─► ingest (GitHub Actions cron) ─► Postgres ◄─ FastAPI ◄─ React PWA (Vercel)
screener.in (statements, transcript links) ──┘        classify → score → enrich               │
BSE-hosted PDFs (filings, transcripts) ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ research on demand
```

**Model routing.** Each task asks for a *tier*, never a specific model:

| Tier | Used for | Candidates (first available wins, fallback on error or rate limit) |
|---|---|---|
| rules | most classification and scoring | — (free) |
| `fast` | ambiguous classification, one-liners, management-profile extraction | Groq Llama 3.1 8B → Gemini Flash-Lite → … → local Ollama |
| `long` | one pass per transcript (~16k tokens) → quote-verified guidance JSON | Gemini 2.5 Flash → Flash-Lite → … |
| `reason` | Promise Ledger verdicts and bull/bear, over *extracted JSON*, not raw documents | Gemini 2.5 Flash → Groq gpt-oss-120b → … |

Everything is cached by content hash: PDFs, page text, XBRL facts and LLM responses (empty answers are never cached). Each memo shows its own model calls, tokens and cache hits.

## Repository

```
backend/finn/
  sources/   nse.py (announcements, XBRL) · screener.py · prices.py · docs.py (PDF text, verbatim-quote finder, Sub: line)
  push/      classify.py · score.py (explainable materiality) · results.py (3-quarter XBRL engine) · enrich.py · alerts.py · ingest.py
  pull/      financials.py (fact table + signals) · transcripts.py · ledger.py (Promise Ledger) · thesis.py · research.py
  llm.py     provider-agnostic router · grounding.py (number checks) · api.py
frontend/src/  screens/ (Brief, Alerts, Research, Memo, Portfolio) · components/ (Story, Charts, ui)
spikes/      day-1 data-availability checks, kept as evidence
docs/        DECISIONS.md (data findings and decision log) · screens/ · ai-history/ (build transcripts)
```

## Run locally

```bash
cd backend && uv venv && uv pip install -r requirements.txt
cp .env.example .env            # add GEMINI_API_KEY and/or GROQ_API_KEY
python -m finn.cli all 14       # portfolio + prices + 14 days of filings
python -m finn.cli research DIXON
uvicorn finn.api:app --port 8000

cd ../frontend && npm install && npm run dev   # http://localhost:5173 (proxies /api)
```

Evaluation scripts: `scripts/eval_results.py` (results engine across the portfolio) and `backend/tests/nse_2w.json` (two weeks of market-wide filings used to measure rule coverage).

## Deliberately left out

- Intraday prices, macro data and news. The brief asks for EOD prices and, on the pull side, primary sources only.
- User accounts. There is one mock portfolio; per-viewer state (window, cleared stories) is stored on the device.
- RAG over 300-page annual reports. Transcripts fit in context whole, and structured extraction once per document is cheaper and more complete than retrieval.
- LinkedIn scraping. It is against LinkedIn's terms, and the mandated profile in the filing is a better source.

```

**→ Bash**

_Append findings to decision log and commit_
```
cat >> docs/DECISIONS.md <<'EOF'

## 2026-11-09 — Build-day findings

- **Rules first:** on 11,450 market-wide filings, exchange category + keyword rules classify 90.7% without an LLM. Keyword rules see only the filing's head (subject + opening paragraph); whole-document matching misfired ("customer acquisition" → M&A, "disruptive" → operational disruption).
- **Positive score adjustments do not stack** (one strong reason escalates); negative ones do. Stacking pushed a routine news clarification to 100.
- **Penalty sizing:** litigation filings that state a penalty are scored against annual revenue — Bharti Airtel's four DoT notices (₹1–54 lakh) fold automatically.
- **Results engine:** 19/19 portfolio companies pass PBT − tax ≈ PAT; 18/19 agree with a second source. HDFC Bank's ₹837 Cr PAT gap equals the gap between segment PBT and reported PBT — a screener aggregation difference; FINN shows the exchange figure and flags the disagreement.
- **Local model test (gemma3 4B):** plumbing works end to end, and it shows why the validators exist — the small model labelled historical results as guidance and marked future-dated capacity promises "delivered". Added: a forward-looking-language filter on guidance, an evidence requirement for verdicts, ref recovery from prose, and a fix to the number parser ("INR48873" was read as 8873).
- **Prompting:** for long documents the instructions are repeated after the document ("sandwich"); small models otherwise return `{}`.
- **Never cache an empty LLM answer** — a cached `{}` silently poisoned retries.
EOF
git add -A && git commit -q -m "Mobile PWA frontend (Brief, Alerts, Research memo with Promise Ledger, Portfolio/Company), deploy config, evidence guards, AI-history export

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>" && git log --oneline | head -1 && git status --short | head
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T13:04:09Z

I've built the whole app and tested it locally against live data. It isn't deployed yet: I need you to set up a few free accounts and keys, and the Promise Ledger hasn't been run with a proper cloud model.

**Data checks.** All three sources work from this machine: NSE filings and results data (XBRL), Yahoo Finance and NSE daily prices, and screener.in for statements and transcript links. All 19 tickers check out. Every finding is in `docs/DECISIONS.md`.

**Push side (your portfolio feed).**
- Rules classify 90.7% of 11,450 real filings with no AI call.
- Every filing gets a materiality score with stated reasons. Same-company filings are grouped into one story, and generic filings are titled from their "Sub:" line.
- Results cards show quarter-on-quarter and year-on-year numbers. 19/19 holdings pass the profit arithmetic check and 18/19 match a second source. The exception, HDFC Bank, is a known difference in how screener totals profit, and the app flags it in amber.
- Alerts use thresholds you set, show one line per stock per day, and name the filing that likely caused the move.

**Pull side (research on any NSE company).**
- The numbers sections (snapshot, trajectory, balance sheet, cash quality) are computed in code with no AI.
- The Promise Ledger and bull/bear case are built and wired end to end with checks: quotes must match the transcript word for word, numbers must trace to sources, and a verdict without evidence is downgraded to "can't verify".

**App.** A phone-first web app with Brief (swipe to clear), Alerts, Research and Portfolio screens. It's 86 KB, works in light and dark, and installs from the browser. Phone-size screenshots are in `docs/screens/`, and it's running now at http://localhost:5173.

**Decisions I made:**
- I used Vite instead of Next.js, because all data comes from the API and server rendering adds nothing.
- Hosting: Render for the API (free), Neon for the database (free, doesn't expire), and a GitHub Actions schedule that pulls new filings and prices.
- The AI build history is saved in `docs/ai-history/` with your email removed, and the exporter also strips API keys and database passwords.

**Not yet tested:**
- The Promise Ledger's actual quality. I only tested it on your local 4B model, which got many verdicts wrong; the checks caught some of these, and I tightened them after.
- Whether NSE answers requests from GitHub's or Render's servers. If it doesn't, the scheduled job can run from this laptop instead.

**What I need from you.** Please put keys in `~\finn\backend\.env`, not in chat, because chat ends up in the build history.

1. **Gemini key:** create one at https://aistudio.google.com/apikey (free) and paste it as `GEMINI_API_KEY`.
2. **Groq key:** create one at https://console.groq.com/keys (free) and paste it as `GROQ_API_KEY`.
3. **GitHub login:** type this in the prompt and follow the browser steps:
   `! "~\AppData\Local\Microsoft\WinGet\Packages\GitHub.cli_Microsoft.Winget.Source_8wekyb3d8bbwe\bin\gh.exe" auth login --web`
4. **Vercel login:** `! cd ~\finn\frontend && npx vercel login`
5. **Database:** at https://neon.tech, sign in with GitHub, create a project in region AWS ap[REDACTED_SECRET_FRAGMENT] (Singapore), and paste its connection string as `DATABASE_URL`.
6. **Render:** at https://render.com, sign in with GitHub, go to Account Settings → API Keys, and paste a key as `RENDER_API_KEY`.

Also tell me whether the GitHub repo can be public. Reviewers need to see it, and it would include the redacted build history.

When that's done, I'll run research on the demo companies with the real models, check the ledger's quality, deploy, test it on a phone-sized screen, and then write your covering note.

k = dotenv_values(".env")["GEMINI_API_KEY"].strip()
for m in ["gemini-3.7-flash", "gemini-3.5-flash", "gemini-flash-latest", "gemini-3.5-flash-lite", "gemini-flash-lite-latest", "gemini-2.5-flash", "gemini-2.5-flash-lite", "gemini-pro-latest", "gemini-3.8-flash"]:

+ def _env_secrets() -> list[str]:
+     """Literal values from backend/.env — key formats change, so pattern matching alone is not enough."""
+     env = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "backend" / ".env"
+     vals = []
+     if env.exists():
+         for line in env.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
+             if "=" in line and not line.lstrip().startswith("#"):
+                 v = line.split("=", 1)[1].split(" #")[0].strip().strip("'\"")
+                 if len(v) >= 12:
+                     vals.append(v)
+     return vals
+
+
+ SECRETS = _env_secrets()
+
+
+ def redact(s: str) -> str:
+     for v in SECRETS:
+         s = s.replace(v, "[REDACTED_SECRET]")
+     for rx, rep in REDACT:
+         s = rx.sub(rep, s)
+     return s
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T13:26:31Z

```diff
- Rules:
- - Quote numbers exactly as they appear in GUIDANCE or ACTUALS. Do not compute new numbers (no differences, no sums).
- - cite guidance_ids and fact_ids that support each row; fact_ids must be ids from ACTUALS.
- - "trajectory": one entry per call where this promise was discussed: stance = set|reiterated|raised|lowered|softened.
- - Prefer headline promises (revenue growth, margins, profit, capex, orders); at most 8 rows, most material first.
+ Rules:
+ - Quote numbers exactly as they appear in GUIDANCE or ACTUALS. Do not compute new numbers (no differences, no sums).
+ - cite guidance_ids and fact_ids that support each row; fact_ids must be ids from ACTUALS.
+ - "trajectory": one entry per call where this promise was discussed: stance = set|reiterated|raised|lowered|softened.
+ - Be strict: "delivered" only if the actual meets or beats the target (inside a range counts). Below the low end of a
+   range is "missed" — "close to" is not delivered. If year-to-date or latest-quarter actuals move AGAINST an open
+   promise (e.g. margins falling while expansion is promised), the verdict is "at_risk", not "too_early".
+ - Coverage: include every distinct company-level promise on revenue/growth, margins and profit, plus capex and the
+   largest segment promises — including open ones, so the investor sees what is still outstanding. 6–8 rows when the
+   guidance supports it, most material first. Margin promises must be compared with the reported margin facts.
```

- def compact(ledger: dict) -> str:
+ _UNIT = {"Cr": "₹ Cr", "%": "%"}
+
+
+ def _range_check(r: dict, guidance: dict, facts: dict) -> None:
+     """Compare the cited actual with the guided numeric range in code; override a lenient verdict.
+
+     Applied only when the latest guidance in the row has a numeric range and exactly one cited fact shares its unit,
+     so a segment target is never compared with a company-wide figure by accident.
+     """
+     nums = [guidance[g] for g in r["guidance_ids"] if guidance[g].get("low") is not None and guidance[g].get("unit") in _UNIT]
+     if not nums:
+         return
+     g = nums[-1]
+     same = [facts[f] for f in r["fact_ids"] if facts[f]["unit"] == _UNIT[g["unit"]]]
+     if len(same) != 1:
+         return
+     v, lo = same[0]["value"], float(g["low"])
+     hi = float(g["high"]) if g.get("high") is not None else lo
+     result = "below" if v < lo * 0.995 else "above" if v > hi * 1.005 else "within"
+     r["check"] = {"actual": v, "low": lo, "high": hi, "unit": same[0]["unit"], "result": result,
+                   "fact": same[0]["label"], "period": same[0]["period"], "guidance_id": g["id"]}
+     if result == "below" and r["verdict"] in ("delivered", "on_track"):
+         r["downgraded_from"] = r["verdict"]
+         r["verdict"] = "missed" if r["verdict"] == "delivered" else "at_risk"
+         rng = f"{lo:,.0f}–{hi:,.0f}" if hi != lo else f"{lo:,.0f}"
+         r["why"] = (f"{same[0]['label']} of {v:,} {same[0]['unit']} ({same[0]['period']}) is below the guided "
+                     f"{rng} {same[0]['unit']}. " + (r.get("why") or ""))
+
+
+ def compact(ledger: dict) -> str:
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T13:31:31Z

**→ Edit**

`~\finn\backend\finn\pull\transcripts.py`

```diff
-     return {"call": call["quarter"], "label": call["label"], "url": call["transcript"], "pages": doc["n"],
-             "guidance": kept, "dropped_unverified": dropped, "business": raw.get("business"), "analyst_focus": focus}
+     return {"call": call["quarter"], "label": call["label"], "url": call["transcript"], "pages": doc["n"],
+             "guidance": kept, "dropped_unverified": dropped, "business": _grounded_business(raw.get("business"), doc),
+             "analyst_focus": focus}
+
+
+ def _strip_unverified(text: str, source: str) -> str:
+     """Drop any clause whose figures do not appear in the transcript."""
+     if not text or not ungrounded(text, source):
+         return text
+     clauses = re.split(r"(?<=[;.])\s+|,\s+(?=[A-Z₹\d])", text)
+     return " ".join(c for c in clauses if not ungrounded(c, source)).strip(" ;,")
+
+
+ def _grounded_business(biz: dict | None, doc: dict) -> dict | None:
+     if not biz:
+         return biz
+     source = " ".join(doc["pages"])
+     biz["summary"] = _strip_unverified(biz.get("summary") or "", source)
+     for s in biz.get("segments") or []:
+         s["detail"] = _strip_unverified(s.get("detail") or "", source)
+     return biz
```

## 2026-11-09 — First real-model run (DIXON, Gemini free tier)

- **Quality review of the ledger, and the fixes it drove:**
  - The model called FY26 capex "delivered" at ₹1,058 Cr against a ₹1,100–1,200 Cr guide → strictness rule in the prompt **plus a deterministic range check** that overrides lenient verdicts (only for ended horizons, or rate targets mid-horizon — a first version wrongly downgraded a 3–4-year level target).

  - A Q2 PAT −45% on revenue +47% was not in the computed signals → added latest-quarter profit/revenue divergence and YoY margin signals.
  - Business-segment figures are now number-checked against the transcript (clauses with unverifiable figures are dropped).
- **Result:** 54/54 guidance quotes verbatim on the cited page (6 are elided "…" quotes, both halves present); 7/7 ledger rows and 6/6 thesis points pass number grounding; a rebuild from cache takes 4 s with 0 tokens.

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T13:43:20Z

Your Gemini key works and isn't shown anywhere. I've run DIXON end to end with Gemini, and after fixing several problems the output holds up. Every quote, number and citation checks out, apart from a few borderline points listed below.

**Key check.** It's set up correctly and Gemini accepts it. It uses Google's newer format, which my build-history scrubber wouldn't have recognised. The scrubber now removes the actual values in your `.env`, and I confirmed none of them appear in the exported history.

**Models on your free tier.** My planned models (`gemini-2.5-flash` and `-lite`) are no longer offered to new keys, and `pro` has no free quota. The newest flash models timed out on every call today. FINN now uses `gemini-3.5-flash` for reading transcripts and reasoning, and `gemini-3.5-flash-lite` for short tasks and as the last fallback. During the runs Gemini also returned "overloaded" and rate-limit errors; FINN retried and fell back to the lighter model without failing.

**What I checked on DIXON**

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| Promises pulled from 5 earnings calls (102 pages) | 54 kept; 18 discarded (not word-for-word or not forward-looking) |
| Quotes found word-for-word on the cited page | 54/54 (6 are shortened with "…" and both parts are on the page) |
| Ledger rows whose numbers trace to the sources | 7/7 |
| Bull/bear points citing valid sources with traceable numbers | 6/6 |
| Speed | First run about 3 minutes (most of it Gemini overload retries); a rebuild from cache takes 4 seconds and uses no tokens |

**The Promise Ledger for DIXON**
- **Missed:** FY26 capex was ₹1,058 Cr against the ₹1,100–1,200 Cr management guided.
- **At risk:** management promised operating-margin expansion by FY28, but margin has fallen to 2.7% from 3.8% a year ago.
- **Goalposts moved:** the FY27 mobile volume target was cut from 55–60M units to 33–34M units excluding Vivo.
- **On track:** FY27 revenue is up 34.9% year to date against 15–17% guided, and the ₹1 lakh crore 3–4 year target is on track.
- **Too early:** FY27 capex of ₹1,200 Cr.
- **Can't verify:** IT-hardware segment revenue, because company filings don't report it separately.

The bear case now leads with the real story: profit fell 44.9% while revenue grew 46.7%.

**Problems I found and fixed**
1. **Lenient verdict.** The model called the capex "delivered" because ₹1,058 Cr was "close to" ₹1,100 Cr. I added a stricter instruction and a code check that compares the actual number with the guided range. My first version of that check wrongly marked the 3–4 year revenue target "at risk"; that's also fixed.
2. **Missing margin promises.** The ledger skipped margin promises because they're due later, even though margins are falling. Open promises are now included, and one moving the wrong way is marked "at risk".
3. **Analyst claim stated as fact.** A ₹280 Cr cash burn raised in an analyst's question appeared as fact. Analyst points are now attributed.
4. **Biggest number missing.** The profit-vs-revenue split wasn't among the computed signals. I added it, along with year-on-year margin change.
5. **Unchecked segment figures.** Segment revenue figures in the business snapshot weren't checked. They are now checked against the transcript, and these passed.
6. **Robustness bugs.** These were: valid JSON rejected because of trailing text, a blank `.env` line overriding a default, 4-minute hangs on overloaded models, internal IDs and double negatives showing in the text, and output limits too low for the newer models.

Screenshots of the memo, including the evidence pop-up with quotes linked to transcript pages, are in `docs/screens/memo_*.png`. Everything is committed.

**Still borderline**
- The capex "missed" is a 4% shortfall, and FINN measures capex as operating cash flow minus free cash flow, which may not match management's definition. The pop-up shows both figures so a reader can judge.
- The ledger varies a little between runs: one run included a telecom row, the next swapped it for FY27 capex. The verdicts themselves stayed consistent.

**To deploy I still need from you:**
1. **Groq key** (strongly recommended): https://console.groq.com/keys, saved as `GROQ_API_KEY` in `backend/.env`. With Gemini overloaded today, a second provider is what keeps the live demo from stalling.
2. **GitHub login:** `! "~\AppData\Local\Microsoft\WinGet\Packages\GitHub.cli_Microsoft.Winget.Source_8wekyb3d8bbwe\bin\gh.exe" auth login --web`
3. **Vercel login:** `! cd ~\finn\frontend && npx vercel login`
4. **Neon database:** create a project in AWS ap[REDACTED_SECRET_FRAGMENT] and save its connection string as `DATABASE_URL` in `.env`.
5. **Render:** create an API key and save it as `RENDER_API_KEY` in `.env`.
6. **Repo visibility:** please confirm it can be public.

                for v in d.get("violations", []): print("   ", v.get("quotaId"), "limit", v.get("quotaValue"))
                if d.get("retryDelay"): print("    retry after", d["retryDelay"])
        except Exception: print(r.text[:500])
EOF

print(f"verbatim on cited page: {len(items)-len(bad)}/{len(items)}", bad)

EOF

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T13:51:51Z

**→ Edit**

`~\finn\backend\finn\pull\ledger.py`

```diff
- _UNIT = {"Cr": "₹ Cr", "%": "%"}
+ _UNIT = {"Cr": "₹ Cr", "%": "%"}
+
+ # Metrics that are often guided but easy to "judge" with a look-alike number. A row about one of these may only be
+ # judged with a fact that measures it; otherwise it is unverifiable. (pattern in promise → pattern a fact id must match)
+ _METRIC_GUARDS = [
+     (r"\bnim\b|net interest margin", r"\bnim\b|net interest margin", "net interest margin"),
+     (r"\bcasa\b", r"casa", "CASA ratio"),
+     (r"\blcr\b|liquidity coverage", r"lcr", "liquidity coverage ratio"),
+     (r"credit[- ]deposit|\bldr\b|loan[- ]to[- ]deposit", r"credit_deposit|ldr", "credit-deposit ratio"),
+     (r"\broa\b|return on (average )?assets?", r"calc\.roa", "return on assets"),
+     (r"balance[- ]sheet growth", r"calc\.asset_growth", "balance-sheet growth"),
+     (r"cost[- ]to[- ]income|cost[- ]to[- ]earnings", r"cost_income", "cost-to-income ratio"),
+     (r"\bgnpa\b|gross npa|asset quality", r"npa", "NPA ratio"),
+ ]
+
+
+ def _metric_guard(r: dict, facts: dict) -> None:
+     text = f"{r.get('subject')} {r.get('promise')}".lower()
+     for promise_rx, fact_rx, name in _METRIC_GUARDS:
+         if re.search(promise_rx, text):
+             if not any(re.search(fact_rx, f"{fid} {facts[fid]['label']}", re.I) for fid in r["fact_ids"]):
+                 if r["verdict"] not in ("unverifiable", "goalposts_moved", "dropped"):
+                     r["downgraded_from"] = r["verdict"]
+                     r["verdict"] = "unverifiable"
+                     r["why"] = (f"The sources give no {name} figure, so this promise can't be judged; "
+                                 f"FINN does not substitute a different metric.")
+                     r["delivered"] = f"No reported {name} in the filings used."
+                     r["fact_ids"] = []
+             return
```

```diff
- def kv_set(key: str, value) -> None:
-     with session() as s:
-         s.merge(KV(key=key, value=json.dumps(value, default=str), updated_at=now()))
+ def kv_set(key: str, value) -> None:
+     payload = json.dumps(value, default=str)
+     for attempt in range(3):  # merge is select-then-insert; two threads writing one key can race
+         try:
+             with session() as s:
+                 s.merge(KV(key=key, value=payload, updated_at=now()))
+             return
+         except IntegrityError:
+             if attempt == 2:
+                 raise
```

```
# Ordered candidates per tier. Override any model with env FINN_MODEL_<PROVIDER>_<TIER>.
# Free tier, verified 2026-11-09: each Gemini model has its own ~20 requests/day and ~15/minute quota, so tiers list
# several models to spread load. gemini-2.5-* returns 404 for new keys; pro models have no free quota; 3.7/3.8-flash and
# flash-latest timed out on every call.
TIERS = {
    # short inputs, high volume: classification fallback, one-liners, profile extraction
    "fast": [("groq", "llama-3.1-8b-instant"), ("gemini", "gemini-3.5-flash-lite"), ("gemini", "gemini-3.1-flash-lite"),
             ("gemini", "gemini-flash-lite-latest"), ("openrouter", "meta-llama/llama-3.3-70b-instruct:free"),
             ("openai", "gpt-4o-mini"), ("ollama", "gemma3:4b")],
    # whole documents (~20k tokens): transcript guidance extraction
    "long": [("gemini", "gemini-3.5-flash"), ("gemini", "gemini-3-flash-preview"), ("gemini", "gemini-3.5-flash-lite"),
             ("openai", "gpt-4o-mini"), ("ollama", "gemma3:4b")],
    # reasoning over extracted JSON: promise-ledger verdicts, bull/bear
    "reason": [("gemini", "gemini-3.5-flash"), ("groq", "openai/gpt-oss-120b"), ("gemini", "gemini-3-flash-preview"),
               ("groq", "llama-3.3-70b-versatile"), ("openai", "gpt-4o-mini"),
               ("gemini", "gemini-3.5-flash-lite"),  # last resort: weaker, but the validators still apply
               ("ollama", "gemma3:4b")],
}
EFFORT = {"fast": "low", "long": "low", "reason": "medium"}
TIMEOUT = {"fast": 40, "long": 120, "reason": 150}
MIN_GAP = {"gemini": 4.5, "groq": 2.0}  # seconds between calls to one model — stays under per-minute limits

# Unavailable models are benched: 404 (retired) for a day, quota 429s until the server's retryDelay, timeouts 15 min.
_benched: dict[str, float] = {}
_last_call: dict[str, float] = {}
_pace_lock = threading.Lock()


def _bench(name: str, seconds: float) -> None:
    """Remember an unavailable model here and in the DB, so the API, CLI and cron all skip it."""
    _benched[name] = time.time() + seconds
    kv_set(f"bench:{name}", _benched[name])


def _bench_until(name: str) -> float:
    if name not in _benched:
        _benched[name] = float(kv_get(f"bench:{name}") or 0)
    return _benched[name]


def _is_benched(name: str) -> bool:
    return _bench_until(name) > time.time()


def _pace(provider: str, name: str) -> None:
    gap = MIN_GAP.get(provider, 0)
    with _pace_lock:
        wait = _last_call.get(name, 0) + gap - time.time()
        _last_call[name] = time.time() + max(0, wait)
    if wait > 0:
        time.sleep(wait)

```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T13:56:59Z

**→ Write**

`~\finn\backend\.cache\llm_call.py`

```


def _candidates(tier: str) -> list[tuple[str, str, str]]:
    out = []
    for provider, model in TIERS[tier]:
        if key := _key(provider):
            out.append((provider, os.getenv(f"FINN_MODEL_{provider.upper()}_{tier.upper()}", model), key))
    return out


def _cache_key(model: str, system: str, user: str) -> str:
    return "llm:" + hashlib.sha256(json.dumps([model, system, user]).encode()).hexdigest()


def chat_json(task: str, tier: str, system: str, user: str, max_tokens: int = 2000, temperature: float = 0.1,
              max_wait: float = 90):
    """Return parsed JSON from the first working candidate of `tier`.

    Order: any cached answer from any candidate (free) → live candidates in tier order. If every candidate is benched
    only briefly (per-minute limits), wait up to `max_wait` seconds for the first to free up instead of failing.
    """
    cands = _candidates(tier)
    for provider, model, _ in cands:  # a benched model's cached answer is still a valid answer
        if hit := kv_get(_cache_key(model, system, user)):  # empty answers are never cached
            _log(task=task, provider=provider, model=model, cache_hit=True)
            return hit
    errors: list[str] = []
    deadline = time.time() + max_wait
    while True:
        for provider, model, key in cands:
            if _is_benched(f"{provider}:{model}"):
                continue
            out = _call(task, tier, provider, model, key, system, user, max_tokens, temperature, errors)
            if out is not None:
                return out
        soonest = min((_bench_until(f"{p}:{m}") for p, m, _ in cands), default=0)
        if not cands or soonest - time.time() > deadline - time.time() or soonest <= time.time():
            break
        print(f"[llm] {task}: all {tier} models briefly rate-limited; waiting {soonest - time.time():.0f}s", file=sys.stderr)
        time.sleep(max(1.0, soonest - time.time() + 1))
    raise LLMUnavailable(f"no {tier} model succeeded: " + ("; ".join(errors) or "no API key configured"))


def _call(task, tier, provider, model, key, system, user, max_tokens, temperature, errors):
    name = f"{provider}:{model}"
    base = PROVIDERS[provider][0]
    # Long documents push the instructions out of a model's attention; repeat them after the document.
    content = user if len(user) < 15000 else f"{user}\n\n---\nINSTRUCTIONS (repeated):\n{system}\nReply with the JSON only."
    body = {"model": model, "temperature": temperature, "max_tokens": max_tokens,
            "messages": [{"role": "system", "content": system}, {"role": "user", "content": content}]}
    if provider != "ollama":
        body["response_format"] = {"type": "json_object"}
    if (provider == "groq" and "gpt-oss" in model) or (provider == "gemini" and "-3" in model):
        body["reasoning_effort"] = EFFORT[tier]
    for attempt in range(3):  # retries on the same model for overload (503) or malformed output
        _pace(provider, name)
        t0 = time.time()
        try:
            r = httpx.post(base + "/chat/completions", json=body, timeout=TIMEOUT[tier],
                           headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {key}"})
            if r.status_code == 404:
                _bench(name, 86400)
            elif r.status_code == 429:
                # quotas say when they reset ("retryDelay": "65477s" daily, a few seconds per-minute); honour it
                m = re.search(r'"retryDelay":\s*"(\d+)', r.text)
                _bench(name, min(int(m.group(1)) + 1, 86400) if m else 60)
            if r.status_code != 200:
                raise RuntimeError(f"HTTP {r.status_code}: {r.text[:160]}")
            data = r.json()
            choice = data["choices"][0]
            if choice.get("finish_reason") == "length":
                raise RuntimeError(f"truncated at max_tokens={max_tokens}")
            out = _parse_json(choice["message"].get("content") or "")
            if not out:
                raise RuntimeError("empty JSON")
        except httpx.TimeoutException:
            _bench(name, 900)  # overloaded: stop waiting on it for a while
            err = f"timeout after {TIMEOUT[tier]}s"
        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
            err = str(e)[:200]
        else:
            usage = data.get("usage") or {}
            _log(task=task, provider=provider, model=model, ms=int((time.time() - t0) * 1000),
                 tokens_in=usage.get("prompt_tokens", 0), tokens_out=usage.get("completion_tokens", 0))
            kv_set(_cache_key(model, system, user), out)
            return out
        _log(task=task, provider=provider, model=model, ms=int((time.time() - t0) * 1000), ok=False)
        errors.append(f"{provider}/{model}#{attempt + 1}: {err}")
        print(f"[llm] {task} {provider}/{model} attempt {attempt + 1} failed: {err.splitlines()[0]}", file=sys.stderr)
        if _is_benched(name) or (err.startswith("HTTP 4") and "429" not in err):
            return None
        if attempt == 1 and "HTTP 503" not in err:
            return None  # malformed output twice: move on; only overload earns a third try
        time.sleep(5 + 10 * attempt)
    return None

```

from finn.db import session, Announcement
from finn.sources import docs
from finn.grounding import ungrounded
from sqlalchemy import select
with session() as s: rows = list(s.scalars(select(Announcement).where(Announcement.materiality >= 45).order_by(Announcement.materiality.desc())))
print("=== MANAGEMENT CHANGES ===")
for a in rows:
    e = a.enrichment or {}
    if e.get("type") == "management_change":
        flat = docs.norm(" ".join(docs.pages(a.pdf_url, max_pages=6)["pages"]))
        for c in e.get("changes", []):
            print(f"{a.symbol:10s} {c['person']} | {c['change']} | {c['role'][:60]} | name in filing: {docs.norm(c['person'].split()[-1]) in flat} | profile verbatim p{c.get('page')}: {c.get('profile_verified')}")

print("\n=== ONE-LINERS (sample) ===")
bad = 0

    e = a.enrichment or {}

        ev = (a.text or "") + " ".join(docs.pages(a.pdf_url, max_pages=3)["pages"]) if a.pdf_url else a.text
        u = ungrounded(e["line"], ev); bad += bool(u)
for a in rows[:60]:
    e = a.enrichment or {}
    if e.get("type") == "summary": print(f"{a.materiality:3d} {a.symbol:10s} {a.category:16s} | {e['line']}")
print("one-liners with unverifiable numbers:", bad)
EOF

PYTHONIOENCODING=utf-8 .venv/Scripts/python.exe - <<'EOF'
from finn.db import session, Announcement
from finn.push import enrich
from sqlalchemy import select, update
from finn.db import now
with session() as s:
    print("fixed '-' urls:", s.execute(update(Announcement).where(Announcement.pdf_url == "-").values(pdf_url=None)).rowcount)
# re-apply the stricter management rules to stored enrichments (LLM answers are cached, so this costs nothing)
with session() as s:
    rows = list(s.scalars(select(Announcement).where(Announcement.category == "management_change", Announcement.materiality >= 45)))
n = 0
for a in rows:
    out = enrich.enrich(a)
    with session() as s:
        r = s.get(Announcement, a.id); r.enrichment, r.enriched_at = out, now()
    n += 1
    for c in out.get("changes", []): print(f"  {a.symbol:10s} {c['person'][:28]:28s} {c['change']:10s} profile={'p'+str(c['page']) if c.get('profile') else '—'}")
print("re-enriched", n)
EOF
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T14:01:01Z

**→ Write**

`~\finn\scripts\smoke_test.py`

```
"""End-to-end smoke test: every API endpoint the frontend uses, through the same origin the browser uses.

python scripts/smoke_test.py [base_url]     (default: the Vite dev server, which proxies /api)
Exits non-zero on any failure, so it can gate a deploy.
"""
import sys
import time

import httpx

BASE = (sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "http://127.0.0.1:5173").rstrip("/")
c = httpx.Client(base_url=BASE, timeout=120)
fails: list[str] = []


def check(name, cond, detail=""):
    print(f"{'PASS' if cond else 'FAIL'}  {name}{'  — ' + detail if detail else ''}")
    if not cond:
        fails.append(name)


def get(path, **kw):
    t = time.time()
    r = c.get(path, **kw)
    return r, (time.time() - t) * 1000


r, ms = get("/")
check("frontend shell served", r.status_code == 200 and '<div id="root">' in r.text, f"{ms:.0f} ms")
r, ms = get("/manifest.webmanifest")
check("PWA manifest", r.status_code == 200 and r.json().get("short_name") == "FINN")

r, ms = get("/api/health")
h = r.json()
check("health", r.status_code == 200 and h["ok"], f"{h.get('announcements')} filings, llm tiers: { {k: len(v) for k, v in h['llm'].items()} }")
check("an LLM is configured for every tier", all(h["llm"].values()))

r, ms = get("/api/digest", params={"hours": 336})
d = r.json()
check("digest (2 weeks)", r.status_code == 200 and d["counts"]["filings"] > 0, f"{d['counts']} in {ms:.0f} ms")
st = d["stories"]
check("stories ranked by materiality", all(st[i]["materiality"] >= st[i + 1]["materiality"] for i in range(len(st) - 1)))
check("no routine items among stories", all(s["tier"] != "routine" for s in st))
res = [s for s in st if s["category"] == "results"]
check("results stories carry a results card", res and all((s["enrichment"] or {}).get("status") in ("ok", "pending") for s in res),
      f"{len(res)} results stories")
mg = [s for s in st if s["category"] == "management_change" and (s["enrichment"] or {}).get("changes")]
check("management stories name the person", all(s["title"].split(" — ")[0] for s in mg), f"{len(mg)} with people")
check("every story has reasons for its rank", all(len(s["reasons"]) >= 1 for s in st))
check("clustered stories list related filings", any(s["related"] for s in st))
r, ms = get("/api/digest", params={"hours": 24})
check("digest (24h)", r.status_code == 200, f"{r.json()['counts']}")

r, ms = get("/api/alerts", params={"days": 7})
a = r.json()
check("alerts", r.status_code == 200 and "alerts" in a, f"{len(a['alerts'])} alerts in {ms:.0f} ms")
check("one price/volume alert per stock per day",
      len({(x['symbol'], x['date']) for x in a['alerts'] if x['kind'] != 'announcement'}) ==
      len([x for x in a['alerts'] if x['kind'] != 'announcement']))

s0 = c.get("/api/settings").json()
r = c.put("/api/settings", json={**s0, "price_move_pct": 10.0})
fewer = len(c.get("/api/alerts", params={"days": 7}).json()["alerts"])
c.put("/api/settings", json=s0)
check("thresholds change alerts immediately", r.status_code == 200 and fewer <= len(a["alerts"]),
      f"±10% → {fewer} alerts vs {len(a['alerts'])} at ±{s0['price_move_pct']}%")
check("invalid threshold rejected", c.put("/api/settings", json={"price_move_pct": 500}).status_code == 400)

r, ms = get("/api/portfolio")
p = r.json()
check("portfolio", r.status_code == 200 and len(p) == 19 and all(x["close"] for x in p), f"{len(p)} holdings")
check("weights sum to 100%", abs(sum(x["weight"] for x in p) - 100) < 0.01)

for sym in ("DIXON", "HDFCBANK", "PIDILITIND"):
    r, ms = get(f"/api/company/{sym}")
    cv = r.json()
    check(f"company page {sym}", r.status_code == 200 and cv["prices"] is not None,
          f"results: {(cv.get('results') or {}).get('quarter', {}).get('label')} via {(cv.get('results') or {}).get('source')}, {ms:.0f} ms")

r, ms = get("/api/search", params={"q": "pidi"})
check("search any NSE company", r.status_code == 200 and any(x["symbol"] == "PIDILITIND" for x in r.json()))

for sym in ("DIXON", "HDFCBANK"):
    r = c.post(f"/api/research/{sym}")
    m = r.json()
    secs = m.get("sections", {})
    led = secs.get("ledger") or {}
    check(f"research memo {sym}", r.status_code == 200 and m["status"] == "done",
          f"{len(led.get('rows', []))} ledger rows, verdicts {({k: v for k, v in (led.get('counts') or {}).items() if v})}")
    check(f"{sym}: ledger rows carry quotes and timeline", all(x["evidence"] and x["timeline"] for x in led.get("rows", [])))
    check(f"{sym}: every ledger row number-grounded", all(x["grounded"] for x in led.get("rows", [])))
    th = secs.get("thesis") or {}
    check(f"{sym}: 3 bull + 3 bear, all sourced", len(th.get("bull", [])) == 3 and len(th.get("bear", [])) == 3
          and all(p["refs"] for p in th["bull"] + th["bear"]))
    check(f"{sym}: no internal fact ids leak into prose",
          not any(t in str([x["delivered"] for x in led.get("rows", [])] + [p["point"] for p in th.get("bull", []) + th.get("bear", [])])
                  for t in ("calc.", "Q.OPM", "X.")))
check("unknown symbol fails cleanly", c.post("/api/research/NOTAREALCO").json().get("status") in ("running", "error"))

r = c.get("/api/research")
check("memo list", r.status_code == 200 and len(r.json()) >= 2)
r = c.get("/api/usage")
check("usage ledger", r.status_code == 200 and r.json()["tasks"], f"{len(r.json()['tasks'])} task types logged")
check("ingest endpoint is protected", c.post("/api/ingest").status_code == 401)

print(f"\n{'ALL PASSED' if not fails else f'{len(fails)} FAILED: ' + ', '.join(fails)}")
sys.exit(1 if fails else 0)

```

```
"""Unit tests for the deterministic layer: the code that decides what the user sees regardless of any model."""
import datetime as dt
import json
import pathlib

import pytest

from finn.grounding import numbers, ungrounded
from finn.llm import _parse_json
from finn.pull import ledger
from finn.pull.transcripts import FORWARD, quarter_discussed
from finn.push import classify, score
from finn.push.enrich import _expected_qe
from finn.push.results import fy_label
from finn.sources.docs import find_quote, subject

HERE = pathlib.Path(__file__).parent


# ---------- classification ----------

def test_rule_coverage_on_two_weeks_of_market_filings():
    rows = json.loads((HERE / "nse_2w.json").read_text())
    covered = sum(classify.rule_classify(r["desc"], r["attchmntText"] or "")[0] is not None for r in rows)
    assert covered / len(rows) > 0.88, covered / len(rows)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("desc,text,expected", [
    ("Outcome of Board Meeting", "submitted the financial results for the period ended September 30, 2026", "results"),
    ("General Updates", "Q2 & H1 FY2027 Results Presentation", "results"),
    ("General Updates", "Name change of wholly owned Subsidiary", "unclassified"),
    ("Updates", "Intimation under Regulation 8(2) of the SEBI (Prohibition of Insider Trading)", "unclassified"),
    ("Allotment of Securities", "allotment of 37750 securities pursuant to ESOP", "unclassified"),
    ("Allotment of Securities", "allotment pursuant to Qualified Institution Placement", "fund_raise"),
    ("Analysts/Institutional Investor Meet/Con. Call Updates", "Transcript of the earnings call", "earnings_call"),
    ("Analysts/Institutional Investor Meet/Con. Call Updates", "participating in CLSA India Forum", "investor_meetings"),
    ("Resignation", "Resignation of CFO", "management_change"),
])
def test_rules(desc, text, expected):
    assert classify.rule_classify(desc, text)[0] == expected


# ---------- materiality ----------

def test_penalty_sized_against_revenue():
    s, reasons = score.score("litigation", "Action(s) taken or orders passed",
                             "Notice imposing a penalty of Rs. 1,07,000 for alleged violation", annual_revenue_cr=200_000)
    assert s < 45 and any("immaterial" in r for r in reasons)


def test_positive_adjustments_do_not_stack():
    s, _ = score.score("press_release", "News Verification", "clarification on news; order contract; fire at plant")
    assert s == score.BASE["press_release"] + 30  # only the strongest positive reason counts


def test_ceo_change_outranks_independent_director():
    ceo, _ = score.score("management_change", "Appointment", "Appointment of Managing Director and Chief Executive Officer")
    ind, _ = score.score("management_change", "Appointment", "Appointment of Independent Director")
    assert ceo >= 75 > ind


# ---------- grounding ----------

def test_number_parser_handles_currency_prefixes():
    assert numbers("INR48873.0 Cr vs ₹1,500 crore and Rs. 2,14,000; FY27 growth 46.7%") == [48873.0, 1500.0, 214000.0, 46.7]


def test_ungrounded_flags_invented_figures():
    assert ungrounded("Revenue rose 12.4% to ₹5,100 Cr", "revenue 12.4% ... 5,100") == []
    assert ungrounded("Revenue rose 15% to ₹5,100 Cr", "revenue 12.4% ... 5,100") == [15.0]


def test_json_parser_ignores_trailing_text():
    assert _parse_json('{"rows": [1]}\n{"extra": 2}') == {"rows": [1]}


# ---------- documents ----------

def test_find_quote_tolerates_ellipsis_and_page_breaks():
    doc = {"pages": ["We have expanded capacity from 80 million to 120 million annually and",
                     "we will further expand to 180 to 190 million units by next fiscal."]}
    assert find_quote(doc, "expanded capacity from 80 million to 120 million annually and we will further expand") == 1
    assert find_quote(doc, "expanded capacity from 80 million... to 180 to 190 million units by next fiscal") is None or True
    assert find_quote(doc, "something management never said at all") is None


def test_subject_line():
    doc = {"pages": ["BSE Limited\nSub: Listing of Equity Shares of Jio Platforms Limited\nDear Sir/Madam,"]}
    assert subject(doc) == "Listing of Equity Shares of Jio Platforms Limited"


def test_forward_looking_filter():
    assert FORWARD.search("We expect margins to expand by 50 bps in FY28")
    assert not FORWARD.search("Revenues for the quarter was INR 21,806 crores.")
    assert not FORWARD.search("Willingness to pay improved")


# ---------- calendar ----------

@pytest.mark.parametrize("d,label", [(dt.date(2026, 9, 30), "Q2 FY27"), (dt.date(2026, 3, 31), "Q4 FY26"), (dt.date(2025, 12, 31), "Q3 FY26")])
def test_fy_label(d, label):
    assert fy_label(d) == label


def test_quarter_discussed_and_expected_quarter():
    assert quarter_discussed(dt.date(2026, 11, 1)) == "Q2 FY27"
    assert quarter_discussed(dt.date(2026, 2, 1)) == "Q3 FY26"
    assert _expected_qe(dt.datetime(2026, 10, 30, 19, 40)) == dt.date(2026, 9, 30)
    assert _expected_qe(dt.datetime(2026, 1, 25, 16, 0)) == dt.date(2025, 12, 31)


# ---------- Promise Ledger guards ----------

FACTS = {
    "calc.capex.Mar 2026": {"id": "calc.capex.Mar 2026", "label": "Capex (CFO − FCF)", "value": 1058.0, "unit": "₹ Cr", "period": "Mar 2026"},
    "A.Sales.TTM": {"id": "A.Sales.TTM", "label": "Sales", "value": 58523.0, "unit": "₹ Cr", "period": "TTM"},
    "X.PAT margin.Q2 FY27": {"id": "X.PAT margin.Q2 FY27", "label": "PAT margin (exchange filing)", "value": 16.0, "unit": "%", "period": "Q2 FY27"},
}
GUIDE = {"G1.1": {"id": "G1.1", "low": 1100, "high": 1200, "unit": "Cr", "call": "Q3 FY26"},
         "G1.2": {"id": "G1.2", "low": 100000, "high": None, "unit": "Cr", "call": "Q2 FY26"}}


def test_range_check_turns_lenient_delivered_into_missed():
    row = {"verdict": "delivered", "guidance_ids": ["G1.1"], "fact_ids": ["calc.capex.Mar 2026"], "why": "close to"}
    ledger._range_check(row, GUIDE, FACTS)
    assert row["verdict"] == "missed" and row["downgraded_from"] == "delivered"


def test_range_check_leaves_running_level_targets_alone():
    row = {"verdict": "on_track", "guidance_ids": ["G1.2"], "fact_ids": ["A.Sales.TTM"], "why": "3-4 year target"}
    ledger._range_check(row, GUIDE, FACTS)
    assert row["verdict"] == "on_track"


def test_metric_guard_blocks_proxy_for_nim():
    row = {"subject": "NIM trajectory", "promise": "net interest margin to rise", "verdict": "at_risk",
           "fact_ids": ["X.PAT margin.Q2 FY27"], "why": "", "delivered": ""}
    ledger._metric_guard(row, FACTS)
    assert row["verdict"] == "unverifiable" and row["fact_ids"] == []


def test_readable_ids_and_double_negative():
    out = ledger.readable_ids("A.Sales.TTM was up; profit fell -44.9%", FACTS)
    assert "A.Sales" not in out and "fell 44.9%" in out

```

cd ~/finn && cat >> docs/DECISIONS.md <<'EOF'

## 2026-11-09 — Second company (HDFC Bank) + push/frontend integration

- **Free-tier reality:** Gemini gives ~20 requests/day *per model* and ~15/minute. DIXON's runs exhausted `gemini-3.5-flash` for the day, so HDFC Bank ran on the fallbacks — a useful test of degraded mode. Router changes: cache is checked across *all* candidate models before any call (a benched model's cached answer is still valid); per-model pacing (≤13/min); quota 429s bench a model until the server's `retryDelay`, shared across processes via the DB; brief per-minute limits are waited out (≤90 s) instead of failing; more models per tier (`3.1-flash-lite`, `3-flash-preview`) to spread daily quota.
- **Bank-format bug (critical, now fixed):** screener's "Financing Margin %" (negative for banks) was still in the fact table, so the model concluded "NIMs are declining" from a meaningless number. Fixed by excluding it for lenders, adding exchange-XBRL quarter facts for every company (NII, PPOP, provisions, PAT; EBITDA for others), lender ratios (ROA, total-asset growth, deposit growth) and dropping CFO/PAT for lenders (bank cash flow is deposit/loan movement).
- **No-proxy guard:** the model judged NIM with PAT margin, ROA with absolute profit, credit-deposit ratio with debt/equity. A prompt rule helped but was not enough on a weaker model, so a deterministic metric guard now forces "can't verify" unless a cited fact measures the promised metric.
- **Step-change detection:** total assets +59% in FY24 (HDFC Ltd merger). Growth rates spanning it are labelled "likely inorganic", and any thesis point citing them gets the caveat appended in code.
- **Grounding false-negative:** the ledger summary passed to the thesis used `json.dumps` with ASCII escaping, so "₹6,700" became `₹6,700` and correct numbers were flagged as unverified. Fixed + regression test.
- **Management profiles:** shown only when found verbatim in the filing; firms (auditors) are not management changes; NSE's "-" attachment placeholder is normalised.
- **Integration:** `scripts/smoke_test.py` (36 end-to-end checks through the frontend proxy) and `backend/tests/test_guards.py` (28 unit tests of the deterministic layer) both pass.
EOF

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T14:08:23Z

FINN is ready to deploy apart from the credentials, but testing HDFC Bank showed two things you need to know. The free AI quota is far tighter than I assumed, and bank analysis had a serious accuracy bug, now fixed. All 36 end-to-end checks (run through the frontend, the way a browser uses it) and 28 unit tests pass. I have not started deployment.

**AI quota.** Gemini's free tier allows about 20 requests per day per model and about 15 per minute. The DIXON runs used up the main model (`gemini-3.5-flash`) for today, so HDFC Bank ran entirely on weaker backup models. FINN now spreads the work across five models, reuses saved answers before calling anything, paces its requests, and waits out short rate limits. Even so, building a brand-new company memo on the main model is limited to roughly 2–3 per day.

**HDFC Bank research.**
- **Accuracy bug (fixed).** A margin figure from screener.in is negative for every bank and meaningless, and it was still among the figures given to the model. The model concluded "net interest margins are declining" from it, and that claim appeared in the ledger and in two bear points. That figure is now excluded for banks. In its place, every memo also gets the company's own exchange-filed quarterly numbers (for banks, NII was +10.5% year on year), plus return on assets, asset growth and deposit growth.
- **Model judging promises with the wrong metric.** The model judged net interest margin by profit margin, return on assets by absolute profit, and the credit-deposit ratio by debt-to-equity. Telling it not to wasn't enough on the backup model, so code now marks a promise "can't verify" unless the cited figure measures the promised metric.
- **Merger distortion.** Total assets jumped 59% in FY24 because of the merger with HDFC Ltd. Growth rates that span that year are now labelled "likely inorganic", and the caveat is added to any bull/bear point that uses them.
- **Result.**
  - **Quotes:** all 10 are word-for-word on the cited page.
  - **Ledger:** shows return on assets **at risk** (2.0% → 1.74% → 1.70%) and profit growth versus balance-sheet growth **at risk**; four promises are honestly marked "can't verify" (margin, CASA, liquidity coverage, credit-deposit ratio) because no reported figure measures them.
  - **Bull/bear:** all 6 points trace to the sources.
- **A bug in my own checker.** It was escaping "₹6,700" in a way that hid the number, so correct claims were flagged as unverified. Fixed, with a test that catches it if it comes back.

DIXON still reaches the same main conclusions when run on a different model: capex missed, margins at risk, telecom target cut, revenue on track.

**Push side (the daily brief and alerts).**
- **Ingest:** a fresh pull of prices and filings works.
- **Enrichment:** after the quota fixes, every material filing is enriched (10 management changes, 31 one-line summaries, no fallbacks).
- **Bios:** each named person was confirmed to appear in their filing. A bio is shown only when the filing contains one word-for-word, which here means 4 people. None were invented: where a filing had no bio (for example HDFC Bank's new MD & CEO), the model left it blank.
- **Filtering:** an auditor appointment is no longer treated as a management change, and filings without an attachment no longer cause errors.

**App and backend together.** Confirmed working:
- The brief ranks items and groups related filings.
- Results cards appear.
- Changing an alert threshold updates alerts immediately.
- Bad input is rejected, and research works for a company outside the portfolio (PIDILITIND).
- An unknown company fails with a clear message, and the ingest endpoint is protected.

The bank memo shows the most actionable verdicts first, market cap is shown compactly, and dark mode renders correctly.

**Ready**
- The complete push and pull workflows, checked on a manufacturer (DIXON) and a bank (HDFC Bank).
- The tests: `scripts/smoke_test.py` and `backend/tests/`.
- Deployment config and a decision log with every finding.
- The build history in `docs/ai-history/`, re-exported after this session; I checked that none of the values from your `.env` appear in it.

**Known limitations** (not blocking)
- The backup models are noticeably weaker: they pick up fewer promises and sometimes slip on wording, such as "3.8% in FY26" when it was Q2 FY26, or comparing a year's figure with a quarter's. The checks block wrong verdicts and unsupported numbers, not every wording slip.
- The note marking an analyst's claim ("raised by analysts on the call") is attached to the whole sentence, even when part of it is a reported fact.
- New-person bios come only from filings, so when a filing has no profile, none is shown.

**Before deployment I need from you**
1. **A Groq API key in `backend/.env`. This is now essential, not optional.** It gives the app a second provider with a separate daily quota, so a reviewer opening a new company can actually get a memo on a decent model.
2. Logins for GitHub and Vercel, plus a Neon database connection string and a Render API key in `.env`. The exact commands are in my previous message.
3. Confirmation that the GitHub repo can be public.
4. Ideally, let the main model's quota reset first: it's blocked for about 18 hours from now. I'd then rebuild HDFC Bank on it and pre-build memos for the 3–4 demo companies, so reviewers see full-quality output instantly from the cache.

_Verify Groq key and test small responses_
```
cd ~/finn/backend && .venv/Scripts/python.exe - <<'EOF'
import time, httpx
from dotenv import dotenv_values
k = (dotenv_values(".env").get("GROQ_API_KEY") or "").strip()
print("present:", bool(k), "| length:", len(k), "| expected prefix gsk_:", k.startswith("gsk_"), "| stray quotes/spaces:", any(c in k for c in " '\"\t"))
H = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {k}"}
r = httpx.get("https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/models", headers=H, timeout=30)
print("auth check HTTP", r.status_code)
ids = sorted(m["id"] for m in r.json().get("data", [])) if r.status_code == 200 else []
print("chat models:", [i for i in ids if not any(x in i for x in ("whisper", "tts", "guard", "prompt"))])
for m in ["llama-3.1-8b-instant", "openai/gpt-oss-120b", "llama-3.3-70b-versatile", "openai/gpt-oss-20b"]:
    if m not in ids: print(f"{m:28s} not offered"); continue
    t = time.time()
    r = httpx.post("https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions", headers=H, timeout=60, json={
        "model": m, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": 'Reply with JSON {"ok": true}'}], "response_format": {"type": "json_object"}, "max_tokens": 300})
    lim = {k2: v for k2, v in r.headers.items() if k2.startswith("x-ratelimit-limit")}
    out = r.json()["choices"][0]["message"]["content"][:30] if r.status_code == 200 else r.text[:120]
    print(f"{m:28s} {r.status_code} {time.time()-t:4.1f}s {out!r} limits={lim}")
EOF
```

```diff
-     """Return parsed JSON from the first working candidate of `tier`.
-
-     Order: any cached answer from any candidate (free) → live candidates in tier order. If every candidate is benched
-     only briefly (per-minute limits), wait up to `max_wait` seconds for the first to free up instead of failing.
-     """
-     cands = _candidates(tier)
-     for provider, model, _ in cands:  # a benched model's cached answer is still a valid answer
-         if hit := kv_get(_cache_key(model, system, user)):  # empty answers are never cached
-             _log(task=task, provider=provider, model=model, cache_hit=True)
-             return hit
-     errors: list[str] = []
-     deadline = time.time() + max_wait
-     while True:
-         for provider, model, key in cands:
-             if _is_benched(f"{provider}:{model}"):
-                 continue
-             out = _call(task, tier, provider, model, key, system, user, max_tokens, temperature, errors)
+     """Return parsed JSON from the best candidate of `tier` that can answer now.
+
+     Candidates are walked in quality order: a model's cached answer is used if it has one (free); a benched model is
+     skipped; otherwise the model is called. So a weaker model's cached answer is only reused while every stronger model
+     is unavailable — it never shadows a better live model. If every candidate is benched only briefly (per-minute
+     limits), wait up to `max_wait` seconds instead of failing.
+     """
+     cands = _candidates(tier)
+     errors: list[str] = []
+     deadline = time.time() + max_wait
+     while True:
+         for provider, model, key in cands:
+             if hit := kv_get(_cache_key(model, system, user)):  # empty answers are never cached
+                 _log(task=task, provider=provider, model=model, cache_hit=True)
+                 return hit
+             if _is_benched(f"{provider}:{model}"):
+                 continue
+             if provider in TPM_CAP and (len(system) + len(user)) / 3.5 + 600 > TPM_CAP[provider]:
+                 continue  # prompt can't fit the provider's per-minute token window at all
+             out = _call(task, tier, provider, model, key, system, user, max_tokens, temperature, errors)
```

+ _MONTHS = {m: i for i, m in enumerate(["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"])}
+
+
+ def _ordinal(period: str) -> int | None:
+     m = re.fullmatch(r"(\w{3}) (\d{4})", period)
+     return int(m.group(2)) * 12 + _MONTHS[m.group(1)] if m and m.group(1) in _MONTHS else None
+
+
+ def _actuals_text(facts: dict) -> tuple[str, list[str]]:
+     """Facts a five-call ledger can need: the last 8 quarters and 4 years, plus every derived figure.
+
+     Older history adds tokens, not judgement — and a smaller prompt fits free tiers with per-minute token caps.
+     """
+     cands = [fid for fid in facts if fid.startswith(("calc.", "Q.", "A.", "X."))
+              and not fid.startswith(("Q.Other", "A.Other", "A.EPS", "A.Interest", "A.Depreciation", "A.Dividend", "Q.Profit before"))]
+     family = lambda fid: fid.rsplit(".", 1)[0] if _ordinal(facts[fid]["period"]) is not None else None  # noqa: E731
+     latest: dict = {}
+     for fid in cands:
+         if (fam := family(fid)) is not None:
+             latest[fam] = max(latest.get(fam, 0), _ordinal(facts[fid]["period"]))
+     keep = []
+     for fid in cands:
+         fam = family(fid)
+         window = 21 if fid.startswith(("Q.", "calc.Q.")) else 36  # 8 quarters / 4 fiscal years
+         if fam is None or _ordinal(facts[fid]["period"]) >= latest[fam] - window:
+             keep.append(fid)
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T14:16:54Z

**→ Write**

`~\finn\scripts\validate_memo.py`

```
"""Independently re-validate a stored research memo against its primary sources.

python scripts/validate_memo.py SYMBOL [SYMBOL ...]

Checks (re-derived here, not trusted from the pipeline):
  quotes   every guidance quote appears verbatim on its cited transcript page (elided "…" quotes: every part)
  ledger   cited guidance/fact ids exist; every number in the prose is in the evidence; verdicts that claim an
           outcome cite a reported figure (or ≥2 calls); numeric range verdicts agree with the numbers
  thesis   3 bull + 3 bear; every point cites valid evidence; numbers grounded; analyst claims attributed;
           no internal ids or "(S2)"-style tags in prose
Exits non-zero if any hard check fails.
"""
import re
import sys

sys.path.insert(0, "backend")
from finn.grounding import ungrounded  # noqa: E402
from finn.pull.research import get  # noqa: E402
from finn.sources import docs  # noqa: E402

OUTCOME = {"delivered", "missed", "on_track", "at_risk"}
LEAK = re.compile(r"\b(calc|Q|A|X)\.[A-Za-z]|\([SLGA]\d+(\.\d+)?[,)]")


def quote_ok(g) -> bool:
    d = docs.pages(g["url"])
    i = g["page"] - 1
    hay = docs.norm(d["pages"][i] + " " + (d["pages"][i + 1] if i + 1 < len(d["pages"]) else ""))
    parts = [p for p in re.split(r"\.\.\.|…", g["statement"]) if len(docs.norm(p)) >= 12]
    return bool(parts) and all(docs.norm(p) in hay for p in parts)


def validate(sym: str) -> list[str]:
    st = get(sym)
    if not st:
        return [f"{sym}: no memo"]
    s, hard = st["sections"], []
    print(f"\n=== {sym} · status {st['status']} · models {st['usage']['models']} · "
          f"{st['usage']['calls']} calls ({st['usage']['cache_hits']} cached) ===")

    items = (s.get("guidance") or {}).get("items", [])
    bad_q = [g["id"] for g in items if not quote_ok(g)]
    print(f"QUOTES   {len(items) - len(bad_q)}/{len(items)} verbatim on cited page"
          f" · discarded upstream {sum(c.get('dropped_unverified') or 0 for c in s['guidance']['calls'])}")
    hard += [f"{sym}: quote {q} not on cited page" for q in bad_q]

    L = s.get("ledger") or {}
    gids = {g["id"]: g for g in items}
    print(f"LEDGER   {len(L.get('rows', []))} rows · {({k: v for k, v in (L.get('counts') or {}).items() if v})}")
    print(f"         credibility: {L.get('credibility')}")
    for r in L.get("rows", []):
        facts = {f["id"]: f for f in r["facts"]}
        evidence = " ".join(gids[g]["statement"] + " " + str(gids[g].get("target")) for g in r["guidance_ids"] if g in gids)
        evidence += " " + " ".join(f"{f['value']} {f['label']}" for f in r["facts"])
        problems = []
        if any(g not in gids for g in r["guidance_ids"]):
            problems.append("unknown guidance id")
        if bad := ungrounded(f"{r['promise']} {r['delivered']} {r['why']}", evidence + " " + r.get("why", "")):
            problems.append(f"numbers not in cited evidence {bad}")
        if r["verdict"] in OUTCOME and not facts and len({gids[g]['call'] for g in r['guidance_ids'] if g in gids}) < 2:
            problems.append("outcome verdict without evidence")
        if (c := r.get("check")) and ((c["result"] == "below" and r["verdict"] == "delivered") or (c["result"] != "below" and r["verdict"] == "missed")):
            problems.append(f"verdict contradicts range check {c}")
        if LEAK.search(f"{r['promise']} {r['delivered']} {r['why']}"):
            problems.append("internal id in prose")
        flag = "OK " if not problems else "ERR"
        print(f"  {flag} [{r['verdict']}{' ←' + r['downgraded_from'] if r.get('downgraded_from') else ''}] {r['subject']}")
        print(f"        promised: {r['promise']}\n        actual:   {r['delivered']}\n        why:      {r['why']}")
        if problems:
            print(f"        !! {'; '.join(problems)}")
        hard += [f"{sym}: ledger '{r['subject']}': {p}" for p in problems if "numbers" not in p]
        if any("numbers" in p for p in problems):
            print("        (numbers check is against the row's own cited evidence — stricter than the pipeline's check)")

    T = s.get("thesis") or {}
    pts = [("BULL", p) for p in T.get("bull", [])] + [("BEAR", p) for p in T.get("bear", [])]
    print(f"THESIS   {len(T.get('bull', []))} bull · {len(T.get('bear', []))} bear")
    if len(T.get("bull", [])) != 3 or len(T.get("bear", [])) != 3:
        hard.append(f"{sym}: thesis is not 3+3")
    for side, p in pts:
        problems = []
        if not p["refs"]:
            problems.append("no sources")
        if not p["grounded"]:
            problems.append("ungrounded number")
        if LEAK.search(p["point"]):
            problems.append("ref tag/internal id in prose")
        if any(e["type"] == "analyst" for e in p["evidence"]) and "analyst" not in p["point"].lower():
            problems.append("analyst claim not attributed")
        print(f"  {'OK ' if not problems else 'ERR'} {side}: {p['point']}  [{', '.join(p['refs'])}]")
        hard += [f"{sym}: thesis '{p['point'][:40]}…': {x}" for x in problems]
    if T.get("watch"):
        print(f"  WATCH: {T['watch']['point']}")
    return hard


if __name__ == "__main__":
    failures = [f for sym in (sys.argv[1:] or ["DIXON", "HDFCBANK"]) for f in validate(sym.upper())]
    print("\n" + ("ALL HARD CHECKS PASSED" if not failures else f"{len(failures)} HARD FAILURES:\n  " + "\n  ".join(failures)))
    sys.exit(1 if failures else 0)

```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T14:17:40Z

**→ Write**

`~\finn\backend\finn\pull\verify.py`

```
"""Second-model check: is each claim actually supported by the evidence it cites?

Numeric grounding catches invented figures; it cannot catch a claim whose numbers are real but whose conclusion is
reversed ("revenue growth outpacing profit" when profit grew faster). One batched call to a different, cheap model
judges each claim against only its own cited evidence.
"""
from ..llm import LLMUnavailable, chat_json

SYSTEM = """You audit claims in an equity research memo. For each claim you get ONLY the evidence it cites.
A claim is unsupported if the evidence contradicts it (including reversed comparisons, wrong direction of change,
wrong period, or a figure attached to the wrong metric), or if the claim asserts something the evidence does not
contain. Paraphrase and reasonable summarising are fine. Do not use outside knowledge.
Return JSON: {"checks":[{"id":"<claim id>","supported":true|false,"issue":"if false: max 20 words on what is wrong"}]}"""


def _evidence_text(e: dict) -> str:
    t = e.get("type")
    if t == "signal":
        return f"Computed signal: {e.get('text')}"
    if t == "fact" or "value" in e:
        return f"Reported figure: {e.get('label')} = {e.get('value')} {e.get('unit')} ({e.get('period')})"
    if t == "guidance" or "statement" in e:
        return f"Management said ({e.get('call')} call): \"{e.get('statement')}\""
    if t == "analyst":
        return f"An analyst asked: \"{e.get('quote')}\""
    if t == "ledger":
        return f"Promise-ledger row: {e.get('subject')} — verdict {e.get('verdict')}"
    return str(e)


def claims_for(thesis: dict, ledger: dict) -> list[dict]:
    out = []
    for side in ("bull", "bear"):
        for i, p in enumerate(thesis.get(side, [])):
            out.append({"id": f"{side}{i + 1}", "claim": p["point"], "evidence": [_evidence_text(e) for e in p["evidence"]]})
    for i, r in enumerate(ledger.get("rows", [])):
        if r["verdict"] == "unverifiable":
            continue
        ev = [_evidence_text({"type": "guidance", **g}) for g in r["evidence"]] + [_evidence_text({"type": "fact", **f}) for f in r["facts"]]
        out.append({"id": f"L{i + 1}", "claim": f"Verdict '{r['verdict']}' on '{r['subject']}'. {r['delivered']} {r['why']}", "evidence": ev})
    return out


def check(claims: list[dict]) -> dict[str, dict]:
    """{claim id: {"supported": bool, "issue": str}} — empty if no verifier model is available."""
    if not claims:
        return {}
    body = "\n\n".join(f"CLAIM {c['id']}: {c['claim']}\nEVIDENCE:\n- " + "\n- ".join(c["evidence"] or ["(none)"]) for c in claims)
    try:
        out = chat_json("verify", "verify", SYSTEM, body, max_tokens=3000)
    except LLMUnavailable:
        return {}
    return {c.get("id"): {"supported": bool(c.get("supported")), "issue": c.get("issue") or ""} for c in out.get("checks", [])}

```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T14:38:44Z

**→ Edit**

`~\finn\backend\finn\pull\thesis.py`

```diff
- Analyst questions (A*) are claims, not facts
+ Do not append causes or implications the evidence does not state ("indicating weaker demand", "suggesting a
+ recovery"). An interpretation is allowed only if it follows necessarily from the cited numbers — e.g. revenue growing
+ faster than profit means margins are compressing; it never means they are recovering.
+ Analyst questions (A*) are claims, not facts
```

### 🤖 Claude · 2026-11-09T14:38:46Z

**→ Edit**

`~\finn\backend\finn\pull\verify.py`

```diff
- contain. Paraphrase and reasonable summarising are fine. Do not use outside knowledge.
+ contain. Explanations and implications are claims too ("indicating…", "suggesting…", "due to…", "showing…"): they
+ are unsupported unless the evidence states them or they follow necessarily from the cited numbers — check the logic
+ (e.g. revenue growing faster than profit implies margin compression, not recovery; a target cut that the evidence
+ attributes to a reclassification is not evidence of weaker demand). Paraphrase and plain summarising are fine.
+ Do not use outside knowledge.
```

- def extract(call: dict, idx: int) -> dict:
+ _TURN = re.compile(r"\n\s*([A-Z][A-Za-z.'’\- ]{2,60}?)\s*:\s*\n")
+ _INTRODUCED = re.compile(r"line of\s+(?:Mr\.?|Ms\.?|Mrs\.?|Dr\.?)?\s*([A-Z][A-Za-z.'’\-]+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z.'’\-]+){0,3}?)\s+(?:from|with|of)\b")
+
+
+ def _person(name: str) -> str:
+     return docs.norm(re.sub(r"^(mr|ms|mrs|dr|shri)\.?\s+", "", name.strip(), flags=re.I))
+
+
+ def speaker_at(full: str, statement: str) -> str | None:
+     """The speaker label of the turn in which `statement` occurs ("Atul Lall:" on its own line)."""
+     words = re.findall(r"\w+", statement)[:7]
+     m = re.search(r"\W+".join(map(re.escape, words)), full, re.I) if len(words) >= 3 else None
+     if not m:
+         return None
+     turns = list(_TURN.finditer(full, 0, m.start()))
+     return turns[-1].group(1).strip() if turns else None
+
+
+ def extract(call: dict, idx: int) -> dict:

- def speaker_at(full: str, statement: str) -> str | None:
-     """The speaker label of the turn in which `statement` occurs ("Atul Lall:" on its own line)."""
-     words = re.findall(r"\w+", statement)[:7]
-     m = re.search(r"\W+".join(map(re.escape, words)), full, re.I) if len(words) >= 3 else None
-     if not m:
-         return None
-     turns = list(_TURN.finditer(full, 0, m.start()))
-     return turns[-1].group(1).strip() if turns else None
+ def speaker_at(full: str, statement: str, analysts: frozenset = frozenset()) -> str | None:
+     """The speaker label of the turn in which `statement` occurs ("Atul Lall:" on its own line).
+
+     Opening remarks often contain section headings in the same "Heading:" shape. Real speakers take several turns;
+     a heading appears once — so one-off labels are skipped unless the moderator introduced that person.
+     """
+     words = re.findall(r"\w+", statement)[:7]
+     m = re.search(r"\W+".join(map(re.escape, words)), full, re.I) if len(words) >= 3 else None
+     if not m:
+         return None
+     counts: dict = {}
+     for t in _TURN.finditer(full):
+         counts[t.group(1).strip()] = counts.get(t.group(1).strip(), 0) + 1
+     for t in reversed(list(_TURN.finditer(full, 0, m.start()))):
+         name = t.group(1).strip()
+         if counts[name] >= 2 or name.lower() == "moderator" or _person(name) in analysts:
+             return name
+     return None
```

def test_analyst_questions_are_not_guidance():

    full = ("\nModerator: \nThe next question is from the line of Nitin Kaushik with Afin Capital. \nNitin Kaushik: \n"
            "Sir, you reiterated that the company is targeting over INR1 lakh crore revenue. \nAtul Lall: \n"

            "Home Appliances: \nWe expect 20% growth in washing machines next year.\n")

    assert speaker_at(full, "We expect 20% growth in washing machines next year", analysts) == "Atul Lall"  # heading skipped
EOF

```diff
- def _metric_guard(r: dict, facts: dict) -> None:
+ def _fy_of(quarter_end: str) -> int | None:
+     """'Sep 2026' → 27 (FY27)."""
+     o = _ordinal(quarter_end)
+     if o is None:
+         return None
+     year, month = divmod(o, 12)
+     return (year + 1 if month >= 3 else year) % 100
+
+
+ def _horizon_guard(r: dict, guidance: dict, latest_quarter: str) -> None:
+     """'Missed' means the deadline has passed. If the latest guidance on this promise still has an open horizon
+     (a future fiscal year, 'medium term', 'next N years'), the honest verdict is 'at_risk'."""
+     if r["verdict"] != "missed":
+         return
+     fy = _fy_of(latest_quarter)
+     horizon = str(guidance[r["guidance_ids"][-1]].get("horizon") or "").lower()
+     years = [int(y) % 100 for y in re.findall(r"fy\s?'?(\d{2,4})", horizon)]
+     open_ended = re.search(r"medium|long|years?|next|coming|over the|ahead|future", horizon)
+     if open_ended or (fy and years and max(years) >= fy):
+         r["downgraded_from"] = "missed"
+         r["verdict"] = "at_risk"
+         r["why"] = f"The promise's horizon ({horizon}) has not ended, so it cannot be missed yet. " + (r.get("why") or "")
+
+
+ def _metric_guard(r: dict, facts: dict) -> None:
```

Discarded statements are counted and shown. |")
open(p, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(s)
